# Fork and distribution polish — file 2 of 4: the three routes

This file is self-contained. It is the third of four `/st-plan` artifacts for **Package 20** (stamity's fork support
and its three distribution routes, end to end). It follows file 1 (`docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md`, fork
correctness and the round trip), whose units all land first (after file 0, `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-00.md`, which runs before Package 19), and ships as two pull requests, one per session, without a release; file 3
(`docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-03.md`) writes the docs, proves every route and cuts 1.12.0.

1.12.0 is taken by file 0's release (2026-10-07); a release this file names as 1.12.0 takes the next minor number when it runs.

intent chosen: feature because the request names net-new capability on each route — `stamity upgrade`, a slim APM
package with an `apm-backed` install mode, release channels with a verified promotion, `plugin setup --declare`,
overrides in plugin mode and the review gate on every client — and no dependency moves, so the migration intent does
not fire.

## Context

The three routes as they stand at 1.11.0, measured on 2026-10-01 with the released CLI and the distribution at
`plugins/v1.11.0`:

- **CLI route.** Three clients in generated mode write 181 files (172 at 1.10.0), against 29 in plugin-backed mode; 25
  of them are Codex skill metadata (`agents/openai.yaml`) written with no Codex selected, and 10 skill folders sit
  byte-identical in `.claude/skills/` and `.agents/skills/`. There is no `upgrade` verb, so a fork runs "unpin, sync,
  repin" by hand.
- **APM route.** The package carries agents, commands, rules and skills only, with 36 `${STAMITY:*}` placeholders only
  the CLI fills; an install vendors the whole repository; there is no Renovate preset for APM refs; CI and the release
  test apm-cli 0.30.0 while 0.33.0 (2026-10-02) is current; and `check` looks for APM duplicates only in plugin mode. The retired
  `stamity-apm` mirror (1.4.0) was the last slim, APM-only source.
- **Plugin route.** No channel exists: `plugin-dist` moves to every release at once. The Claude managed-settings
  template pins a tag, never sets auto-update and always writes the strict allowlist; Copilot's enterprise managed
  settings are generally available (2026-07-01) and stamity ships no template for them. The default branch carries only
  an npm-sourced Claude catalog, so Codex, the Copilot CLI or VS Code adding the repository without a ref get a root with
  no hooks and no runtime. Overrides, packs and model pins never reach a plugin-carried artifact; the review gate blocks
  only on Claude Code; VS Code ignores hook matchers; Cursor's rules live only inside the plugin; and plugin skills never
  call the runtime they bundle.

This file builds each route's missing half. **Out of scope:** file 1's fixes, the docs pages, the route proofs and the
release (file 3), and the drop list.

## Decisions

### The maintainer's walk (2026-10-01, through the question tool; every answer the recommended option unless the row says otherwise)

| Question | Answer |
|---|---|
| Where the package goes | Its own **Package 20**, after Package 11 track B and before Package 12, so Package 12 re-proves the final distribution once and its "nothing actionable left" close stays true. Order: Package 19 → Package 18 → Track B → Package 20 → Package 12. |
| The 1.11.0 ops-pack regression (`stamity add ops`, then `sync` fails on Cursor and on Codex alone) | Its own `/st-debug` run before Package 19, merged to `main` without a release; not in this plan. |
| Where each release's slim APM package lives | A folder of the distribution tree, fetched alone (`apm install <owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>`); measured first, with an orphan tag as the fallback. |
| What APM alone can do, and how the CLI joins | APM alone works: every placeholder is filled at generation time and the charter rides through APM where a target can take it. A repository that also runs the CLI gets an `apm-backed` mode in which the CLI writes only the charter, hooks, MCP wiring and state, and `check` fails any class delivered twice. |
| How the package ships | **1.12.0 at its end:** one fresh full eval run (about 3.5 h unattended), QA and the tag. It also carries Packages 19 and 18 and the ops-pack fix. |
| Optional plugin items | **All four:** release channels with a verified promotion and a soak; `plugin setup --declare`; overrides in plugin mode; the review gate on every client. |
| Optional CLI and fork items | **`stamity upgrade` only.** Review reasons in `--json` and `release.json`, `stamity status --json` and a release on lane merge go to the drop list. |
| Accounts for live proofs | **VS Code with Copilot on the maintainer's machine only.** The Copilot organization install, the auto-update legs and Cursor's team marketplace stay `Not done:` with their owner. |

### What 1.11.0 already settled (no unit here)

- **Codex no longer grows the shared `AGENTS.md`.** 1.11.0 moved Codex's rules into a root `AGENTS.override.md`; with Codex selected `AGENTS.md` stays 5,330 bytes and the 25,360-byte override is read by Codex alone (measured 2026-10-01 with the released 1.11.0 CLI; at 1.10.0 the same fixture's `AGENTS.md` grew from 5,279 to 25,277 bytes).
- **Codex gets the nine touchpoint workflows** as shared `.agents/skills/` skills (`$st-<id>`).
- **`test/replay/fixture.test.ts` is gone** with the rest of the replay (`d5d3fee7`).
- **Owned by other packages:** the import without `--mirror`, the lane's release-tag-only fetch with the snapshot sweep, the nightly guard and the records move are Package 18 (`docs/plans/014-lean-repository-01.md`, `-02.md`); the paired with/without value evidence (brief Q5) is Package 11 track B.

### Settled by this plan (declared defaults; the maintainer may reverse any before the run starts)

| # | Default | Why |
|---|---|---|
| S1 | Certain waste goes (Codex skill metadata only when Codex is selected); every other double delivery is measured per reader first, removed only where no client loses its copy, and the residue becomes a capability-matrix row with its reason. | VS Code reads `.claude/` and `.github/` copies additively, while Claude Code, the Copilot CLI and Codex each need their own; a blind dedupe would cost a client its copy. |
| S2 | `stamity upgrade --to <version>` runs the target version's `sync` through the pinned `npx -y <package>@<version>` form and moves the `package.json` dev-dependency pin when one exists; it refuses a changed client set without `--allow-client-change`, a downgrade without `--allow-downgrade` and a major jump without `--allow-major`. | The engine that writes the files must be the target version's; the guards keep a fleet run from changing what a team chose. |
| S3 | The slim APM package lives under `apm/` in each release's distribution tree (`<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>`), chosen by a rule declared before the measurement (with the current apm-cli — the newest at intake, 0.33.0 on 2026-10-06 — a subfolder install costs at most 25% of the whole root's cache, every class deploys on 0.29.1 and the current client, 0.29.1 installs a subfolder); otherwise an orphan `apm/v<version>` tag. The distribution tree keeps its root copy for one compatibility window, so the 1.9–1.11 spec `<owner>/<repo>#plugins/v<version>` keeps working when Renovate moves its ref; the copy goes at the next major release. Kept only if a root install from a tree carrying both copies deploys every primitive once; otherwise the unit stops and the maintainer chooses. Likewise `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` stays as a copy of the new Claude tag `.strict` template until the next major release. | Your convention puts a breaking change in a major release; the old spec is in the wild (release.json named it since 1.9.0). Probe 2026-10-01: a subfolder cost 14.5% of the whole root's cache, because the sparse cone brings the root's top-level files (the four archives, 5,652,504 bytes at `plugins/v1.11.0`; apm's `size=7.31 MB` counts the whole sparse checkout — and they now move to `archives/`). |
| S4 | The APM generator resolves every placeholder with the plugin roots' phrases (REQ-PLUGIN-004); `${STAMITY:CLI}` becomes the pinned call at the package's own version. This reverses the 2026-09-17 decision "APM unchanged", whose revisit trigger (a release of plugin consumers on the phrases) has fired three times. | APM alone has to work. |
| S5 | The charter ships as its own small APM package, `apm-charter/` in the distribution tree, which an APM-only consumer adds beside the main package and an `apm-backed` repository does not (there the CLI's charter, with the detected facts and gates, is the copy of record). Its repo-fact and gate rows read `unknown` (the charter's own honesty rule, REQ-PLUGIN-017) and its invariants version comes from its frontmatter; the main package's 36 other tokens take the plugin phrases (S4). | Probe 2026-10-01 (apm-cli 0.32.0): an instruction with no `applyTo` deploys at install for Claude Code (always-on), Copilot and Cursor, only Codex waits for `apm compile`, `context/` is neither deployed nor folded, and a consumer cannot exclude one instruction of a dependency — so a charter inside the main package would reach every `apm-backed` repository twice. Settles the spec author's marker on REQ-APM-012 with a design none of its three options offered. |
| S6 | The new install-mode value is `apm-backed`, set by `init --install-mode apm-backed` or the config key `installMode`. "Delivered twice" means one client receiving one artifact from APM and from the CLI; one client reading another client's file (Cursor or Copilot listing Codex's touchpoints) is capability-matrix residue, not a failure. `check`'s duplicate row runs in every install mode; a finding whose source is `apm` (read from `apm.lock.yaml`) fails in every mode; findings whose source is `ledger` or `unmanaged` keep today's split — `warn` under `generated`, `fail` under `plugin-backed` — and the accepted mixed state stays. | Settles the marker on REQ-PLUGIN-019 (option a): a repository mid-move to the plugin keeps a green CI, and a class APM and the CLI both deliver still fails. |
| S7 | Hooks and MCP through APM are not built. | microsoft/apm#3129 (Cursor hooks written in the Claude shape; Cursor rejects the whole file) is open on 2026-10-06 and 0.33.0 still writes that shape (measured); the Codex fix (PR #3060) shipped in 0.33.0 (2026-10-02). Note 2026-10-07: apm PR #3149 (merged 2026-10-06, unreleased at v0.33.0) renders native camelCase Cursor hooks, so `.cursor/hooks.json` files will carry both shapes over time, Claude-shaped from 0.33.0 and earlier, native from a later release. `check` names other packages' hooks meanwhile (`u2-apm-deliveries`). |
| S8 | Channels: `stamity.distribution.channels` defaults to `{ stable: "plugin-stable" }`, with an optional `canary`; `plugin-dist` stays the newest release and is not a channel; promotion runs behind a GitHub environment with required reviewers; `soakDays` defaults to 0, and the docs recommend at least three days on canary. | Claude Code has no channel concept and detects updates by version; a moving branch per channel, promoted only after verification, is the vendor-documented pattern. |
| S9 | The managed-settings templates render one per channel and one pinned to the release tag, for Claude Code and for Copilot; `strictKnownMarketplaces` is written only on the organization's opt-in. | A strict allowlist blocks every other marketplace and fails closed on a one-character mismatch. |
| S10 | In plugin mode, overrides, patches and model-pinned agents are emitted as project-level copies with a per-client precedence measured at execution; installed pack skills are exempt from the plugin-owned filter on every client; `check` and `plugin status` report per client what reaches. | The maintainer chose "overrides in plugin mode"; Claude namespaces plugin artifacts, the Copilot CLI lets project skills shadow plugin skills, VS Code prefixes plugin skills. |
| S11 | The review gate maps to a client's completion event only after that client's event and payload are measured; where no event can block, the prose ladder stays and `check` and the docs say so. | The maintainer chose "review gate on all clients"; a gate mapped on an unmeasured payload could block every completion or none. |
| S12 | Cursor's rules become repository-owned `.cursor/rules/*.mdc` in plugin mode and leave the Cursor plugin root; `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` is generated in both modes. | The Cursor CLI reads `.cursor/rules`; plugin rules in the CLI are undocumented; Cursor's Bugbot page says `.cursor/rules` do not apply to Bugbot runs and does not mention plugins (read 2026-10-06). |
| S13 | Live proofs in this file use only what the maintainer's machine has: Claude Code, Codex, the Copilot CLI, the Cursor CLI and VS Code with Copilot. | The maintainer's answer on accounts. |
| S14 | `u2-apm-backed-mode` owns an additive extension to file 1's lock reader, `src/detect/apmLock.ts`: `ApmLockDependency` gains `virtualPath` and `version` (both `string \| null`), and `parseApmLock` falls back to the top-level `deployments[]` rows when a dependency carries no `deployed_files`; file 1's fields, read states (`kind`/`detail`) and `repoUrl: string \| null` keep their shapes. The alternative not taken: fold the extension into file 1's `u1-foreign-paths`. | Taken on the PR #73 review (2026-10-02): file 1 declares the reader file 2 reads, and only file 2's units need these fields; the fallback also widens `apmDeployedPaths` for file 1's consumers on a lock with no `deployed_files`, which only makes their foreign-path guard stricter. |
| S15 | `--json` documents carry `schemaVersion: 2`; a document without it is the ≤1.11.0 shape; the number moves only on a change that is not additive. Warnings are `{ code, severity: "notice" \| "warning", message, path? }` from one registry. Paths inside the repository are relative POSIX; paths outside it stay absolute in named fields. `changedFiles` on a dry run predicts emission and reclaim only. | An orchestrator needs one field to branch on; the two severities are the split `MergeResult` already draws (`notice` plain, `warning` yellow, `src/types/content.ts:229-241`); a dry run cannot know whether the manifest's bytes would change. |
| S16 | The shipped `npm-shrinkwrap.json` is the production and optional subset of the gated `package-lock.json`, written at the root only while `scripts/pack-release.mjs` packs; `files` names it; the runtime builder deletes the extracted copy before `npm ci`. | Measured 2026-10-06: a whole copy of the development lock installs the devDependencies (284 packages); a committed root shrinkwrap would override the repository's own lock; npm packs the file only when `files` names it; the extracted copy makes `npm ci` fail. |
| S17 | The `st` alias becomes a separate entry, `dist/st.js`, that prints one deprecation line and runs the CLI; removed at 2.0.0 (follow-up row). | A notice keyed on `process.argv[1]` misses npm's Windows shims and pnpm's shell shims, which pass the real `cli.js` path; a bin may go only in a major release. |
| S18 | `release.json` moves to schema 2 in one bump (`runtime.tarballIntegrity`, `packages[].rootSha256`, `ownedPaths`); the validator accepts 1 and 2; root digests are sha256-tree-v1; a promotion refuses a schema-1 tag. | One bump instead of three; earlier manifests stay readable; channels start with the first schema-2 release, and a channel must not move to a tree whose roots nothing certifies. |
| S19 | The canonical npm publish becomes idempotent like the fork's (skip when the registry already serves this tarball's integrity; refuse another tarball under the version). | The new post-publish check can fail after an irreversible publish; without this a re-run stops at `npm publish` and never reaches the distribution push or the GitHub release. |
| S20 | Copilot's touchpoints ship only as the shared `.agents/skills/st-<id>/` skills; Copilot's prompt files are no longer written and the next sync removes them; Xcode loses its touchpoints and the capability matrix says so. | Principle 2: VS Code, Visual Studio and JetBrains read both and would list each touchpoint twice; every Copilot surface but Xcode reads skills (cheat sheet, read 2026-10-06). Notes give the other reading. |
| S21 | The locator orders same-identifier prereleases (`^X.Y.Z-<id>.<n>`), release candidates included; other prerelease shapes keep exact equality; released and prerelease stay apart in both directions. | Brief F12: a repository on `-<suffix>.2` then uses its own install under a `-<suffix>.1` root; semver orders `rc.1` before `rc.2` the same way, and one rule is easier to state than two. |
| S22 | The update notice probes only when stderr is a terminal and `--json` is absent; `STAMITY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK` takes `1`, `true` or `yes` in any case; the banner compares with the repository's pin and prints the registry-bound `upgrade --to <v>` (inbox row 394 re-opened). | Principle 6: the banner prints nowhere else, yet every non-interactive verb sent a request a day; the pin is what a fleet changes, and `upgrade` is the supported move. |
| S23 | A tag declaration is `--declare tag` and follows the engine version on `sync` and `upgrade`; its commit pin is operator input (`--declare-sha`, `config set plugin.declarationSha`), written as `sha` in `.claude/settings.json` only while its version is the engine's, and dropped with a warning when the engine moves; Copilot's declaration carries no `sha`. | Principle 6 (offline, away from credentials): the distribution commit exists only after the release pushes, so no engine carries its own; a stale `sha` would hold clients on the old release while the files move on; Copilot's `github` source documents no `sha`. |
| S24 | Each Claude managed template's `requiredMinimumVersion` is the highest cited floor of the keys it writes; a key whose introducing release is unstated keeps 2.1.277. The `sha` rides only on release-asset copies of the Claude tag templates, stamped after the push. | Brief C1: two floors disagreed in one release without a reason; a floor below a key's support could let a client ignore a pin; a tree cannot name its own commit. |
| S25 | P7: the Copilot catalog is the one the Copilot CLI reads first; if a pre-merge VS Code walk shows VS Code reading `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`, a root-level `marketplace.json` naming the `copilot/` root is added only if a re-walk proves it and the APM legs stay green; otherwise the unit stops for the maintainer. | The Claude catalog cannot change roots without breaking Claude Code; a new root file the Copilot CLI reads first must still be proved on VS Code and on APM's format detection. |
| S26 | The Claude-layout scripts skip a foreign client's run only when that client is selected; otherwise they act as today. | The skip's reason is the client's own copy of the hook; without one, the imported hook is the only stamity hook that client runs. |
| S27 | The Cursor cloud-agent gap is a capability-matrix row and one line on `check`'s existing `plugin-runtime` row, with no status change and no new doctor row. | It is a fact about where the plugin cannot load, which `plugin-runtime` already answers; a status change would fail every plugin-backed Cursor repository for a vendor limit it cannot fix. |
| S28 | "The current apm-cli" is the newest release on PyPI at the run's intake — 0.33.0 (2026-10-02) as of 2026-10-06 — recorded with its upload date; every `0.33.0` literal in file 02 follows it. | The plan named 0.32.0 and 0.33.0 shipped four days later; a fixed literal goes stale between planning and the run. |
| S29 | S7 stands (hooks and MCP through APM are not built). Its trigger half fired: PR #3060 shipped in 0.33.0; #3129 is open and 0.33.0 still writes Claude's shape into `.cursor/hooks.json` (measured 2026-10-06; note 2026-10-07: apm PR #3149, merged 2026-10-06 and unreleased at v0.33.0, renders native camelCase Cursor hooks, so both shapes will meet over time). Meanwhile `check` names what any APM package places (REQ-APM-017). | Shipping stamity hooks through APM today would disable every Cursor hook in the file, this setup's guards included. |
| S30 | `apm-deliveries` fails on a `.cursor/hooks.json` Cursor rejects (whoever wrote the entry) (an APM server table in `.codex/config.toml` is listed, not failed: file 0 owns that file per table, its S16); it warns on executables placed without an `apm.yml` gate and on stale sidecar entries; otherwise it passes with a listing. Malformed entries under a valid key fail too until Cursor's reaction is measured. | Fail only where this setup's own protection or files break; APM's gate is the consumer's policy, so its absence is advisory. |
| S31 | A plugin declaration (C14) warns under `generated`, fails under `apm-backed`, and is not a finding under `plugin-backed`. | Generated mid-migration is legitimate (S6's precedent); `apm-backed` would load three copies; `plugin-backed` declares on purpose (u2-declare). |
| S32 | APM hook and MCP duplicates fail in every mode, Copilot's two MCP documents (`.github/mcp.json` from APM, `.vscode/mcp.json` here) included. Alternative not taken: warn on the Copilot cross-document case until its readers are measured. | One rule for the `apm` source (S6); the remedy is one line. |
| S33 | The skew check runs under `apm-backed` only, compares the stamity dependency's lock `version` with `manifest.generatedBy`, warns within a major and fails across majors. | Principle 1 (one release, one version); a different major refuses the other's state (brief C4). |
| S34 | The archives move to `archives/`, named by `packages[].archivePath` on `u2-release-integrity`'s single schema bump, with no compatibility copy at the root. Alternative not taken: keep schema 1 and fix the location by convention (README and REQ-PLUGIN-011). | A root copy keeps the cone heavy, which is the move's purpose; asset names do not change, so only tree-path fetchers move. |
| S35 | Renovate grouping: `groupName: "stamity"` in `renovate/apm.json` and `renovate/companion.json`; `minimumGroupSize: 2` only in the guide's opt-in rule for a repository holding both pins. | A preset cannot know whether a repository holds both pins, and `minimumGroupSize` would block a one-pin repository forever. |
| S36 | The guide passes `GITHUB_APM_PAT` through `secrets` + `customEnvVariables` and sets `APM_NO_SCRIPTS=1`; the preset sets no `env`. | Renovate's `env` is top-level only and needs the admin's `allowedEnv`; a raw secret in `customEnvVariables` may be logged (Renovate's own warning). |
| S37 | A8 under `apm-backed` is reported (`check`, `validate --json`), never answered with project copies. | APM writes the same paths the CLI would; file 1's foreign-path guard refuses them. |
| S38 | The live Renovate run (A11c) is a maintainer-run rehearsal on the maintainer's own private fixture repositories, recorded with versions, not a scheduled job; CI carries the offline bump with the same verdict function. | No new account is needed (the 2026-09-22 rehearsal), but it needs the maintainer's tokens and Renovate on Node ≥ 24.11. |
| S39 | A new unit, `u2-apm-deliveries`, carries the new doctor row, A8 and the skew check; `u2-apm-backed-mode` keeps everything that decides "delivered twice". | Backed-mode is already the largest unit; the row is its own contract (doctor id, module, docs row, tests). |

to a token: offline unless asked, never interactive, kept away from the caller's credentials. Eight research passes

repositories, mapped each to this plan, and re-read the vendor documentation. Five security defects of the released

#### REQ-FLOW-033 — Each client gets each artifact once where its readers allow (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-repo-file-waste`. Generated mode writes no file that no selected client reads, and no
second copy of an artifact a selected client already gets, wherever removing it costs no client its copy.

- **Certain waste, removed.** Codex skill metadata (`agents/openai.yaml` beside a `SKILL.md`) is written only when Codex
  is selected, and only under `.agents/skills/`, the one skills folder Codex reads; no copy goes under `.claude/skills/`.
- **Measured first, then removed or documented.** The nine touchpoint skills in `.agents/skills/` that also reach
  Copilot, which has them in `.github/prompts/`; the rule-skills Codex adds to the shared `.agents/skills/` that Cursor and
  Copilot read; skill folders written byte-identical to both `.claude/skills/` and `.agents/skills/` (VS Code reads both);
  `.claude/rules/` and `.github/instructions/`, both read by VS Code's Local agent. Each is removed only on a dated vendor
  citation per reader showing no client loses its copy.
- **The residue** — every double delivery that stays — is a row in the capability matrix, per client pair, with its
  reason.

- **Units:** `u2-repo-file-waste`.
- **Evidence (before):** the touchpoint companion is `TOUCHPOINT_POLICY_FILE = "agents/openai.yaml"`
  (`src/emit/skillsProjection.ts:529`), emitted with every touchpoint row (`:563-593`) whenever Codex or Cursor emits;
  the bundled skills ship their own `agents/openai.yaml` (`:525-528`). Measured 2026-10-01: three-client generated mode
  (claude, cursor, copilot) writes 181 files (172 at 1.10.0) against 29 plugin-backed, 25 of them `agents/openai.yaml`
  with no Codex selected; 10 skill folders are byte-identical in `.claude/skills/` and `.agents/skills/`.
- **Proof:** `test/emit/skillsProjection.test.ts`, `test/emit/touchpointSkills.test.ts`, the cross-client goldens,
  `test/emit/capabilityMatrix.test.ts`; the measurement record per reader; QA.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-033**):
- GIVEN tools `[claude, cursor, copilot]` WHEN `sync` runs THEN no emitted path ends in `agents/openai.yaml`; GIVEN tools
  `[claude, codex]` THEN no path under `.claude/skills/` ends in `agents/openai.yaml`.
- GIVEN tools `[codex, cursor]` THEN each of the nine touchpoint directories carries `agents/openai.yaml`; WHEN codex is

#### REQ-FLOW-034 — `stamity upgrade --to <version>` moves the pins and rewrites the engine's files in one step (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-upgrade-verb`. A new verb:
`stamity upgrade --to <version> [--json] [--allow-client-change] [--allow-downgrade] [--allow-major]`. It resolves
`<version>` in the running package's own registry, runs that version's `sync` through the pinned
`npx -y <package>@<version> sync` form, moves the `package.json` dev-dependency pin when one exists, never touches user
state, and lists the files it changed. It refuses, writing nothing, when the target would change the selected client set
(unless `--allow-client-change`), when the target is older (unless `--allow-downgrade`), and when the target is a
different major version (unless `--allow-major`).

- **Units:** `u2-upgrade-verb`.
- **Evidence (before):** no `upgrade` verb exists (exit 2), and `sync --help` says none does (`src/cli/commands/sync.ts:63-74`).
  A new verb moves seven hand-kept surface pins (learning `surface-pins-are-literals-that-drift`), two of them arrays in
  `test/docsPages.test.ts`.
- **Expand/contract:** additive; no existing verb changes. Rollback is `upgrade --to <prior> --allow-downgrade`, or the
  pinned sync at the prior version.
- **Proof:** `test/cli/commands/upgrade.test.ts` over fixture repositories with a stubbed registry;
  `test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts`; `test/docsPages.test.ts`; QA.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-034**):
- GIVEN a setup at version A with `@<pkg>` pinned in `devDependencies` WHEN `upgrade --to B` runs (B newer, same major)
  THEN the pin reads B, the manifest's `generatedBy` reads B, and the files written equal what `npx -y <pkg>@B sync`
  writes on a copy of the same tree.
- GIVEN the same with no `package.json` THEN the files change and no `package.json` is created.
- GIVEN learnings, handoffs, overrides and run records THEN each is byte-identical after the upgrade.
- GIVEN a target whose sync would change the selected clients THEN the verb exits 1 naming the change and writes nothing;
  with `--allow-client-change` it proceeds.
- GIVEN an older target THEN it exits 1 unless `--allow-downgrade`; GIVEN a different major THEN it exits 1 unless
  `--allow-major`.
- GIVEN a version the registry does not have THEN it exits 1 naming the version and writes nothing.
- GIVEN `--json` THEN one document lists every changed path, with `ok`, `command` and `version`.
- GIVEN the README's commands list, the CLI reference and the docs pins THEN each names `upgrade`.

#### REQ-FLOW-047 — `--json` documents follow one versioned contract (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-json-contract`. Every command run with `--json` that reaches its action writes one
document whose envelope carries `ok`, `command`, `version` and `schemaVersion: 2`. A document without `schemaVersion`
is the shape 1.11.0 and earlier printed. The number moves when a key is removed or renamed, or a type or a meaning
changes; an added key does not move it.

- **Warnings.** Every `warnings` array holds objects `{ code, severity, message, path? }`: `code` is a stable name from
  the registry the CLI reference prints, `severity` is `notice` or `warning`, `message` is the line the human output
  prints, and `path` is the repository-relative file the warning is about, when there is one.
- **Paths.** Every path field that names a file or folder inside the repository is repository-relative with `/`
  separators. A location outside the repository stays absolute, in the fields the CLI reference lists.
- **Changed files.** `init`, `sync`, `clean`, `plugin setup` and `upgrade` list every file whose bytes the run changed
  in `changedFiles: { path, action }[]`, sorted by path, `action` being `created`, `updated` or `deleted`, with the
  manifest and `.gitignore` among them. Under `--dry-run` the list predicts emitted and reclaimed files only.
- **The Copilot advisory** (`copilot-hook-fallback`) appears only on a run that created or updated
  `.github/hooks/stamity.json` while that file wires a session-start hook.

- **Units:** `u2-json-contract`.
- **Evidence (before):** the envelope is `ok`, `command`, `version` (`src/cli/kit/output.ts:36-52`,
  `src/cli/kit/program.ts:362`); warnings are strings (`src/types/content.ts:145`,
  `src/cli/commands/sync/report.ts:347`); `init` publishes the writer's absolute paths (`src/cli/commands/init/apply.ts:320`,
  `:377`) and `plugin setup` the same (`src/cli/commands/plugin.ts:408-409`). Measured 2026-10-06 (r3): `init` 201 of
  201 paths absolute, `plugin setup` 14 of 14; a no-op `sync --json` lists no changed file while the manifest changed;
  the Copilot advisory prints on every run (`src/adapters/copilot.ts:327`), plugin mode included.
- **Expand/contract:** the warning type and the path spelling change for a consumer, which `schemaVersion: 2` names; the
  other keys are additive. Rollback: the prior release's unversioned documents.
- **Proof:** `test/cli/kit/jsonContract.test.ts`, `test/cli/jsonContract.e2e.test.ts`, the command suites,
  `test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts`.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-047**):
- GIVEN any advertised command run with `--json` that reaches its action WHEN it exits 0 or 1 THEN the document carries
  `schemaVersion: 2` beside `ok`, `command` and `version`.
- GIVEN `init -y --tools claude,cursor,copilot,codex --json` in a fixture THEN no string value in the document starts
  with the fixture's absolute path, and `report.manifestPath` reads `.stamity/manifest.json`.
- GIVEN a user hook file that does not parse WHEN `sync --json` runs THEN `warnings` holds an object with
  `code: "hook-rejected"`, `severity: "warning"` and the hook file as `path`.
- GIVEN a Copilot setup WHEN a second `sync --json` changes nothing THEN `warnings` holds no `copilot-hook-fallback`;
  GIVEN a plugin-backed Copilot setup THEN no document holds it.
- GIVEN a live `sync --json` that rewrote the manifest THEN `changedFiles` holds
  `{ path: ".stamity/manifest.json", action: "updated" }`.
- GIVEN the CLI reference THEN it documents `schemaVersion`, the warning object, the path rule, `changedFiles` and one
  row per warning code.

#### REQ-FLOW-048 — The update notice asks the registry only when its banner can show, and names the pinned upgrade (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-upgrade-verb` (amended 2026-10-06).

- **When it asks.** Only when stderr is a terminal and the run has no `--json`. `STAMITY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK` set to `1`,
  `true` or `yes`, in any letter case, switches it off; `NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER` and `CI` do on any non-empty value.
- **What it compares.** The registry's newest version with the repository's pin: the package's own entry in the root
  `package.json` (`devDependencies`, else `dependencies`; an exact version or the version of a `^`/`~` range), else the
  manifest's `generatedBy`, else the running version.
- **What it prints.** `Update available: <pin> -> <new>. To move: <pinned call at <new>> upgrade --to <new>. To stay
  on <pin>, do nothing.`, with `--allow-major` when the majors differ; a target below the running version, which may
  predate the verb, gets the pinned `sync` form instead. For a package naming `publishConfig.registry` the pinned call is
  file 0's registry-bound form, so the fetch asks the registry the notice asked.

- **Units:** `u2-upgrade-verb`.
- **Evidence (before):** the probe fires before every command (`src/cli.ts:125-131`) and the banner prints only to a
  terminal (`:141-144`); the opt-out matches exactly `1` (`src/cli/notice/updateNotice.ts:207-208`); the banner compares
  with the running version and prints the pinned `sync` (`:310-323`). Measured 2026-10-06: five verbs, non-TTY, each
  sent one request and wrote the stamp; `STAMITY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK=true` still probed (r2 P6-1, r3 C11.1); a fork's banner
  named no registry while its probe asked the fork's (r2 M15).
- **Expand/contract:** fewer probes; a wider opt-out. Rollback: the prior release.
- **Proof:** `test/cli/notice/updateNotice.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/upgrade.test.ts`.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-048**):
- GIVEN stderr that is not a terminal, or `--json`, and no opt-out WHEN any command runs THEN the notice makes no
  request and writes no stamp.
- GIVEN `STAMITY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK` set to `true`, `YES` or `1` THEN no request; GIVEN `0` or `false` THEN the notice runs.
- GIVEN a root `package.json` pinning the package at 1.12.0, a running CLI at 1.13.0 and a registry answering 1.13.0
  THEN the banner reads `Update available: 1.12.0 -> 1.13.0. To move: npx -y <name>@1.13.0 upgrade --to 1.13.0. To stay
  on 1.12.0, do nothing.`; GIVEN the pin at 1.13.0 THEN no banner.
- GIVEN a registry answer of another major THEN the move command ends `--allow-major`.
- GIVEN a package naming `publishConfig.registry` THEN the move command names that registry in file 0's
  registry-bound form.

#### REQ-FLOW-049 — The published package carries the dependency tree it was tested with (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-shrinkwrap`. The package ships an `npm-shrinkwrap.json` holding the production and
optional rows of the `package-lock.json` the release was gated with: no row marked `dev`, a `devOptional` row kept as
`optional`, and a root row without `devDependencies`. The file exists in the working tree only while
`scripts/pack-release.mjs` packs, so the repository's own installs keep reading `package-lock.json`. The runtime a
plugin root bundles keeps installing from the repository lock. The `st` alias still works, prints one deprecation line
on stderr, and goes at 2.0.0.

- **Units:** `u2-shrinkwrap`.
- **Evidence (before):** `files: ["dist"]` and no shrinkwrap (`package.json:41-43`); caret ranges (`:63-71`);
  `bin.st` (`:30-33`). Measured 2026-10-06: a fresh resolution gives `@types/node` 26.6.4 and `ip-address` 10.7.3
  against the tested 26.6.3 and 10.7.2 (r2 M13). Lab (planning lab; npm 10.9.8 and 11.21.0; a one-package registry stub for the
  scope): npm packs the file only when `files` names it; a registry install and `npx -y` honour it only when the
  registry flags the version `_hasShrinkwrap: true`, which `npm publish` does not send and the public registry sets
  itself; a local tarball install and `npx --package <file>` ignore it; a copy of the development lock installs the
  devDependencies (284 packages); the runtime builder's `npm ci` fails `EUSAGE` until the extracted copy is removed.
- **Expand/contract:** additive for consumers; a registry that does not flag it behaves as today. Rollback: a release
  without the file.
- **Proof:** `test/ci/packRelease.test.ts`, `scripts/tarball-smoke.mjs`, `test/ci/pluginRuntime.test.ts`,
  `test/cli/stAlias.e2e.test.ts`, the measurement record.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-049**):
- GIVEN the packed release tarball THEN it lists `package/npm-shrinkwrap.json`, no row of it is marked `dev`, its root
  row has no `devDependencies`, and every production and optional package of the tested lock has a row.
- GIVEN the repository after a pack THEN no `npm-shrinkwrap.json` exists at its root.
- GIVEN the runtime build from that tarball THEN its `npm ci` succeeds against the repository lock.
- GIVEN `st --version` THEN stdout equals `stamity --version`'s and stderr carries the deprecation line; GIVEN
  `stamity --version` THEN stderr is empty.

#### REQ-FLOW-050 — Copilot gets each touchpoint once, as a shared skill every skill-reading surface loads (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-copilot-touchpoints`. When Copilot is selected, sync emits each touchpoint as the shared
skill `.agents/skills/st-<id>/SKILL.md` with `disable-model-invocation: true`, co-owned with Cursor and Codex when
either is selected, and writes no `.github/prompts/st-<id>.prompt.md`. The Copilot CLI, VS Code (the Local agent and the
Copilot agent host), Visual Studio, JetBrains (preview) and the cloud agent load skills; none of them lists a touchpoint
twice. Xcode reads prompt files (preview) and no skills, so it lists none, which the capability matrix says. The
Copilot plugin root carries the touchpoints under `skills/st-<id>/` as class `command`.

- **Units:** `u2-copilot-touchpoints`.
- **Evidence (before):** Copilot writes prompt files only (`src/adapters/copilot.ts:302-311`, `:482-494`); measured
  2026-10-06 (r5): a Copilot-only setup has nine prompt files and no touchpoint skill; inbox row 479: the Copilot CLI and
  VS Code's agent host do not load prompt files. GitHub's customization cheat sheet and VS Code's agent-skills page,
  read 2026-10-06 (cited in the unit).
- **Expand/contract:** Copilot's prompt files are removed by the next sync's reclaim; rollback is the prior release's
  sync, which writes them again.
- **Proof:** `test/adapters/copilot.test.ts`, `test/emit/touchpointSkills.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts`,
  `test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts`, `test/emit/capabilityMatrix.test.ts`.

Criteria (merged as **REQ-FLOW-050**):
- GIVEN tools `[copilot]` WHEN `sync` runs THEN nine `.agents/skills/st-<id>/SKILL.md` files exist with
  `disable-model-invocation: true` and no `.github/prompts/st-<id>.prompt.md` exists.
- GIVEN tools `[copilot, cursor]` THEN each touchpoint `SKILL.md` is written once and owned by both clients.
- GIVEN a ledger from 1.11.0 holding the nine prompt files THEN the next sync removes them and leaves every other file
  under `.github/` byte-identical.
- GIVEN the capability matrix THEN Copilot's command surface names the surfaces that load the skills and Xcode, which
  loads none.

#### REQ-FLOW-022 — First-run output matches what setup did (MODIFIED)

Replaces `docs/specs/everyday-flows.md:471-477`, the two bullets "**The update notice** reads `Update available:
<current> -> <new>. To move: npx -y <name>@<new> sync. To stay on <current>, do nothing.` …" and "**`sync --help`**
names the exact-version form with a `<version>` placeholder. …". New text:

- **The update notice** reads `Update available: <pin> -> <new>. To move: <pinned call at <new>> upgrade --to <new>.
  To stay on <pin>, do nothing.`, where `<pin>` is the repository's pin and the call is registry-bound for a package
  that names a registry (REQ-FLOW-048). Both halves name one normalized version, and it never says `@latest`. A package
  with no npm channel is `private`, so it never reaches the notice.
- **`sync --help`** names `upgrade --to <version>` (REQ-FLOW-034) and the pinned `sync` as the regenerate-only form,
  in the same registry-bound spelling. A package with no npm channel keeps its line: install the newer release into the
  project, then `npx --no <name> sync`.

(Amended `<date>`, plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-upgrade-verb`; they read as quoted.) The criterion at `:850-852` becomes:
"GIVEN a repository pinned at 1.12.0 and a registry answering 1.13.0 WHEN the notice prints THEN it contains
`npx -y <name>@1.13.0 upgrade --to 1.13.0` and a way to stay, and no `@latest`. GIVEN the registry answer `v1.13.0` THEN
both halves of the banner name `1.13.0`."

### B. `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md`

#### REQ-APM-010 — Each release ships a slim APM package (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, units `u2-apm-sparse-measure` and `u2-apm-slim-package`. Each release's distribution tree carries
the APM package — `apm.yml` and `.apm/` — under `apm/`, installed as `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>`, and
`release.json` names that spec as `apm.installSpec`. The release generates the copy with the same generator; a test holds
it byte-identical to the source tree's projection for the same commit. The source tree keeps its root `apm.yml` and
`.apm/`, so `<owner>/<repo>#v<version>` keeps working. The distribution tree also keeps its root copy for one
compatibility window, so a consumer pinned to the 1.9–1.11 spec `<owner>/<repo>#plugins/v<version>` keeps working when
Renovate moves its ref; the docs mark that spec deprecated, and the root copy goes at the next major release.

A measurement comes first, with apm-cli 0.32.0 and 0.29.1 and a rule declared before the run: the folder form stands when,
with 0.32.0 in its default cache mode, a subfolder install's cache is at most 25% of the whole distribution root's, a local
fixture with the package under `apm/` deploys every class for every target on both clients, and 0.29.1 installs a
subfolder through its subdirectory route; otherwise the slim package moves to an orphan tag `apm/v<version>` holding only
the package. The same run installs the root spec from a tree carrying both copies and keeps the root copy only when every
primitive deploys once; when one deploys twice, the unit stops and the maintainer chooses between a breaking change at the
next major release and a dependency-only root `apm.yml` that redirects to the folder.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-sparse-measure` (the research gate), `u2-apm-slim-package`.
- **Evidence:** the distribution tree carries `apm.yml` and `.apm/` at its root today
  (`scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:9-13`, `:513`); `release.json`'s `apm.installSpec` is built and validated in
  `scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs:33`, `:215-223`. Probe 2026-10-01 (apm-cli 0.32.0, fresh cache each run): the whole
  distribution root `#plugins/v1.11.0` cost 65,240 KB of cache and vendored 2,883 files; the one-file subfolder
  `admin#plugins/v1.11.0` cost 9,480 KB (14.5%), because the sparse cone always brings the root's top-level files, the four
  plugin archives (about 7.3 MB) among them.
- **Expand/contract:** expand — the slim copy and the new spec are added and the old spec keeps working through the
  window. Rollback: the next release builds without `apm/`, and consumers keep the root spec.
- **Proof:** `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`, `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts`, `test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts`; the
  measurement record; the slim-ref smoke of REQ-APM-016.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the measurement record THEN it names both client versions, every arm with its cache bytes, `apm_modules` bytes and
  file count, deployed file count and exit code, its date, the verdict line, and the dual-copy result.
- GIVEN a distribution build (folder form) THEN `apm/apm.yml` and `apm/.apm/**` exist, every file under `apm/` is
  byte-identical to the checkout's `apm.yml` and `.apm/` for that commit, and the root copy is byte-identical too.
- GIVEN `release.json` THEN `apm.installSpec` equals `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>` (or the fallback tag's spec,
  when the record chose it), and the validator refuses any other shape.
- GIVEN installs from the slim spec, the root distribution spec and the source-tree spec at one version THEN the deployed
  files are byte-identical.

#### REQ-APM-011 — The APM package carries no unresolved placeholder (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-placeholders`. The APM generator resolves every `${STAMITY:*}` token at generation time
with the mapping REQ-PLUGIN-004 states for plugin roots: the gate and fact tokens read as the charter-reference phrases,
and `${STAMITY:CLI}` as the pinned call at the package's own version. `--check` fails, naming the file and the token,
when any `${STAMITY:` remains. This reverses the 2026-09-17 decision "APM unchanged".

- **Units:** `u2-apm-placeholders`.
- **Evidence:** 36 `${STAMITY:*}` occurrences in 11 files over 6 tokens (`CLI` ×12; `VERIFY_GATE_ALL`, `_TEST`, `_LINT`,
  `_TYPECHECK` and `TEST_FRAMEWORK` ×24) ship unresolved on purpose (`scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:92-99`); the phrases
  are `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs`, bound to `REPO_SUBSTITUTION_TOKENS` (REQ-PLUGIN-004).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts`; `node scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs --check`; a test binding the APM mapping
  to the plugin mapping.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the committed `.apm/` and `apm.yml` THEN no file contains `${STAMITY:`.
- GIVEN a corpus body with an unknown `${STAMITY:` token WHEN the generator runs THEN it exits 1 naming the file and the
  token, and writes nothing.
- GIVEN one token's phrase changed in `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs` WHEN `--check` runs on the committed tree THEN it
  exits 1, so the two mappings cannot part.
- GIVEN the APM body of `st-handoff` THEN its CLI calls read `npx -y <package>@<version>` at the package's version.

#### REQ-APM-012 — The charter reaches APM-only consumers as its own package (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-charter`. The charter ships as its own small APM package, `apm-charter/` in each
release's distribution tree (`<owner>/<repo>/apm-charter#plugins/v<version>`), which an APM-only consumer adds beside the
main package and an `apm-backed` repository does not: there the CLI's charter, with the detected facts and gates, is the
copy of record. The charter package holds one instruction with no `applyTo`; its repo-fact and gate rows read `unknown`,
the charter's own honesty rule for unknown facts, and its invariants version comes from the charter's frontmatter, so no
`${STAMITY:` token remains. The docs list, per target, how the charter reaches the consumer: Claude Code, Copilot and
Cursor take it at install as an always-on instruction; Codex takes it through `apm compile`. A target that cannot deliver
it is listed as such, never presented as covered.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-charter`.
- **Evidence:** the generator leaves the charter out by settled scope (`scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:161-170`); the
  charter's fact and gate rows are `content/charter/stamity-charter.md:23-35` and its invariants token `:38`. Probe
  2026-10-01 (apm-cli 0.32.0): `apm install` deploys an instruction with no `applyTo` straight away — to `.claude/rules/`
  with no frontmatter (always-on), to `.github/instructions/` and to `.cursor/rules/` — and only codex waits for
  `apm compile`; a `context/` primitive is neither deployed nor folded; a consumer cannot exclude one instruction of a
  dependency (the filters are `skill_subset` and `target_subset`). So the charter cannot ride in the main package without
  reaching every `apm-backed` repository twice, with `unknown` facts beside the detected ones.
- **Proof:** `test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts`; the APM smoke per target; the docs pin.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the generated charter package THEN it carries exactly one instruction with no `applyTo`, holding no `${STAMITY:`
  token, its repo-fact and gate rows reading `unknown`, and the main package carries no charter.
- GIVEN apm-cli 0.29.1 and 0.32.0 WHEN a consumer installs both packages for each target THEN the deployed or compiled
  output contains the charter's `## Invariants` heading exactly once per target the docs list as delivered (measured at
  execution, per target).
- GIVEN a consumer in mode `apm-backed` that installed the charter package anyway WHEN `check` runs THEN the duplicates row
  fails naming the charter's two copies.
- GIVEN the docs' per-target table THEN every target the smoke covers has a row, and each row says delivered or not
  delivered, and how.

#### REQ-APM-013 — An APM-backed install mode, and a duplicate check in every mode (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-backed-mode`. The install mode gains the value `apm-backed` beside `generated` and
`plugin-backed`, set by `init --install-mode apm-backed` or the config key `installMode`. In it, the CLI writes only what
APM does not carry: the charter, hooks, MCP wiring and state, and — because APM gives Codex no commands — Codex's nine
touchpoint skills. `check`'s duplicates row runs in every install mode and reads the deployed paths `apm.lock.yaml`
records (the reader file 1 introduced). "Delivered twice" means one client receives one artifact through two
routes, APM and the CLI, for that client; one client reading another client's file (Cursor or Copilot listing Codex's
touchpoints in `.agents/skills/`) is residue, listed in the capability matrix and not failed. An unparseable `apm.yml` or
`apm.lock.yaml` is a failing line of the duplicates row naming the file. A dependency on a mirror under another owner is
matched through the lock's deployed paths, not by name.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-backed-mode`. The manifest schema is a shared contract (REQ-PLUGIN-014 as amended below).
- **Evidence (before):** the mode is one value per repository, `generated` or `plugin-backed`
  (`src/manifest/manifest.ts:1172`); duplicate detection returns `pass` when no client records a plugin
  (`src/cli/commands/check.ts:996-999`); the `apm` source matches dependency lines by name
  (`src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:628-632`, `:665-677`, `:704-723`); an unparseable `apm.yml` reports nothing and a
  mirror under another owner is not reported (`docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:769-774`; two inbox rows).
- **Expand/contract:** expand — the value, the key and the flag are added; `generated` and `plugin-backed` behave as
  before apart from the duplicates row's reach. An engine at 1.11.0 refuses a manifest whose mode it does not know
  (the REQ-PLUGIN-014 "exactly"), so a downgrade fails loudly rather than re-emitting APM's classes. Rollback:
  `config set installMode generated`, then the prior version's sync.
- **Proof:** `test/manifest/`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/init.test.ts`, the coexistence leg of
  REQ-APM-016; QA.

Criteria:
- GIVEN mode `apm-backed`, tools `[claude, copilot]` and a lock deploying the stamity package's agents, commands, rules
  and skills WHEN `sync` runs THEN it writes the charter, hook configuration, MCP documents and state, and 0 files of
  those four classes.
- GIVEN mode `generated` and the same lock WHEN `check` runs THEN it exits 1 with a duplicates row naming each class, its
  paths and the source `apm`; GIVEN mode `apm-backed` THEN that row passes.
- GIVEN mode `apm-backed` with Codex and Cursor selected THEN the nine touchpoint skills are written, the row passes, and
  the capability matrix lists the cross-read.
- GIVEN an `apm.lock.yaml` or an `apm.yml` that does not parse THEN `check` exits 1 with a duplicates line naming the
  file.
- GIVEN a lock deploying the stamity package from `acme/stamity-mirror` THEN the duplicates row finds it.
- GIVEN `init -y --install-mode apm-backed` and `config set installMode <value>` THEN both record the mode, and any other
  value exits 1 naming the three values.

#### REQ-APM-014 — The client floor is checked and the current client is tested (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-floor-current`. CI and the release's `apm-route` job test 0.29.1 (the floor) and
0.32.0 (current), and CI keeps 0.29.0 as the `--expect-failure` witness. A new doctor row, `apm-client`, reads
`apm_version` from the consumer's `apm.lock.yaml` and fails below 0.29.1, naming the found version, the floor and the
upgrade remedy; with no lock it passes, saying so.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-floor-current`.
- **Evidence (before):** CI tests 0.29.1, 0.30.0 and 0.29.0 `--expect-failure` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:378-450`,
  `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:109-132`); the release `apm-route` job tests 0.30.0 only (`.github/workflows/release.yml:554-623`,
  `:652`). Newest is 0.33.0 (2026-10-02); 0.31.0 and later exit 1 on a zero deploy (microsoft/apm PR #2806), re-measured at
  execution. `apm_version` exists only in the lockfile (`docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:88-89`).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`; the smoke runs.

Criteria:
- GIVEN `ci.yml` THEN the APM smoke matrix is exactly 0.29.1, 0.33.0 and 0.29.0 with `--expect-failure` on 0.29.0 only.
- GIVEN `release.yml`'s `apm-route` job THEN it runs the smoke at 0.29.1 and 0.32.0.
- GIVEN a lock whose `apm_version` is `0.29.0` WHEN `check` runs THEN the `apm-client` row fails naming `0.29.0`, `0.29.1`
  and the upgrade command; GIVEN `0.29.1` or newer THEN it passes; GIVEN no lock THEN it passes with "no apm.lock.yaml".

#### REQ-APM-015 — A Renovate preset tracks the slim APM ref (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-renovate-preset`. A new preset, `renovate/apm.json`, configures Renovate's `apm`
manager with `pinDigests` and one group for stamity's APM pins, matched by dependency name (`matchDepNames`), because the
manager's package name is the repository for both the source-tree and the slim dependency. Rule 0 covers the
distribution packages (`<owner>/<repo>/apm`, `<owner>/<repo>/apm-charter`) with regex versioning on `plugins/v<X.Y.Z>`;
rule 1 covers the source tree (`<owner>/<repo>`) with semver versioning on `v<X.Y.Z>`. The canonical preset is strict.
`scripts/fork-identity.mjs` retargets both rules to the fork's slug, and with `--suffix <id>` requires the fork's own
`-<id>.<n>` in both: rule 0's template becomes `tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix: "plugins/v", suffix: <id>, required:
true })` (file 1), and rule 1 gains `ignoreUnstable: false` and an `allowedVersions` pattern that needs the
suffix — required, unlike the plugins preset, because a fork imported by Package 18's recipe carries upstream's `v*` tags,
which an optional suffix would offer to its consumers. Its description and the docs say the manager reads only the string
form `owner/repo[/path]#<ref>` and drops object entries silently, and runs `apm install`, so `apm` must be on Renovate's
`PATH` with `GITHUB_APM_PAT` for a private repository.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-renovate-preset`.
- **Evidence:** `renovate/` holds only `plugins.json` and `companion.json`; the `apm` manager reads `depName` as the text
  before `#` and `packageName` as the first two segments, parses `LooseArray(z.string()).catch([])`, defaults to
  `semver-coerced` versioning and runs `apm install` (renovatebot/renovate `lib/modules/manager/apm/`, read 2026-10-01;
  the upstream-lane spec records the ordering rule).
- **Proof:** a preset test beside `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`; `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the canonical `renovate/apm.json` THEN it parses as a Renovate preset with `pinDigests: true` on both rules, rule
  0's pattern matches `plugins/v1.12.0` and neither `plugins/v1.12.0-rc.1` nor `plugins/v1.12.0-acme.1`, and no rule
  carries `ignoreUnstable: false`.
- GIVEN `fork-identity.mjs --repository <fork> --suffix acme` THEN both rules name the fork's dependencies, rule 0 matches
  `plugins/v1.12.0-acme.1` and `plugins/v1.12.0-acme.2` and not `plugins/v1.12.0` or `plugins/v1.12.0-other.1`, and rule 1
  allows `v1.12.0-acme.1` and not `v1.12.0`.
- GIVEN the APM docs THEN they state the string-form limit and the `PATH` and token needs.

#### REQ-APM-016 — The APM route is proved next to another APM package, and a fork release smoke-tests its slim ref (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-coexistence-fork`. A CI leg installs the slim package from a local distribution build
next to another APM fixture package. `fork-release.yml` gains an `apm-route` job with `contents: read`, after the
publish job, that smoke-tests the fork's slim ref and prints the install spec; the publish job, which holds the write
credentials, runs no package manager.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-coexistence-fork`.
- **Evidence (before):** `fork-release.yml` runs only `generate-apm-package.mjs --check` (`:315-321`, `:842`); no leg
  installs beside another package; the publish job's own comment keeps package managers out of it
  (`.github/workflows/release.yml:670-672`, mirrored in the fork workflow).
- **Proof:** the CI leg; `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`; `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the leg WHEN both packages install THEN no deployed path collides, and the lock records both.
- GIVEN that consumer in mode `apm-backed` WHEN `sync` and `check` run THEN no class is written twice and `check` exits 0.
- GIVEN the same consumer in mode `generated` WHEN `check` runs THEN it exits 1 with the duplicates row.
- GIVEN `fork-release.yml` THEN its `apm-route` job needs the publish job, holds `contents: read` only, runs the smoke
  against `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>` and prints that spec; a dry run prints the spec it would test.

#### APM spec — "The intentional difference from the mirror" (MODIFIED, context)

Replaces `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:80-83`, "The cost is disk and clone time on the consumer's side; it is
accepted rather than worked around, because every workaround (a subdirectory package, a second repository, stripping
tracked files) reintroduces the second copy this decision removes. If APM gains a package-side include list, the
projection can adopt it in the generator." New text:

That cost holds for an install from the source tree (`<owner>/<repo>#v<version>`). Since `<date>` each release also ships
a slim package: the same projection, generated by the release with the same generator, placed under `apm/` in the
distribution tree and installed as `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>` (REQ-APM-010). It is a release artifact, not a
second maintained copy; a test holds it byte-identical to the source projection. The measurement of `<date>` recorded
what APM fetches for it and chose the folder form or the orphan-tag fallback. (Amended `<date>`, plan 016 file 2, unit
`u2-apm-slim-package`; it read as quoted above. Package 18 amends the figures at `:77-78` first.)

#### APM spec — "The client floor, and why it cannot be enforced package-side" (MODIFIED, context)

#### REQ-APM-017 — `check` names what APM placed in the files this setup shares, and fails when it breaks one (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-deliveries`. A doctor row, `apm-deliveries`, runs in every install mode. It lists, per
APM package, the hook entries APM merged into `.claude/settings.json`, `.cursor/hooks.json` and `.codex/hooks.json` —
attributed through the `apm-hooks.json` sidecar APM keeps beside each — the Copilot hook files the lock lists, and the
MCP servers the lock records per client. It fails when `.cursor/hooks.json` holds an event key Cursor does not list or an
entry with no `command`, because Cursor then rejects the whole file and loads none of its hooks, this setup's
`failClosed` guards included; and when APM placed an MCP server in `.codex/config.toml`, which this setup writes whole.
It warns when APM placed hooks or servers while `apm.yml` carries no executables gate, and when a sidecar records an
entry its hook file no longer holds. It reads only: nothing in the sidecars, the lock or `apm.yml` can make the CLI
write, refuse or delete.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-deliveries`.
- **Evidence (before):** measured 2026-10-06 with apm-cli 0.33.0 beside a 1.11.0 setup: `apm install` of a package with
  one hook file and one MCP server merged entries into the three files and `.codex/config.toml`, kept the markers in
  sidecars and left all of it out of `deployed_files`; `check` exited 1 with unattributed drift and `plugin-duplicates`
  passing; `sync -y` removed everything behind four `.bak` files and the next `apm install` restored it.
  microsoft/apm#3129 (open, read 2026-10-06): Cursor 3.13.10 rejects the whole `.cursor/hooks.json` for one unknown
  event key (unverified on Cursor 3.23.23, whose hooks page, read 2026-10-07, lists the same 21 events; note
  2026-10-07: apm PR #3149, merged 2026-10-06 and unreleased at v0.33.0, renders native camelCase Cursor hooks).
- **Expand/contract:** additive — one doctor row, no stored shape moves. Rollback: the prior release's `check`.
- **Proof:** `test/apm/executables.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`, `test/adapters/cursor.test.ts`; consumer
  A2 of the coexistence of REQ-APM-016 leg.

Criteria:
- GIVEN no `apm.yml`, no `apm.lock.yaml` and no `apm-hooks.json` sidecar WHEN `check` runs THEN `apm-deliveries` passes
  with "no APM install in this repository".
- GIVEN a package's hook entries in `.claude/settings.json` recorded in `.claude/apm-hooks.json` THEN the row names the
  package, the file and each event with its matcher, and no line carries a whole hook command.
- GIVEN cursor selected and `.cursor/hooks.json` holding the key `PreToolUse` THEN the row fails naming the file and the
  key, and `check` exits 1.
- GIVEN codex selected and the lock's `mcp_target_servers.codex` naming a server THEN the row fails naming
  `.codex/config.toml` and the server.
- GIVEN APM-placed hooks and no `executables` or `allowExecutables` mapping in `apm.yml` THEN the row warns naming
  `apm approve`; GIVEN either mapping THEN that warning is absent.
- GIVEN a sidecar entry with no matching entry in its hook file THEN the row warns naming both files.
- GIVEN any of these states WHEN `check` runs THEN no file changes.

#### REQ-APM-018 — Hooks, MCP servers and plugin declarations count in the duplicate check, and coverage is per package (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-backed-mode`. The duplicates row (REQ-APM-013) also finds a hook APM delivers that
runs this setup's own hook script or equals one of this setup's entries in the same file, and an MCP server APM and this
setup both configure for one client; both fail in every install mode. It also reads `.claude/settings.json` and
`.github/copilot/settings.json` for a declaration of this package's plugin (`enabledPlugins` `<plugin>@…` set true, or
an `extraKnownMarketplaces` entry naming the plugin or this repository): a declaration warns under `generated`, fails
under `apm-backed`, and is expected under `plugin-backed`. Under `apm-backed`, coverage counts only the stamity package's
deployed paths, so another APM package cannot stand in for a class stamity's lock is missing. The lock reader takes only
repository paths from `deployments[]` (`kind: project-relative`, `scope: project`). The row's "nothing can duplicate"
detail stays byte-identical only with no plugin record, no `apm.yml`, no lock, no APM sidecar and no plugin
declaration.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-backed-mode`.
- **Evidence (before):** r1 fx `c14` (2026-10-06): a generated setup whose `.claude/settings.json` declares
  `stamity@stamity` passes `check` with "no client records a plugin, so nothing can duplicate"
  (`src/cli/commands/check.ts:1120-1123`); `apmDuplicates` compares agents, skills and commands only
  (`src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:713-715`); inbox row 446 (coverage over every dependency); apm-cli 0.33.0's lock
  carries `uri` and `target-relative` rows beside file rows (`apm_cli/core/deployment_state.py:16-21`).
- **Expand/contract:** additive findings; a repository that declares the plugin in generated mode starts to warn (exit 0).
- **Proof:** `test/detect/apmLock.test.ts`, `test/apm/deliveries.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN an APM hook entry in `.claude/settings.json` that runs a script under `.stamity/generated/hooks/` THEN the
  duplicates row fails in every mode naming the event and the package.
- GIVEN `manifest.mcp.servers` selecting `github` and the lock's `mcp_target_servers.claude` naming `github` THEN the row
  fails naming `.mcp.json`.
- GIVEN mode `generated` and `.claude/settings.json` with `enabledPlugins["stamity@stamity"]: true` THEN the row warns
  naming the file and the key, and `check` exits 0; GIVEN `apm-backed` THEN it fails; GIVEN `plugin-backed` THEN no
  declaration finding.
- GIVEN mode `apm-backed`, tools `[claude, cursor]`, the stamity dependency deploying only `.claude/**` and another
  dependency deploying `.cursor/agents/acme-auditor.md` THEN the row fails "cursor: APM delivers no agent, skill,
  command, rule".
- GIVEN a lock with no `deployed_files` whose `deployments[]` holds `uri` and `target-relative` rows THEN none of their
  values is read as a repository path.

#### REQ-APM-019 — Under `apm-backed`, an override APM carries is reported as not reaching, and the APM package's version must match the engine's (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-deliveries`. Under `apm-backed`, an override or patch of a class APM delivers to a
client (agent, skill, command and rule for Claude Code, Copilot and Cursor; agent and skill for Codex, plus the rules
the APM package ships as skills) reaches no client there: `check`'s `apm-deliveries` row warns naming the override and
those clients, and `validate --json` lists it under `apmUnreached`; the fork layer stays APM's customization path. The
same row compares the stamity APM dependency's lock `version` with the engine version that wrote the repository's
files: a difference within one major warns, naming both moves (the `apm.yml` ref, or `upgrade --to`); a different
major fails.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-deliveries`.
- **Evidence (before):** `withoutPluginOwnedRows` drops every row of an owned class (`src/emit/ownership.ts:147-162`),
  overrides included, and no surface reports it per client (`src/cli/commands/validate.ts:132-182`); the APM bodies pin
  the package's own version (REQ-APM-011) while `upgrade --to` moves the npm pin (REQ-FLOW-034).
- **Expand/contract:** additive (a warning line and an optional JSON key, absent outside `apm-backed`).
- **Proof:** `test/apm/executables.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/validate.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`;
  consumer A of REQ-APM-016.

Criteria:
- GIVEN mode `apm-backed`, tools `[claude, codex]` and `.stamity/overrides/rules/testing.md` WHEN `validate --json` runs
  THEN `apmUnreached` names that path, `rule`, `testing` and `claude`; WHEN `check` runs THEN `apm-deliveries` warns with
  the same; GIVEN mode `generated` THEN neither does.
- GIVEN an override of a rule the APM package ships as a skill THEN codex is named too.
- GIVEN mode `apm-backed`, the stamity dependency's lock `version` `1.12.0` and `generatedBy` `1.12.0` THEN no skew line;
  GIVEN `1.13.0` THEN the row warns naming both versions; GIVEN `2.0.0` THEN it fails; GIVEN no `version` THEN it warns.

#### REQ-APM-020 — The distribution tree keeps its plugin archives under `archives/`, so an APM subfolder fetch brings only the package (ADDED)

From plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-slim-package` (inbox row 390, re-opened). Each release's distribution tree holds its
four plugin archives and their `.sha256` files under `archives/`; no archive or checksum file sits at the root, so the
sparse cone APM uses for `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>` brings only the root's `README.md`, `release.json` and
`apm.yml` beside `apm/`. `release.json` names each archive's tree path as `packages[].archivePath` (schema version 2);
`packages[].archive` stays the release-asset file name, and the release assets keep their names. A reader of an earlier
release's manifest, which has no `archivePath`, finds the archive at the root.

- **Units:** `u2-apm-slim-package`; the schema bump is `u2-release-integrity`'s.
- **Evidence (before):** at `plugins/v1.11.0` the root's 11 top-level files are 5,662,175 bytes, the four archives
  5,652,504 of them (`git ls-tree --long`, 2026-10-06); apm-cli 0.33.0 still fetches a subfolder through a `--cone`
  sparse checkout (`apm_cli/cache/git_cache.py:229-231`, `:266-280`), which always brings the root's top-level files.
- **Expand/contract:** a layout change at 1.12.0 for whatever fetches an archive from the tree by its root path; release
  assets are unchanged. Rollback: the next release builds without the folder.
- **Proof:** `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`, `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts`, `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`,
  `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN a distribution build THEN no top-level file of the tree ends in `.zip` or `.zip.sha256`, and `archives/` holds one
  archive and one `.sha256` per client.
- GIVEN `release.json` THEN each `packages[]` entry's `archivePath` is `archives/<archive>` and the file there hashes to
  its `sha256`.
- GIVEN `release.yml` and `fork-release.yml` THEN the digest checks read `plugins/<archivePath>`, the attestation and
  the asset upload name `plugins/archives/*`, and each uploaded asset's name equals its entry's `archive`.
- GIVEN a version-1 manifest with no `archivePath` THEN the promotion's archive check reads the root path and passes for
  an unchanged 1.11.0 tag.

### C. `docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md`

#### REQ-PLUGIN-034 The default branch carries a catalog per client that resolves to the full release root (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-main-catalogs`). A client that adds this repository without a ref reads the
default branch. The default branch carries one catalog per client that reads it — `.agents/plugins/marketplace.json`
(Codex), `.github/plugin/marketplace.json` (Copilot CLI and VS Code) and `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` (Claude Code,
and the fallback both others read) — and each entry points at the release's own root in the distribution, at the
`plugins/v<version>` tag, through the source kind that client supports. No default-branch entry has an npm source. The
catalogs are regenerated at each release and by `scripts/fork-identity.mjs`, and `--check` fails on drift.

- **Units:** `u2-main-catalogs`.
- **Evidence (before):** the default branch carries only `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`, npm-sourced
  (`.claude-plugin/marketplace.json:12-16`), and no Codex or Copilot catalog. Codex without `--ref` reads the default
  branch's Codex catalog, then its Claude catalog, and installs a root with no runtime and no hooks while both commands
  exit 0 (walked 2026-09-24, `docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:923-927`). The Copilot CLI searches `marketplace.json`, then
  `.plugin/`, then `.github/plugin/`, then `.claude-plugin/` (research return, 2026-10-01). The per-client source kinds
  are the plugin spec's (`docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:372-375`).
- **Proof:** a catalog test beside `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`; `node scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs --check`;
  file 3's route proofs.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the default branch at a release THEN the three catalogs exist, each entry's `version` equals the release version
  where the client documents one, and each entry resolves to that client's root at `plugins/v<version>`.
- GIVEN any of the three THEN no entry carries `"source": "npm"`.
- GIVEN a fork identity applied by `fork-identity.mjs` THEN every entry names the fork's repository, and none names the
  canonical one.
- GIVEN a catalog edited by hand WHEN the generator's `--check` runs THEN it exits 1 naming the file.
- GIVEN `codex plugin marketplace add <owner>/<repo>` with no `--ref` WHEN `codex plugin add stamity@stamity` runs THEN the
  installed root carries `runtime/` and its hook configuration (measured at execution on the maintainer's machine).
  Whether VS Code resolves the Copilot catalog's source kind is measured at execution as well.

   Copilot CLI searches before `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` names another root, and for VS Code the rule declared
   in `u2-main-catalogs` decides between this layout, a root-level `marketplace.json` and a stop for the maintainer." Its
   last criterion's sentence "Whether VS Code resolves the Copilot catalog's source kind is measured at execution as
   well." becomes "GIVEN the VS Code walk THEN its record names the catalog file VS Code read, the root it installed and
   one verdict, `COPILOT`, `ROOT-FILE` or `STOP`."

   the tag is at least that many days old (default 0)." becomes "every archive matches its release-asset `.sha256`,
   every client root at the tag hashes to its `rootSha256` (REQ-PLUGIN-054), and, when `soakDays` is set, the tag is at
   least that many days old (default 0); a tag whose `release.json` is schema 1 is refused." After "runs in an
   environment with required reviewers." add "Each channel branch's ruleset restricts creations, updates and deletions

   changed in the tag's tree THEN it exits 1 naming that client, and no ref moves." and "GIVEN `promote.yml` THEN

3. **REQ-PLUGIN-036** (file 2:577-615): after "a tag template pins the tag and carries no `autoUpdate`." add "The release
