   setup that selects neither Claude Code nor Copilot, saying Cursor has no committed declaration, and a repository that
   already enables the plugin from another marketplace." The criterion "GIVEN `--declare plugins/v<version>` THEN the
   declared `ref` is that tag." becomes "GIVEN `--declare tag` on an engine at <v> THEN the declared `ref` is
   `plugins/v<v>`; GIVEN a later `sync` by an engine at <w> THEN both files declare `plugins/v<w>` and a `sha` recorded
   for <v> is dropped with the warning `declaration-sha-stale`."; "GIVEN `--client cursor --declare stable` THEN the
   command exits 1 naming Claude Code and Copilot." gains "and saying Cursor has no committed declaration".
5. **REQ-PLUGIN-038** (file 2:648-674): "Installed pack skills are exempt from the plugin-owned filter on every client."
   becomes "Installed pack skills reach every client (`efc7b79d`); pack agents, commands, rules and hooks belong to the
   packs rework, and `check`'s `pack-reach` row reports them." The criterion "GIVEN an installed pack skill THEN every
   client, plugin-backed or not, gets the pack's copy." is kept as a regression of `efc7b79d`.
6. **REQ-PLUGIN-039** (file 2:676-704): "Claude Code (`Stop` beside `TaskCompleted`, which fires only through the task
   tools — off by default on models newer than Opus 4.7 since Claude Code 2.1.233, per its hooks and tools pages)"
   becomes "Claude Code (`Stop` beside `TaskCompleted`, which fires only through the TaskUpdate tool or when an
   agent-team teammate finishes, the task tools being off by default on models newer than Opus 4.7 since Claude Code
   2.1.233; read 2026-10-06, when the client was at 2.1.291)".
7. **REQ-PLUGIN-040** (file 2:706-736): "The Claude-layout scripts also skip a run another client started from
   `.claude/settings.json` — Cursor runs those hooks by default and the Copilot CLI reads them as a repository source —
   identified only by a payload or environment signature that appears in every captured payload of that client and in
   no captured Claude payload." becomes "The Claude-layout scripts also skip a run another client started from
   `.claude/settings.json` when that client is selected, because its own wiring runs the same hook: Cursor runs every
   matching hook from `.cursor/hooks.json` and from `.claude/settings.json` (read 2026-10-06), so a Claude-plus-Cursor
   repository fires each stamity hook twice there, and the Copilot CLI reads `.claude/settings.json` as a repository
   source. The other client is identified only by a payload or environment signature present in every captured payload
   of that client, imported runs included, and in no captured Claude payload; with none, the double run stays and the
   capability matrix says so."
8. **REQ-PLUGIN-041** (file 2:738-756): "because Bugbot reads neither plugins nor `.cursor/rules`." becomes "because
   Cursor's Bugbot page says project rules in `.cursor/rules/` do not apply to Bugbot runs and does not mention plugins
   (read 2026-10-06)."

#### REQ-PLUGIN-035 Release channels move only through a verified promotion (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-channels-promote`). `stamity.distribution.channels` names moving branches:
`stable` (default `plugin-stable`) and an optional `canary`. It is validated like `branch`. `plugin-dist` stays the
newest release. A `promote.yml` workflow, shared by the canonical repository and forks, takes a tag and a channel on
dispatch and runs in an environment with required reviewers. `scripts/promote-channel.mjs` verifies before it moves
anything: the tag's orphan commit message reads `plugins: v<version> from <sha>`, `release.json`'s version equals the
tag's, every archive matches its release-asset `.sha256`, and, when `soakDays` is set, the tag is at least that many days
old (default 0). Then it force-pushes the channel to the tag's commit. Rollback is promoting the previous tag.

- **Units:** `u2-channels-promote`.
- **Evidence (before):** no channel exists; `plugin-dist` is force-pushed to each release's orphan commit
  (`.github/workflows/release.yml:916-1003`, `.github/workflows/fork-release.yml:671-751`); `branch` is validated at
  `scripts/distribution-identity.mjs:25`, `:113-120`, `:229-250`; each catalog entry pins `ref: plugins/v<version>` with
  the release version (`scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs:158-165`, `:181`), and Claude detects updates by version (research
  return, 2026-10-01).
- **Expand/contract:** additive; `plugin-dist` and the tags are unchanged. Rollback is a promotion.
- **Proof:** `test/ci/promoteChannel.test.ts` over a fixture remote; `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`;
  `test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN a valid tag WHEN `promote-channel.mjs --tag plugins/v<X> --channel stable` runs THEN `plugin-stable` points at the
  tag's commit and nothing else moves.
- GIVEN a commit message, a `release.json` version or an archive digest that does not match THEN it exits 1 naming the
  check, and no ref moves.
- GIVEN `soakDays: 3` and a tag one day old THEN it exits 1 naming the remaining days; GIVEN three days THEN it promotes.
- GIVEN the channel at `plugins/v<B>` WHEN `plugins/v<A>` (older) is promoted THEN the channel points at A's commit.
- GIVEN a `channels` value that names `plugin-dist`, fails the branch-name rule or carries an unknown key THEN identity
  resolution exits 1 naming it.
- GIVEN `promote.yml` THEN its job names an environment and takes `tag` and `channel` inputs only.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-036 Admin templates per channel and per tag for Claude Code and Copilot (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-admin-templates`). The distribution builder renders managed-settings templates
for Claude Code and for Copilot under `admin/<client>/`: one per channel (`managed-settings.stable.json`, and
`managed-settings.canary.json` when a canary is configured) and one pinned to the release tag
(`managed-settings.tag.json`), each with a `.strict.json` twin. A channel template points the marketplace at the channel
and carries `autoUpdate: true` beside the source; a tag template pins the tag and carries no `autoUpdate`. Only the
`.strict` twin writes `strictKnownMarketplaces`, so the strict allowlist is the organization's choice of file, with no
build switch and no config key. The Copilot templates are the files an enterprise commits to its `.github-private`
repository as `copilot/managed-settings.json`: `extraKnownMarketplaces` with a `github` source (`repo`, `ref`),
`enabledPlugins`, per-entry `autoUpdate`, and `strictKnownMarketplaces` in the `.strict` twin; they write no key the
Copilot reference does not document. `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` stays for one compatibility window, byte-identical to
`admin/claude/managed-settings.tag.strict.json`, and goes at the next major release.

- **Units:** `u2-admin-templates`.
- **Evidence (before):** only `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` exists, pinned to the tag, with no `autoUpdate`, and
  `strictKnownMarketplaces` always written (`scripts/plugins/managed-settings.mjs:24-37`, `:100-112`); the builder writes
  it at `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:565-569`. Claude Code reads a managed `autoUpdate` beside the entry's
  `source` and locks the user's toggle (its plugins and org pages, read 2026-10-01). Copilot's enterprise managed
  settings are GA since 2026-07-01, legacy path `.github/copilot/settings.json` (research return, 2026-10-01).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`. A live install through an
  organization's Copilot settings needs a Copilot Business or Enterprise organization, which this project lacks; that
  leg is `Not done:` with its owner in file 3's route proofs.

Criteria:
- GIVEN a build for the canonical identity (no canary) THEN `admin/` holds exactly
  `claude/managed-settings.{tag,tag.strict,stable,stable.strict}.json`, the same four under `copilot/` and the legacy
  `claude-managed-settings.json` (equal to the Claude tag `.strict` file); with a canary configured, two more per client;
  a build of Cursor alone writes no `admin/`.
- GIVEN the Claude stable template THEN its keys are `extraKnownMarketplaces`, `enabledPlugins` and
  `requiredMinimumVersion` in that order, the marketplace entry is `{source: {source: "github", repo, ref:
  "plugin-stable"}, autoUpdate: true}`, `requiredMinimumVersion` is at or above 2.1.277, and there is no
  `strictKnownMarketplaces`.
- GIVEN any `.strict` twin THEN its last key is `strictKnownMarketplaces`, holding exactly one entry that equals the
  declared source field for field.
- GIVEN a tag template THEN its `ref` is `plugins/v<version>` and its entry carries no `autoUpdate`.
- GIVEN the Copilot stable template THEN its keys are exactly `extraKnownMarketplaces` and `enabledPlugins`, and its key
  set matches the vendor page the unit cites with its access date.
- GIVEN two builds of one commit THEN every template is byte-identical and the leak gate over `admin/` reports 0 hits.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-037 `plugin setup --declare <channel|tag>` writes the project's plugin declaration as ledger-owned keys (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-declare`). `plugin setup --declare <channel|tag>` writes
`extraKnownMarketplaces` and `enabledPlugins` into `.claude/settings.json` when Claude Code is set up (the Copilot CLI
reads it too) and into `.github/copilot/settings.json` when Copilot is set up (the Copilot CLI, the cloud agent and VS
Code's recommendations), with identical values in both. Each declared key is owned at its nested path — the JSON
Pointers `/extraKnownMarketplaces/<marketplace>` and `/enabledPlugins/<plugin>@<marketplace>` — through file 0's
per-entry ownership, so sibling entries a client or another plugin wrote stay foreign, and a value the client had already
written before setup stays foreign too. The command refuses a repository whose `extraKnownMarketplaces` already declares
the marketplace, and a setup that selects neither Claude Code nor Copilot. `clean` removes exactly the declared keys.

- **Units:** `u2-declare`, which depends on `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-00.md (u0-settings-ownership)`.
- **Evidence:** `claude plugin install … --scope project` writes `enabledPlugins` alone (measured on 2.1.280,
  `docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:126-133`); Claude Code does not install a plugin whose catalog entry is an external
  source from project settings, so each contributor runs `claude plugin install <plugin>@<marketplace> --scope project`
  once (its plugins pages, read 2026-10-01).
- **Proof:** `test/cli/commands/pluginSetup.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/plugin.test.ts`,
  `test/merge/settingsKeyOwnership.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN `plugin setup --client claude,copilot --declare stable` THEN both files carry `extraKnownMarketplaces` naming the
  channel branch and `enabledPlugins` naming the plugin, and the ledger records the two pointers for each file.
- GIVEN `--declare plugins/v<version>` THEN the declared `ref` is that tag.
- GIVEN either file already holding `extraKnownMarketplaces.<marketplace>` THEN the command exits 1 naming the file and
  writes nothing.
- GIVEN `.claude/settings.json` holding only `enabledPlugins["<plugin>@<marketplace>"]: true` written by the client THEN
  setup succeeds, that key is not recorded, and a later `clean -y` leaves it in place.
- GIVEN a declared setup and a foreign key added later WHEN `clean -y` runs THEN only foreign content remains, a file the
  engine created with nothing foreign left is gone, and no `.bak` exists.
- GIVEN `--client cursor --declare stable` THEN the command exits 1 naming Claude Code and Copilot.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-038 Overrides, patches, packs and model pins reach plugin-mode clients (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-plugin-overrides`). Under a plugin-backed setup, an override or patch of a
plugin-owned artifact, and an agent with a `stamity config` model pin, are emitted as project-level copies. Each client
has a defined precedence between the project copy and the plugin's: Claude namespaces plugin artifacts
(`/stamity:<id>`), the Copilot CLI lets project skills shadow plugin skills, VS Code prefixes plugin skills
`/<plugin>:`. Installed pack skills are exempt from the plugin-owned filter on every client. `check` and `plugin status`
report, per client, what reaches it.

- **Units:** `u2-plugin-overrides`.
- **Evidence (before):** `ownedClassOfRow` reads only `artifactType` (`src/emit/ownership.ts:123-129`) and
  `withoutPluginOwnedRows` drops every owned class (`:147-161`); only Claude passes `packSkillPaths`
  (`src/adapters/claude.ts:557-622`; not codex `:496`, copilot `:327`, cursor `:514`); the plugin generator's synthetic
  manifest has no `models` (`scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs:490-501`) while pins are read from `ctx.manifest.models`
  (`src/adapters/claude.ts:692-693`); neither `check` nor `validate` reports it.
- **Proof:** adapter tests per client, `test/emit/ownership.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`; the precedence per
  client measured at execution and recorded with client versions.

Criteria:
- GIVEN a plugin-backed setup and a replacement override of a plugin-owned skill WHEN `sync` runs THEN each client gets a
  project-level copy at its skill path carrying the override's bytes.
- GIVEN a model pin for `stamity-reviewer` THEN each plugin-backed client that carries agents gets a project-level agent
  carrying the pinned model.
- GIVEN an installed pack skill THEN every client, plugin-backed or not, gets the pack's copy.
- GIVEN `check` and `plugin status --json` THEN each lists, per client, every override, patch and pinned agent with
  `reaches: true` or a reason, and the `plugin-duplicates` row does not flag these copies.
- GIVEN each client's measured precedence THEN the docs state which copy wins, with the client version measured.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-039 The review gate enforces on every client that has a blocking completion event (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-review-gate-all`). The review gate holds a run's completion on each client
whose completion event can hold it and whose round event carries the verdict it needs. The unit measures each client's
events and payloads first, Claude Code included: Claude Code (`Stop` beside `TaskCompleted`, which fires only through
the task tools — off by default on models newer than Opus 4.7 since Claude Code 2.1.233, per its hooks and tools pages),
the Copilot CLI and VS Code (`Stop`, `SubagentStop`), Cursor (`stop`, `subagentStop`), Codex (`Stop`). A client is wired
only when its completion event fired headless, a hold continued the agent, its round event carries the finishing agent
and its final text, and the run key matches between the two. A hold uses each client's own continue form (exit 2,
a JSON `decision: block`, or Cursor's `followup_message`), and a run is held at most three consecutive times without a
new recorded round, then released with the reason code `CONTINUATION_CAP`. Where no blocking event exists, the prose
ladder stays, and `check` and the capability matrix say so for that client.

- **Units:** `u2-review-gate-all` (after `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`, which captures the payloads).
- **Evidence (before):** the gate holds only Claude's `TaskCompleted`, with rounds counted on `SubagentStop`
  (`src/hooks/model.ts:161-189`; `REVIEW_GATE_COMPLETION_EVENTS`, `src/hooks/scripts.ts:1984`); Copilot's
  `subagentStop` is named and not wired.
- **Proof:** hook tests per adapter over captured, scrubbed payload fixtures; the measurement record per client, dated,
  with client versions.

Criteria:
- GIVEN the measurement record THEN each of the four clients has a row naming its completion and round events, whether a
  hold continued the agent, and the payload fields the gate reads, or the reason it stays on the prose ladder.
- GIVEN a wired client's captured round payload with `verdict: request-changes` and then its completion payload for the
  same run THEN the gate holds in that client's form; after a round with `verdict: approve` THEN the completion passes.
- GIVEN three consecutive held completions with no new round THEN the fourth passes with `CONTINUATION_CAP` on stderr.
- GIVEN a completion for a run with no recorded round THEN the gate passes and the state file is unchanged.
- GIVEN `check` on a repository selecting all four clients THEN a `review-gate` row names each client once, "enforced on
  <events>" or "prose ladder — <reason>", and the capability matrix shows the same.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-040 Plugin hooks filter themselves, and the Copilot root loads in VS Code (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`). Every hook decides from its own payload whether to
act, so it is correct on a client that ignores matchers: the portable runner applies a row's matcher to the payload's
tool name itself and skips a payload that names another event. The Claude-layout scripts also skip a run another client
started from `.claude/settings.json` — Cursor runs those hooks by default and the Copilot CLI reads them as a repository
source — identified only by a payload or environment signature that appears in every captured payload of that client
and in no captured Claude payload. The Copilot root loads in VS Code with `chat.plugins.enabled`, its hooks run and
filter there, and its README says so.

- **Units:** `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`.
- **Evidence (before):** VS Code's Local hooks ignore matcher values (its hooks page, read 2026-10-01);
  `src/adapters/copilot.ts:925-934` emits PascalCase events with matchers, and the runner reads no matcher
  (`src/hooks/portableRunner.ts:138-209`); `SessionEnd` is not a VS Code event; Cursor's third-party hooks setting is on
  by default (its hooks reference, read 2026-10-01); the root README says "VS Code is not on it (read 2026-09-20)"
  (`scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:267-269`).
- **Proof:** runner and script tests over captured payload fixtures; one VS Code load walk on the maintainer's machine
  (VS Code is not installed there today: the `code` command opens Cursor, so the walk installs VS Code first).

Criteria:
- GIVEN the copilot runner and a row with matcher `Edit|Write` WHEN it receives a `bash` tool payload THEN it exits 0,
  writes nothing and never starts the hook; WHEN it receives an `edit` payload THEN the hook runs once.
- GIVEN a row for one event and a payload that names another THEN the runner exits 0 and writes nothing.
- GIVEN each captured Cursor payload and each captured Copilot import payload WHEN the four Claude-layout scripts run on it
  THEN each exits 0 with nothing on stdout and a stderr line naming the client; GIVEN each captured Claude payload THEN
  none skips, and the guard and the gate behave as before.
- GIVEN the Copilot root's hook configuration THEN every event key is in VS Code's documented list.
- `judgment: maintainer` · GIVEN VS Code with the Copilot root installed WHEN a session starts and a tool runs THEN the
  record names the VS Code version, the session target, the listed skills, the session-start hook line and the filtered
  matcher hook; a walk not performed is `Not done:` with its owner.
- GIVEN the Copilot root README THEN it no longer says VS Code is not supported.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-041 Cursor's rules are repository-owned in plugin mode, and Bugbot gets `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot`). In plugin mode, Cursor's rules are written to the
repository as `.cursor/rules/*.mdc` and leave the Cursor plugin root, so the IDE gets them once and the Cursor CLI reads
them. `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` is generated from the review rules in both modes, because Bugbot reads neither plugins nor
`.cursor/rules`.

- **Units:** `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot`.
- **Evidence (before):** Cursor's 12 rules live only inside the plugin (`scripts/plugins/clients/cursor.mjs:57-59`); the
  Cursor CLI reads `.cursor/rules`, `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md`, and plugin rules in the CLI are undocumented; Bugbot
  reads `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` (research return, 2026-10-01).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/pluginPackages.cursor.test.ts`, `test/adapters/cursor.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/plugin.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN `plugin setup --client cursor` THEN `.cursor/rules/` holds one `.mdc` file per Cursor rule, and the Cursor root's
  capability file declares `rule` as `repository-owned` with a reason.
- GIVEN a Cursor plugin build THEN its root carries no rule file.
- GIVEN generated or plugin mode with Cursor selected THEN `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` exists, derives from the review rules, and
  stays under the size limit the vendor page states, read at execution; the generator exits 1 above it.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-042 Plugin skills reach the bundled runtime before any network fallback (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`). In a plugin root, every body that runs the CLI calls
the bundled runtime first, as `node "<root>/runtime/locate.mjs" -- <verb>`, where `<root>` is the plugin root — the
nearest folder above the body that holds `stamity-plugin.json` (a client that sets its root variable in the shell names
the same folder; the Copilot CLI and Codex set none for a skill's shell). Only when the locator cannot run does the body
fall to `npx --no stamity <verb>`, then to the pinned `npx -y <package>@<version>` call, and then to the `Not done:` line
the everyday-flows spec requires when a CLI cannot run. The CLI-route bodies are unchanged.

- **Units:** `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`.
- **Evidence (before):** `st-learn`, `st-handoff`, `st-debug`, `st-work` and `st-quick` run `npx --no stamity …`, then
  `npx -y @zomarit/stamity@1.11.0 …`, else a `Not done: … CLI unavailable` note (`content/skills/st-learn/SKILL.md:50`
  and the sentence pinned at `test/corpus/cliCallForm.test.ts:32-33`); the four plugin roots reference the bundled
  locator nowhere; the locator runs the repository's own install when its version fits, else the bundled copy, and
  never the network (`scripts/plugins/locate.mjs:12-32`).
- **Proof:** `test/ci/pluginModules.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginLocate.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginPackages.*.test.ts`,
  `test/corpus/cliCallForm.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN each built plugin root THEN every body that runs the CLI names `runtime/locate.mjs` before any `npx` form.
- GIVEN a stub runtime, a `PATH` holding only Node and an unreachable registry WHEN the locator runs `learn capture`
  THEN the stub receives exactly that verb and the locator exits with the stub's status, fetching nothing.
- GIVEN the CLI-route bodies under `.claude/` THEN they are byte-identical to before, and `node dist/cli.js check`
  reports no drift.

   `apm.lock.yaml` records, so a mirror published under another owner (`acme/stamity-mirror/apm#plugins/v1.12.0`) is
   found. An unparseable `apm.yml` or `apm.lock.yaml` is a failing line of this row naming the file. `release.json` carries
   `apm.installSpec` as `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>` (REQ-APM-010)."

(Amended `<date>`, plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-apm-backed-mode`; it read as quoted.)

#### REQ-PLUGIN-028 One command sets a fork's identity (MODIFIED)

Replaces the Renovate-presets bullet as file 1 amended it (the one naming `--suffix`), and the bullet at `:1321`, "- regenerates the plugin and APM manifests." New
text:

#### REQ-PLUGIN-029 A Claude Code managed-settings template for an organization's rollout (MODIFIED)

Replaces `:1365-1371`, from "The distribution builder renders `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` from the fork's
identity:" through the four-key list ending "`requiredMinimumVersion` at least 2.1.277, the first client where an invalid
allowlist fails closed." New text:

The distribution builder renders Claude Code managed-settings templates from the fork's identity under `admin/claude/`,
one per channel and one pinned to the release tag, each with a `.strict` twin (REQ-PLUGIN-036):

- the company marketplace in `extraKnownMarketplaces`, at the channel with `autoUpdate: true`, or at the tag;
- the plugin on for everyone in `enabledPlugins`;
- `requiredMinimumVersion` at least 2.1.277, the first client where an invalid allowlist fails closed;
- in the `.strict` twin only, that marketplace alone admitted by `strictKnownMarketplaces`, as the last key.

The criterion at `:1377-1380` read "GIVEN any identity WHEN the template renders THEN it holds exactly the four keys in a
fixed order, …". New text: "GIVEN any identity WHEN a template renders THEN it holds `extraKnownMarketplaces`,
`enabledPlugins` and `requiredMinimumVersion` in that order, and its `.strict` twin adds `strictKnownMarketplaces` last;
the marketplace key and plugin id equal the rendered Claude catalog's, and an allowlist entry equals the declared
marketplace source field for field. An unequal pair blocks every marketplace for every user." (Amended `<date>`, plan 016
file 2, unit `u2-admin-templates`; it read as quoted.)

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief) — REQ-PLUGIN-029 A Claude Code managed-settings template for an organization's rollout (MODIFIED, plan text):**
In file 2's MODIFIED entry (file 2:861-880), replaces "- `requiredMinimumVersion` at least 2.1.277, the first client
where an invalid allowlist fails closed;" with "- `requiredMinimumVersion` at the highest documented floor of the keys
the template writes: 2.1.277 wherever `strictKnownMarketplaces` is written (the first client where an invalid allowlist
fails closed), and in a plain template the floor recorded for `autoUpdate` or `sha` with its source, or 2.1.277 where
that floor is unstated; the README names each template's floor and its reason;".

#### REQ-PLUGIN-004 Charter-reference substitution of tokens in plugin bodies (MODIFIED)

Replaces, in the paragraph "Amended 2026-09-30" (`docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:178-185`), the sentence "The tenth,
`${STAMITY:CLI}`, maps to a literal rather than a phrase: the pinned call `npx -y <package>@<plugin version>`, or
`npx --no <package>@<plugin version>` for a package with no npm channel, from inputs the plugin build passes in, so the
value is fixed at build time and never read off the consuming repository; a build that passes none leaves the token
unresolved and the body is refused". New text:

The tenth, `${STAMITY:CLI}`, maps to a literal rather than a phrase. In a plugin root it is the locator call
`node "<root>/runtime/locate.mjs" --`, and the running-CLI sentence of the four call sites is rewritten to name the
locator first, then `npx --no stamity`, then the pinned call `npx -y <package>@<plugin version>` (or
`npx --no <package>@<plugin version>` for a package with no npm channel) as the last fallback (REQ-PLUGIN-042). A caller
that passes no runtime — the APM generator (REQ-APM-011) — keeps the pinned call. The value is fixed at build time and
never read off the consuming repository; a build that passes no inputs leaves the token unresolved and the body is
refused. (Amended `<date>`, plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`; it read as quoted.)

#### REQ-PLUGIN-054 A release names one integrity for its npm tarball and asset, a digest per client root, and the paths its engine owns (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-release-integrity`). `release.json` moves to `schemaVersion: 2`. Beside
`runtime.tarballSha256` it records `runtime.tarballIntegrity`, the sha512 integrity npm reports as `dist.integrity` for
the same tarball; the release asset is that tarball byte for byte, and the README and the fork guide say so. Each
`packages[]` entry records `rootSha256`, the sha256-tree-v1 digest of the client root a marketplace installs: the sha256
of the sorted lines `<sha256 hex>  <path>` over every regular file under the root, paths relative to it. `ownedPaths`
records the exact paths and the roots the release's engine may write or remove in a repository (file 0's bound). The
validator accepts schema 1, without these keys, and schema 2, with all of them. Both release workflows check, before
publishing, that the integrity and the root digests match the artifacts, and after publishing, that the registry serves
the recorded integrity; a mismatch stops the run before the distribution push and the GitHub release.

- **Units:** `u2-release-integrity`.
- **Evidence (before):** schema 1 (`scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs:17`, `:31-32`); the fork computes the sha512
  and discards it (`.github/workflows/fork-release.yml:656`); no step reads `dist.integrity` after publishing
  (`.github/workflows/release.yml:898-899`); the roots on `plugin-dist` carry no digest (r4 SEC-2).
- **Expand/contract:** a consumer validating with a 1.11.0 copy of the validator refuses schema 2; the CHANGELOG names
  the bump. Rollback: a release from the prior builder.
- **Proof:** `test/ci/rootDigest.test.ts`, `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginRuntime.test.ts`,
  `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`, `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`, `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN a distribution build THEN `release.json` validates as schema 2, each `packages[].rootSha256` equals the digest
  of `<out>/<client>/`, and `runtime.tarballIntegrity` is `sha512-` plus the base64 sha512 of the runtime's tarball.
- GIVEN the schema-1 manifest of `plugins/v1.11.0` THEN the validator returns no defect; GIVEN it with `ownedPaths`
  added THEN exactly one message names `ownedPaths`.
- GIVEN one byte of one root file changed after the build THEN the publish job's verification exits 1 naming that
  client.
- GIVEN `release.yml` and `fork-release.yml` THEN a step after the publish compares the registry's `dist.integrity`
  with `runtime.tarballIntegrity`, and its `run` is byte-equal in both.
- GIVEN a distribution build's README THEN it states the byte-identity rule and the command that recomputes a root
  digest.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-055 `validate` says, per client and install mode, whether each customization reaches (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-plugin-overrides`). Every replaced or patched row `validate` reports carries
`reach`, one entry per selected client with `reaches` and, when false, the reason; `emits` is true when any client is
reached. Generated mode reaches every client. Under a plugin, an override of a plugin-owned class reaches through the
project copy REQ-PLUGIN-038 writes, and reads "not written yet; run sync" until the ledger records it; a fork-layer
patch reaches through the fork's plugin root. Under `apm-backed`, an override of a class APM delivers to that client
does not reach it.

- **Units:** `u2-plugin-overrides`.
- **Evidence (before):** `emits` is read off `OVERRIDE_EMITTING_CLASSES` with no install mode (`src/cli/commands/validate.ts:620`,
  `:825`); measured 2026-10-06 (r3 C8.1): `emits: true` for an override `sync` then dropped, with no warning anywhere.
- **Proof:** the `validate` command suite (re-read at intake), `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN generated mode and a user override THEN the row has `emits: true` and `reaches: true` for every selected client.
- GIVEN a plugin-backed Claude setup carrying `agent` and a user override of one, before `sync` THEN `reaches: false`
  with "not written yet; run sync"; after `sync` THEN `reaches: true` naming Claude's precedence rule.
- GIVEN `apm-backed` with a lock deploying the package and an override of an agent THEN `reaches: false` with the APM
  reason, and the human output prints it.

#### REQ-PLUGIN-056 A plugin-backed Cursor setup says that Cursor cloud agents run none of its hooks (ADDED)

Added `<date>` (plan 016 file 2, unit `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot`). Cursor cloud agents load no team-marketplace plugin
(Cursor forum 170280, staff, 2026-09-02) and skip `sessionStart`, `sessionEnd` and the MCP hooks even from a committed
`.cursor/hooks.json` (cursor.com/docs/hooks); both read 2026-10-06. A plugin-backed Cursor repository therefore runs no
stamity hook in a cloud agent, the subagent and MCP guards included, and a generated one runs its committed hooks there
except the session-start index and the MCP guard. The capability matrix has a `## Plugin route gaps` row saying so, and
`check`'s `plugin-runtime` row adds one line for a recorded plugin-backed Cursor client without changing its status.

- **Units:** `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot`.
- **Evidence (before):** Cursor `plugin setup` writes no `.cursor/hooks.json` (measured 2026-10-06, r5); session
  context comes only from the session-start script (`src/hooks/scripts.ts:100-101`, `:683`), which Cursor runs on
  `sessionStart` (`src/adapters/cursor.ts:156`; r5 P11.2).
- **Proof:** `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`, `test/emit/capabilityMatrix.test.ts`.

Criteria:
- GIVEN a manifest recording a plugin-backed Cursor client WHEN `check --json` runs THEN the `plugin-runtime` detail
  holds the cloud-agent line, and the row's status equals the status without it.
- GIVEN generated mode with Cursor THEN no such line.
- GIVEN the capability matrix THEN `## Plugin route gaps` has the Cursor cloud-agent row with both sources and their
  access dates.

#### REQ-UPSTREAM-017 — Private bootstrap and operational recovery (MODIFIED)

Replaces `docs/specs/enterprise-upstream-lane.md:551-553`, "A private package has no npm channel, so the regenerated
marketplace entry carries a `github` source naming the repository instead of an npm package the fork never publishes
(`scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs`, its `marketplaceSource` binding)." New text:

The regenerated catalogs on the default branch point at the fork's own distribution tag (REQ-PLUGIN-034), so a private
package, which has no npm channel, gets entries naming its own repository and the release's `plugins/v<version>` tag —
never an npm package the fork never publishes, and never the source checkout's paths. (Amended `<date>`, plan 016 file 2,
unit `u2-main-catalogs`; it read as quoted.)

- **Evidence:** the checked-in entry's private-fork `github` source addresses the source checkout's `content/…` paths,
  which no `plugins/v*` tag satisfies (`docs/specs/plugin-lifecycle.md:377-382`, ledger row build/18).

## Units

Measurements marked **(probe 2026-10-01)** were run by this plan's drafters with apm-cli 0.32.0 and 0.29.1 in scratch
Python virtual environments and with the clients installed on the maintainer's machine; nothing in the repository was
written. Each unit that rests on a measurement repeats it at execution and records it, dated, in the run record (after
Package 18, through `scripts/records.mjs`). No unit edits `content/**`, so no eval case `source:` range moves;
`u2-repo-file-waste`, `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode` and `u2-review-gate-all` change emission and re-sync the dogfood.

### u2-apm-sparse-measure — measure what an APM subfolder install fetches, and choose the folder or the orphan tag

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-apm-sparse-measure |
| `requirements` | REQ-APM-010 |
| `files` | none in the repository. The executing run's own record (`.stamity/runs/<run>/apm-fetch-measure.md`, published through plan 014 file 2's records flow, `scripts/records.mjs`); scratch venvs, consumers and caches live in the session scratchpad only |
| `interfaces` | **Why.** The distribution tree carries `apm.yml` + `.apm/` at its root today (`scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:9-15`, `:513`), and an install of `<slug>#plugins/v<ver>` vendors the whole tree (inbox row at `.stamity/inbox.md:347`: 2,882 files, 21.5 MiB at `plugins/v1.11.0`); the source tree costs about 20 MiB (`.stamity/inbox.md:348`; spec `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:74-83`). **How apm-cli fetches a subfolder (source read 2026-10-01).** 0.32.0: a dependency `owner/repo/<path>#<ref>` is a virtual subdirectory package (`apm_cli/deps/github_downloader.py:1373` `download_subdirectory_package`). With the default persistent cache it uses a partial bare (`blob:none`) plus a sparse cone (`apm_cli/cache/git_cache.py:268-280`); with `APM_NO_CACHE=1` it uses a shared depth-1 bare clone (`apm_cli/deps/bare_cache.py:122-168`, `--depth=1 --branch <ref>`) materialized sparse; either way it vendors only the subfolder to `apm_modules/<owner>/<repo>/<path>/` (`apm_cli/models/dependency/materialization.py:158-170`). `--verbose` prints `[i] perf: materialize sparse=<yes\|no> size=<MB>`. Cache root: `APM_CACHE_DIR`, `APM_NO_CACHE=1` (`apm_cli/cache/paths.py:5-15`). 0.29.1 carries the same three paths (`deps/github_downloader.py:1363-1591`). **Preview (probe 2026-10-01, 0.32.0, `--target claude --verbose`, fresh `APM_CACHE_DIR` each run):** full distribution root `zomarit/stamity#plugins/v1.11.0` — cache 65,240 KB, `apm_modules` 29,800 KB / 2,883 files, exit 0; one-file subfolder `zomarit/stamity/admin#plugins/v1.11.0` — cache 9,480 KB, `materialize sparse=yes size=7.31 MB`, exit 1 (not a package; bytes still count); plugin-root subfolder `zomarit/stamity/claude#plugins/v1.11.0` — cache 16,572 KB, `apm_modules` 6,612 KB / 760 files, `sparse=yes size=12.39 MB`, exit 0; the same with `APM_NO_CACHE=1` — shared bare `--depth=1 --branch plugins/v1.11.0`, peak temp 17,812 KB. Finding: cone mode always materializes the root's top-level files, so the four `stamity-plugin-<client>-<ver>.zip` archives at the distribution root (5,652,504 bytes at `plugins/v1.11.0`; apm's `size=7.31 MB` is the whole sparse checkout) ride along with any subfolder; the vendored copy holds the subfolder only. **Procedure.** (1) Clients: the current client (the newest apm-cli on PyPI at intake, 0.33.0 on 2026-10-06) and 0.29.1, each `python3 -m venv <scratch>/apm-<v> && <scratch>/apm-<v>/bin/pip install --no-cache-dir apm-cli==<v>` (Python >= 3.10; CI pins 3.13, `ci.yml:409-413`; never inside the repository, `ci.yml:414-421`). (2) A `git` shim first on `PATH` that appends `git <args>` and the exit status to `$GIT_MEASURE_LOG` and runs the real git. (3) Arms, each in a fresh `git init` consumer whose `apm.yml` names one dependency, `apm install --target claude,copilot,cursor,codex --verbose`, a fresh `APM_CACHE_DIR`, then again with `APM_NO_CACHE=1` and a fresh `TMPDIR` sampled for peak size every 0.2 s: **T** `zomarit/stamity#v1.11.0`; **F** `zomarit/stamity#plugins/v1.11.0`; **S1** `zomarit/stamity/admin#plugins/v1.11.0` (the floor cost of any subfolder of today's distribution tree); **S2** `zomarit/stamity/claude#plugins/v1.11.0`; **L** a local fixture: `node scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs --out <scratch>/dist --runtime <scratch>/runtime` (runtime from `scripts/build-plugin-runtime.mjs`), then `apm.yml` and `.apm/` moved into `<scratch>/dist/apm/`, `git init` and one commit, consumer dependency `<scratch>/dist/apm` (declarative local route, the one CI uses, `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:42-51`). **D** (dual copy, local): L's tree with the package BOTH at its root and under `apm/`, `git init` and one commit, consumer dependency on the tree's root; its deployed file count must equal a root-only fixture's (the 1.11.0 layout) — every primitive once — for the root copy to stay as the compatibility alias of `u2-apm-slim-package` (verdict line `APM root copy: KEEP`, else `APM root copy: STOP`). **R** (optional, only on the maintainer's approval because it is outward-facing): a throwaway public repository `<maintainer>/stamity-apm-probe` holding L's tree at tag `plugins/v0.0.1`, measuring `…/apm#plugins/v0.0.1` end to end, plus a root `apm.yml` that declares only `dependencies: { apm: [<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v0.0.1] }` (the candidate redirect for consumers of the old `#plugins/v*` spec); deleted after. (4) Metrics per run: exit code; the `perf:` lines; `du -sk $APM_CACHE_DIR`; `du -sk` and file count of `apm_modules/`; deployed file count (lock `deployed_files` entries that are files); wall time; the shim's git lines (`--filter=blob:none`, `--depth=1`, `sparse-checkout set <path>`). (5) **Decision rule, declared before the run:** FOLDER (the package under `apm/` of the distribution tree, spec `<slug>/apm#plugins/v<ver>`) iff (a) with the current client in the default cache mode, S1's cache bytes are at most 25% of F's; (b) L deploys every class for every target on both clients; (c) 0.29.1 installs S2 through the subdirectory route (a sparse or a full clone is acceptable at the floor). Otherwise FALLBACK: an orphan tag `apm/v<ver>` whose tree holds only `apm.yml` and `.apm/`. With the preview numbers (9,480 / 65,240 = 14.5%) the rule picks FOLDER. (6) Record: one table (client, arm, cache mode, every metric), the verdict lines `APM slim route: FOLDER` or `APM slim route: FALLBACK` and `APM root copy: KEEP` or `APM root copy: STOP`, and the cone-mode residue; the same table enters the plan's spec delta for REQ-APM-010 beside the 2026-09-09 probe table (`docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:48-58`). **Follows:** `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:48-58` (a dated probe table with client, route and result) |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** the procedure has run, **then** the record holds at least 14 measured rows (T, F, S1, S2 for the current client and 0.29.1 in the default cache mode, plus S1, S2 and F for both clients with `APM_NO_CACHE=1`), each carrying exit code, cache KB, `apm_modules` KB and file count, deployed file count and the `perf:` line or "no perf line". **Given** arm L's rows, **then** each lists per target the count of agents, prompts, instructions and skills deployed, every count above 0, on both clients. **Given** arm D's rows on both clients, **then** each deployed count equals the root-only fixture's and the record carries `APM root copy: KEEP`; a larger count carries `APM root copy: STOP`, and `u2-apm-slim-package` does not start until the maintainer chooses. **Given** the record's table, **when** rule (a) is recomputed with `node -e` from the two cache figures it names, **then** the result matches the verdict line. |
| `edgeCases` | A GitHub rate limit or a network failure → the run is repeated later; no row is ever estimated. 0.29.1 fails S2 → FALLBACK, unless (c) holds through another documented route the record names. S1 exits 1 because `admin/` is no package → expected; its bytes still count. A cache hit from an earlier run → impossible by construction, each run gets a fresh `APM_CACHE_DIR`. The shim never sees apm's git (apm resolves git elsewhere) → the `perf:` lines carry the verdict and the record says the shim saw nothing. Arm R not approved → the redirect stays unmeasured and is not built (a follow-up). |
| `depends_on` | none |
| `verify` | the record contains the table, the verdict line and the cone-mode note; `node -e` recomputation of rule (a) from the recorded S1 and F cache figures equals the verdict |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **interfaces, added:** The current client is the newest apm-cli on PyPI at intake — 0.33.0, published 2026-10-02
  (GitHub release 18:37Z, PyPI 22:38Z) — recorded with its upload date; it also shipped Codex's native hook shape
  (microsoft/apm PR #3060). Re-read 2026-10-06: 0.33.0 still fetches a subfolder through a partial bare (`blob:none`)
  and a `--cone` sparse checkout (`apm_cli/cache/git_cache.py:229-231`, `:266-280`, `:629-630`; `:240-300` identical
  to 0.32.0). Root sizes (M6): at `plugins/v1.11.0` the four archives are 5,652,504 bytes of 5,662,175 bytes of
  top-level files; apm's `materialize sparse=yes size=7.31 MB` counts the whole sparse checkout, not the archives. Since
  inbox row 390 is re-opened into `u2-apm-slim-package` (REQ-APM-020), the record adds the post-move cone as a computed
  row — `README.md`, `apm.yml` and `release.json`, 9,275 bytes at 1.11.0's sizes — and the decision rule stays as
  declared, measured on today's tags.
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** the record, **when** it is read, **then** it names the current client's version
  and PyPI upload date, and a row "root top-level files" with the byte counts of a tag's top-level files and of its
  archives taken from `git ls-tree --long plugins/v<ver>` (the run record; `node -e` recomputes the percentage from
  them).
- **edgeCases, added:** A newer apm-cli than 0.33.0 on PyPI at intake → it is the current client, and the record and
  `u2-apm-floor-current` name it. 0.33.0 ever changing the cone (no root files) → arm S1's bytes fall and the rule still
  reads FOLDER; the record says so.

### u2-repo-file-waste — write Codex skill metadata only where Codex reads it, measure the other duplicates per reader, and document what stays

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-repo-file-waste |
| `requirements` | REQ-FLOW-033, REQ-FLOW-026 |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **interfaces, added:** **Cursor's Third-Party Imports join the census.** Cursor's import of Claude Code's files is on by default and runs "all matching hooks from every source", `.claude/settings.json` included (https://cursor.com/docs/reference/third-party-hooks, read 2026-10-06), loads `.claude/skills/` (https://cursor.com/docs/skills), and in the CLI loads `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/agents`, `.claude/skills` and `.claude/commands` with no key, flag or variable to turn it off (Cursor forum 166558, staff, read 2026-10-06; a secondary source, so `reads: "unverified"` unless a run measures it). New census locations: `.claude/agents/`, `.claude/commands/`, `CLAUDE.md`, `hooks in .claude/settings.json`, each with rows for the surfaces `Cursor IDE` and `Cursor CLI` (and `VS Code`, `unverified` unless a page says otherwise). New candidate **C7**: Claude Code plus Cursor — Cursor imports Claude's agents, commands, `CLAUDE.md` and hooks: never admitted for removal (Claude Code needs its copies) → one residue row per location naming the IDE switch (Settings → Agents → Third-Party Imports) and that the Cursor CLI has none; the hooks half is `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`'s. Pages to read add the third-party hooks page and forum 166558. **Count correction (r1 N4):** the planner emits 177 unique paths for three clients, so planned − 25 = 152; 156 is the count on disk, which adds the manifest, the two keep files and `.gitignore` (u1-gitignore-lines leaves `.gitignore` with the three gate lines for this set).
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** `renderCapabilityMatrix()`, **then** the Cursor client section's "Locations read" table lists `.claude/agents/`, `.claude/commands/`, `CLAUDE.md` and `hooks in .claude/settings.json` with a reading and a citation each, and `## Repository-file residue` holds one C7 row per location naming the IDE switch and the CLI's lack of one (`test/emit/capabilityMatrix.test.ts`).
- **edgeCases, added:** A Cursor user who switched Third-Party Imports off in the IDE → the residue rows still stand for the CLI; the row says which surface has the switch.

### u2-upgrade-verb — `stamity upgrade --to <version>` runs the target release's sync and moves the package pin

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-upgrade-verb |
| `requirements` | REQ-FLOW-034, REQ-FLOW-048, REQ-FLOW-022 (amended) |
| `files` | `src/cli/commands/upgrade.ts` (new); `src/shared/npmEntry.ts` (new: npm's JavaScript entries, the probes of `runNpm`, `scripts/plugins/runtime.mjs:225-274`, which stays as it is); `src/cli.ts` (imports `:5-17`, docblock `:27-60`, `COMMANDS` `:61-75`); `src/cli/commands/sync.ts` (docblock `:28-33`, `updatePathHelp` `:62-72`); `src/cli/commands/sync/report.ts` (`syncJsonPayload` `:314-365`); `src/cli/notice/updateNotice.ts` (docblock `:9-16`; `UpdateNoticeOptions` `:85-106`; `noticeOptionsFromFacts` `:127-140`; `isOptedOut` `:202-211`; `buildBanner` `:288-323`; new `shouldProbeNotice`, `readRepositoryPin`); `src/cli/docs/llmsIndex.ts` (`:115`); `test/cli/commands/upgrade.test.ts` (new); `test/cli/commands/sync.test.ts` (`:399-400`, `:416-417`, one new payload case); `test/cli/surface.e2e.test.ts` (`:47-58`, `:72-74`, `:102`); `test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts` (two `PLAN_MAP` rows, near `:269` and `:549`); `test/docsPages.test.ts` (`:1176-1214`, `:2449-2462`, `REATTESTATION_DATE` `:597`); `README.md` (`:78-80`); `docs/getting-started.md` (`:197-200`, `:366-376`, `:395`, the "Which verbs need the manifest" bullet `:212`, the currency header `:5`); `docs/cli-reference.md` and `llms.txt` (regenerated); `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** No `upgrade` verb: `stamity upgrade` exits 2 "unknown command" (`test/cli/surface.e2e.test.ts:132-138`). `COMMANDS` holds 13 modules, 10 advertised (`src/cli.ts:61-75`). `CommandModule` = `{ name, summary, hidden?, mutating, args?, configure?(cmd), run(ctx, opts, args) → Promise<{ exitCode: 0 \| 1; json? }> }` (`src/cli/kit/program.ts:112-125`); the funnel registers `--json`, `-y`, and `--dry-run` for `mutating` modules and wraps the payload with `ok`, `command`, `version` (`:26-62`, `:270-291`). `pinnedCliPrefix(packageName, version, { npmChannel })` (`src/shared/cliCall.ts:92-108`) refuses a name outside the npm grammar and a version that is not semver. `packageName()`, `hasNpmChannel()`, `resolveOwnPackageFacts()` (`src/cli/kit/packageName.ts:141`, `:160`, `:92`) answer the running package's name, channel and `publishConfig.registry`. `detectPackageManager(rootDir)` (`src/detect/packageManager.ts:67`) → `{ name: "bun" \| "pnpm" \| "yarn" \| "npm"; lockfile: string \| null; fromPackageJsonField }`. `syncJsonPayload` (`src/cli/commands/sync/report.ts:314-365`) carries `dryRun`, `entries[{ path, action: "create" \| "update" \| "unchanged" \| "collision", adapter, artifactId }]`, `refused`, `reclaim`, `wrote`, `provenance`, `pluginOwned` and no client set. `updatePathHelp()` (`src/cli/commands/sync.ts:62-72`) ends "no separate update command exists". **New, sync payload:** `syncJsonPayload` gains `tools: [...plan.manifest.tools]`, the post-migration client set (additive). **New, the verb** (`src/cli/commands/upgrade.ts`): `export const upgradeCommand: CommandModule = { name: "upgrade", summary: "move this setup to another release: run that release's sync and move the package pin", mutating: true, configure(cmd) { cmd.requiredOption("--to <version>", "the exact release to move to (no range, no tag)"); cmd.option("--allow-client-change", "proceed when that release would change the selected clients"); cmd.option("--allow-downgrade", "proceed when --to is older than the release that wrote this setup"); cmd.option("--allow-major", "proceed when --to is another major release"); }, run }`, and a pure orchestration the tests drive: `export async function runUpgrade(input: UpgradeInput, deps: UpgradeDeps): Promise<UpgradeReport>` with `interface UpgradeInput { rootDir: string; to: string; allowClientChange: boolean; allowDowngrade: boolean; allowMajor: boolean; dryRun: boolean; packageName: string; npmChannel: boolean; registry: string \| null; runningVersion: string }`, `interface ExecResult { status: number \| null; stdout: string; stderr: string }`, `interface UpgradeDeps { exec(command: "npm" \| "npx", args: readonly string[], cwd: string): Promise<ExecResult>; readFile(path: string): Promise<string \| null>; writeFile(path: string, text: string): Promise<void>; packageManager(rootDir: string): Promise<{ name: string; lockfile: string \| null }> }`, `interface UpgradeReport { from: string; to: string; noop: boolean; dryRun: boolean; clientSet: { before: string[]; after: string[] \| null }; changed: string[]; reclaimed: string[]; refused: string[]; pin: { field: "devDependencies" \| "dependencies"; from: string; to: string } \| null; pinNote: string \| null; lockfileStale: string \| null; next: string \| null }`. **Order** (every refusal is an `EngineError` with `code: "VALIDATION_ERROR"`, exit 1, written nothing, its message naming the flag that would allow it): (1) `readManifest(rootDir)` null → "this repository has no stamity setup; run `<packageCommand("init")>` first"; `from` = `manifest.generatedBy` when semver-valid, else `runningVersion`. (2) `pinnedCliPrefix(packageName, to, { npmChannel })` validates `--to`; a `+build` suffix is refused. `semver.eq(to, from)` with no pin, or a pin already reading `to` → `noop: true`, no exec, exit 0; `semver.eq(to, from)` with a pin reading another version → no exec, the pin is prepared (5a) and written (8), `noop: false`, `changed: ["package.json"]`, exit 0 (inbox row 443). `semver.lt(to, from)` without `--allow-downgrade` → "--to <to> is older than <from>, the release that wrote this setup; re-run with --allow-downgrade". `semver.major(to) !== semver.major(from)` without `--allow-major` → "…; re-run with --allow-major". (3) `npmChannel === false` → "this package has no npm channel, so npx cannot fetch <name>@<to>: install it into this project, then run `npx --no <name> sync`". `registry` non-null and not parsing with `new URL()` to the protocol `http:` or `https:` → "publishConfig.registry <registry> is not an http(s) URL; fix it in the package's package.json" (the self-read checks only that it is a non-empty string, `src/cli/kit/packageName.ts:100-110`). (4) `exec("npm", ["view", ...(registry ? [scopeRegistryArg(name, registry)] : []), `${name}@${to}`, "version", "--json"], rootDir)`; a non-zero status or stdout other than the JSON string `"<to>"` → "<name>@<to> is not published at <registry, or the registry npm is configured to use for this scope>". (5) Preflight `exec("npx", ["-y", ...(registry ? [scopeRegistryArg(name, registry)] : []), `${name}@${to}`, "sync", "--dry-run", "--json"], rootDir)`; stdout is parsed as one JSON document; `command !== "sync"` or `version !== to` → "npx ran <version> instead of <to>"; `ok === false` → the target's own `error.message`. `before` = `manifest.tools`; `after` = the envelope's `tools` when it is a string array, else `null`. `after === null` without `--allow-client-change` → "the <to> release does not report its client set; review `npx -y <name>@<to> sync --dry-run`, then re-run with --allow-client-change"; `after` differs from `before` as a set without the flag → "the <to> release would change the clients from <before> to <after>; re-run with --allow-client-change". (5a) Prepare the pin, before any sync: `package.json` at `rootDir` read when present; the dependency located by its key `<name>` in the parsed document, under `devDependencies`, else `dependencies`; an exact semver spec → `to`; `^x.y.z` or `~x.y.z` → the same operator with `to`; any other spec → unchanged, `pinNote: "package.json pins <name> as <spec>; left as written"`. The edited text replaces exactly the quoted JSON spelling of the current value once, searched from the end of the quoted key `"<name>"` that follows the section's own quoted key (`"devDependencies"` or `"dependencies"`), so a top-level `"version"` spelled the same is never the match; the result is re-parsed and must equal the parsed original with only that value changed (the technique of `retargetPreset`, `scripts/fork-identity.mjs:197-214`, whose first-match search is safe only because its refusal comes before every write). A key or value the search cannot find, or a re-parse that differs → "package.json: the <name> pin could not be rewritten in place, so nothing ran; write it as a plain JSON member and re-run". (6) `--dry-run` → report `changed` = preflight entries with action `create` or `update`, `reclaimed` = preflight `reclaim` candidates' paths, `refused` = entries with action `collision`, and `pin` and `pinNote` as step 5a prepared them; exit 0, nothing written. (7) Apply `exec("npx", ["-y", ...(registry ? [scopeRegistryArg(name, registry)] : []), `${name}@${to}`, "sync", "--json"], rootDir)`; a non-zero status or a non-empty `refused` → exit 1 after reporting, and no pin move. (8) Pin: when step 5a prepared a moved pin, `writeFile` writes exactly the prepared text — no second read and no second search — so every other byte survives. When the pin moved: `lockfileStale` = the detected lockfile, `next` = `"<npm \| pnpm \| yarn \| bun> install"`. (9) Human output: one line per step result, `changed N file(s), reclaimed M`, the pin line, `next: <command>` then `git diff`. **Spawn:** npm runs as the JavaScript program it is, as `runNpm` runs it (`scripts/plugins/runtime.mjs:244-274`), never through a shell while an entry resolves. **New, `src/shared/npmEntry.ts`** (node builtins only; `PLAN_MAP` `"src/shared/npmEntry.ts": { unit: "u2-upgrade-verb", wave: 1 }`): `export function npmJsEntry(command: "npm" \| "npx", probe: { execPath: string; npmExecpath: string \| undefined; exists(path: string): boolean } = { execPath: process.execPath, npmExecpath: process.env.npm_execpath, exists: existsSync }): string \| null` — `runNpm`'s two probes (`:226-242`): `npmExecpath` when it names an existing `npm-cli.[cm]?js` (another manager's entry is ignored), answering it for `npm` and its sibling `npx-cli.js` for `npx`; then `node_modules/npm/bin/<npm\|npx>-cli.js` beside `execPath` and `../lib/node_modules/npm/bin/<npm\|npx>-cli.js`; else `null`. `runtime.mjs` keeps its own copy: it runs before `dist/` exists, and `files: ["dist"]` ships no script (`package.json:41-43`). Production `exec` = `createExec({ platform: process.platform, entry: npmJsEntry, spawn })`, exported from `upgrade.ts`: with an entry it runs `spawn(process.execPath, [entry, ...args], { cwd, shell: false })` on every platform. Only when both probes miss does it fall back to the PATH name — `<cmd>` with `shell: false` off Windows; on win32 `<cmd>.cmd` with `shell: true`, since Node 18.20.2 / 20.12.2 (CVE-2024-27980) spawning a `.cmd` without a shell throws `EINVAL` — and in that win32 fallback, because cmd.exe takes the argv unquoted (`runtime.mjs:248-253`), an argument containing a space or one of `&\|<>^%"` → a `VALIDATION_ERROR` refusal naming it, before spawning. Every argument the verb passes is validated before it gets there: the package grammar and semver (`pinnedCliPrefix`), literals, and `registry` (step 3). **Exit:** 0 done, no-op or dry run; 1 refusal or a failed target sync; 2 a missing `--to` (commander). **JSON** (`--json`): the `UpgradeReport` fields under the funnel's envelope. **Surface pins this verb moves:** (1) `src/cli.ts` — import, `upgradeCommand` directly after `syncCommand` in `COMMANDS`, the docblock's "thirteen CommandModules" → "fourteen", "Ten advertised, thirteen registered" → "Eleven advertised, fourteen registered", and its help-order list; (2) `test/cli/surface.e2e.test.ts` — `ADVERTISED` gains `"upgrade"` after `"sync"`, "registers exactly 13" and `.toBe(13)` → 14, "--help lists exactly the 10 advertised commands" → 11; (3) `test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts` `PLAN_MAP` `"src/cli/commands/upgrade.ts": { unit: "u2-upgrade-verb", wave: 14 }` (its imports sit at waves 1, 2, 4 and 13: `shared/cliCall` and `shared/npmEntry`, `detect/packageManager`, `manifest/manifest`, the kit) and `"src/shared/npmEntry.ts": { unit: "u2-upgrade-verb", wave: 1 }` beside the other shared leaves (`:261-269`); (4) `docs/cli-reference.md` regenerated with `node scripts/generate-docs.mjs --page cli` (byte-compared by `test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts`); (5) `test/docsPages.test.ts` README array `:1176-1193` gains `"upgrade"` after `"sync"`, `toHaveLength(10)` → 11, `"ten verbs"` → `"eleven verbs"`; (6) `test/docsPages.test.ts` getting-started array `:2449-2462` gains `"upgrade"`; (7) `README.md:78-80` — the `·` list in CLI order and "ten verbs" → "eleven verbs"; beyond the learning's seven: (8) `src/cli/docs/llmsIndex.ts:115` "the ten verbs" → "the eleven verbs" and `llms.txt` regenerated with `node scripts/generate-docs.mjs --page llms`; (9) `docs/getting-started.md` `## The ten verbs` (`:197`), its `·` list (`:199-200`), the glossary row (`:395`) and the manifest bullet (`upgrade` needs the manifest); (10) `updatePathHelp()` and its two pins: channel line "`update = npx -y <name>@<version> upgrade --to <version> — runs that release's sync and moves the package.json pin; npx -y <name>@<version> sync regenerates without moving it.`", no-channel line "`update = install the newer release into this project, then npx --no <name> sync — upgrade needs an npm channel, which this package does not have.`"; (11) `docs/getting-started.md` "Keeping your setup current" (`:366-376`) leads with `npx -y @zomarit/stamity@<current> upgrade --to <version>` and keeps the sync line. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**`stamity upgrade --to <version>` moves a setup to another release in one step.** It checks the release exists in the package's own registry, previews that release's sync, refuses a changed client set, a downgrade or a major jump unless told otherwise, runs the sync through the pinned `npx` call, moves the `package.json` pin, and lists what changed (`--json` for an orchestrator)." **Follows:** `src/cli/commands/sync.ts:139-168` (a thin `CommandModule` over an engine function the tests drive) and `src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:125-200` (a spawn behind a seam with a bounded, total outcome) |

with `allowClientChange` the apply runs. **Given** a preflight envelope without `tools`, **then** exit 1 unless `allowClientChange`. **Given** envelope `version: "1.12.9"` for `--to 1.13.0`, **then** exit 1 "npx ran 1.12.9". **Given** `npm view` exit 1 with `publishConfig.registry` set, **then** exit 1 and the message names that registry. **Given** `--dry-run`, **then** `writeFile` is never called, the apply exec never runs, and `report.pin` equals the move step 5a prepared. **Given** a `package.json` whose top-level `version` is `"1.12.0"` before `devDependencies["@zomarit/stamity"] = "1.12.0"`, **then** the upgrade moves only the dependency value and `version` still reads `"1.12.0"`. **Given** the dependency key spelled with a JSON escape (`"@zomarit\/stamity": "1.12.0"`), **then** exit 1 with `VALIDATION_ERROR` naming the pin, `exec` called exactly twice (view, preflight), and `writeFile` never called. **Given** `"^1.12.0"`, **then** it becomes `"^1.13.0"`; the stub sync writes nothing). **Given** `createExec` over a stub `spawn` and an entry resolver that answers, on `platform` `"win32"` and `"linux"` alike, **then** `npx` spawns `process.execPath` with `[<…>/npx-cli.js, ...args]` and `npm` with `[<…>/npm-cli.js, ...args]`, both with `shell: false`. **Given** a resolver that answers `null` on `win32`, **then** a validated argv spawns `npx.cmd` with `shell: true`, and **when** `runUpgrade` runs over that exec with a registry containing `&` (`https://r.example/a&b`) or a space, **then** exit 1 with `VALIDATION_ERROR` and the stub `spawn` never called. **Given** `registry` `"file:///srv/npm"` or `"not a url"`, **then** exit 1 before any exec. **Given** `npmJsEntry` with `npmExecpath` naming an existing `npm-cli.js`, **then** `npm` resolves to it and `npx` to its sibling `npx-cli.js`; **with** `npmExecpath` naming another manager's entry, **then** the copy beside `execPath` answers; **with** neither present, **then** `null`. **Given** the built CLI, **when** `stamity upgrade --help` runs, **then** exit 0 and the four flags are listed; `stamity upgrade` without `--to` exits 2. **Given** a sync run, **then** `syncJsonPayload(...).tools` equals the manifest's tools (`test/cli/commands/sync.test.ts`). **Given** the surface suites, **then** `npx vitest run test/cli/surface.e2e.test.ts test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts test/cli/docs/cliReference.test.ts test/cli/docs/llmsIndex.test.ts test/cli/commands/sync.test.ts` exits 0 with the pins above moved, each with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` line. |
| `edgeCases` | No `package.json` → `pin: null`, `pinNote: "no package.json; the pin lives in the npx call your scripts or orchestrator run"`, nothing created. A `package.json` without the dependency → `pin: null`. A pin present but not rewritable in place (its key or value spelled with a JSON escape, or a re-parse that would change another member) → the step-5a refusal, exit 1, before the apply sync and before any write. A workspace monorepo → only the root `package.json` is read; the note says so. A dirty tree → the target sync's own dirty-tree warning passes through on stderr. A registry that needs credentials → npm reads the user's `.npmrc`; a 401 is the step-4 refusal. A fork named `@acme/stamity` with `publishConfig.registry` → `npm view --@acme:registry=<url> @acme/stamity@<to>` and `npx -y --@acme:registry=<url> @acme/stamity@<to>`; a registry outside `REGISTRY_URL` → exit 1 before any exec. A downgrade allowed past a manifest-schema change → the older sync refuses the newer manifest and its error is reported verbatim, exit 1. |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **files, added:** `src/shared/cliCall.ts` (read only: `scopeRegistryArg`, `REGISTRY_URL`, from file 0's `u0-registry-bound-calls`).
- **interfaces, added:** measured 2026-10-06 with npm 10.9.8, 11.21.0 and 12.2.0 against local registry stubs: `--registry <url>` sends every dependency to the fork's registry (`npm error 404` on the first public one), while `--@<scope>:registry=<url>`, placed before the package spec, sends only the scope there; the check and the fetch must name the same registry the same way.
- **files, added:** `src/cli.ts` (`main` `:118-147`: the notice fired at `:125-131`, the banner printed at `:141-144`), `src/cli/notice/updateNotice.ts` (beyond the docblock: `UpdateNoticeOptions` `:85-106`, `noticeOptionsFromFacts` `:127-140`, `checkForUpdateNotice` `:146-180`, `isOptedOut` `:202-211`, `buildBanner` `:288-323`), `test/cli/notice/updateNotice.test.ts` (the opt-out table `:138-169`; the banner cases from `:171`), `docs/getting-started.md` (`:268-271`), `docs/packs-and-trust.md` (`:212-219`), `SECURITY.md` (`:151-158`).
- **interfaces, added:** **The probe gate (principle 6).** `export function shouldProbeNotice(input: { stderrIsTTY: boolean; argv: readonly string[] }): boolean` = `input.stderrIsTTY && !input.argv.includes("--json")`; `src/cli.ts` fires `checkForUpdateNotice` only when it answers true and otherwise races `Promise.resolve(null)`, so a non-interactive or `--json` run makes no request and writes no stamp (measured 2026-10-06, r2 P6-1: `check`, `sync -y`, `init -y`, `clean -y` and `--version`, non-TTY, each sent one request and wrote `update-check.json`). `isOptedOut` takes `STAMITY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK` trimmed and lower-cased as `1`, `true` or `yes` (r2 measured `true` still probing); `NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER` and `CI` keep their any-non-empty rule. **The banner compares with the repository's pin and names the verb** (re-opens inbox row 394): `export async function readRepositoryPin(cwd: string, packageName: string): Promise<string \| null>` — the package's own entry in `<cwd>/package.json` (`devDependencies`, else `dependencies`; an exact version, or the version part of a `^`/`~` range), else `<cwd>/.stamity/manifest.json` `generatedBy` when semver-valid, else `null`; never throws. `noticeOptionsFromFacts(facts, env, cacheDir, repositoryPin)` sets `UpdateNoticeOptions.repositoryPin?: string`. `buildBanner` compares `semver.gt(target, repositoryPin ?? currentVersion)` and prints `Update available: <pin> -> <target>. To move: <call> upgrade --to <target><" --allow-major" when the majors differ>. To stay on <pin>, do nothing.`, where `<call>` is the pinned call at `<target>` in file 0's registry-bound form (the canonical spelling stays `npx -y <name>@<target>`; a package naming `publishConfig.registry` gets the form that passes that registry, so the fetch asks the registry the probe asked — r2 C2-4, M15). **The fetch and the help line.** Steps 5 and 7 and `updatePathHelp()`'s two lines use the same registry-bound form; step 4 keeps `npm view <name>@<to> version --json --registry <registry>`. **Inbox row 443:** the no-op holds only when the pin agrees; when `generatedBy` equals `--to` and `package.json` pins another version (a pinned `sync` moved the files and left the pin), the verb runs no exec, step 5a prepares the pin, step 8 writes it, and the report reads `noop: false`, `changed: ["package.json"]`, exit 0. **Docs:** `docs/getting-started.md:270`, `docs/packs-and-trust.md:217` and `SECURITY.md:157` say the notice asks only in an interactive terminal without `--json`, and that `STAMITY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK` takes `1`, `true` or `yes`.
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** `shouldProbeNotice` with `{ stderrIsTTY: false, argv: ["check"] }`, `{ stderrIsTTY: true, argv: ["check", "--json"] }` and `{ stderrIsTTY: true, argv: ["check"] }`, **then** false, false, true (`test/cli/notice/updateNotice.test.ts`). **Given** `STAMITY_NO_UPDATE_CHECK` set to `true`, `TRUE`, `yes` or ` 1 `, **then** no probe and no stamp; **given** `0` or `false`, **then** the probe runs (the table at `:138-169` gains these rows). **Given** a root `package.json` pinning `1.12.0` in `devDependencies`, a running version `1.13.0` and a stub registry answering `1.13.0`, **then** the banner is exactly `Update available: 1.12.0 -> 1.13.0. To move: npx -y @zomarit/stamity@1.13.0 upgrade --to 1.13.0. To stay on 1.12.0, do nothing.`; **given** the pin at `1.13.0`, **then** no banner; **given** no `package.json` and a manifest whose `generatedBy` is `1.12.0`, **then** the first banner again; **given** a registry answer `2.0.0`, **then** the move ends `upgrade --to 2.0.0 --allow-major`. **Given** a fork fixture whose `publishConfig.registry` is `https://npm.pkg.github.com`, **then** the move names that registry in file 0's form. **Given** the existing banner case (`:171`), **then** it is re-pinned to the `upgrade` form with `TEST CHANGE, justified: REQ-FLOW-048 — the banner names the pinned upgrade`. **Given** `--to` equal to `generatedBy` and `devDependencies` pinning the version before, **when** `runUpgrade` runs, **then** exit 0, `noop: false`, `exec` never called, `package.json` reads `--to` and `changed` is `["package.json"]` (`test/cli/commands/upgrade.test.ts`). **Given** a registry-bound package, **then** the preflight and apply argv carry the registry-bound form and step 4's `--registry` names the same URL.
- **edgeCases, added:** A fleet rollout run in CI with `CI` unset but no terminal → no probe at all. A pin written as a range (`^1.12.0`) → compared by its version; a tag or URL spec → falls back to `generatedBy`. A running CLI older than the pin (an old global install in a pinned repository) → the banner compares with the pin and names the pin as the version to stay on. `--help` and `--version` in a terminal → the probe may run, as the banner can print there. A target below the running CLI's version (the registry's `latest` behind a newer local CLI, above an older pin), which may predate the verb → the move prints the pinned `sync` form instead; `upgrade --to` is printed only when the target is at or above the running version, which carries the verb.

| `interfaces` | **Why `main` is npm-sourced today, and what that protected.** c458bf45 (2026-08-26) added the four root manifests when they were the only plugin route; the source was npm because "npm is this package's published channel … the tarball is what trusted publishing signs and what a user already installs" (`scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs:442-444`). The distribution tree (`plugin-dist`, `plugins/v*`) arrived 2026-09-17 (cba661df) and froze the root files rather than re-deciding them (`docs/plans/008-plugin-lifecycle-01.md:61-62`). 9127b70d (2026-09-19) made a private package's entry `{source:'github', repo}` with checkout paths and no ref (`:461-463`), because an npm source on a private copy "advertises a package name that resolves to nothing (or, worse, to somebody else's package of that name)" (`:446-450`). What the npm root delivers: the tarball (`files: ["dist"]`) has no `hooks/`, no `runtime/`, no `stamity-plugin.json`, and 11 declared bodies still carry `${STAMITY:*}` tokens (compared with `origin/plugin-dist:claude/`, research return 2026-10-01); Codex without `--ref` falls back to it and installs that (walked 2026-09-24, `scripts/plugins/clients/codex.mjs:183-187`); the Copilot CLI reads `marketplace.json`, `.plugin/marketplace.json`, `.github/plugin/marketplace.json`, then `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` (docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-plugin-reference), so it lands on the same file. **Kept from the old decision:** (1) a ref-less add installs a released, immutable, pinned artifact — now the release root at `plugins/v<version>`, a tag the release step refuses to move (`.github/workflows/release.yml:977-983`); (2) a private fork's catalog never names a package or source it does not publish — every entry addresses the identity's own repository, whose tag the fork's `fork-release.yml` publishes (`.github/workflows/fork-release.yml:671-751`); (3) `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, `.cursor-plugin/plugin.json` and `plugin.json` keep their bytes (the APM 0.29.0 witness job installs a clean export that carries them, `.github/workflows/ci.yml:448-450`; `docs/specs/apm-canonical-distribution.md:101-103`); (4) every catalog is generated from `package.json` and byte-checked (`ci.yml:327`, `:714`, `fork-release.yml:321`). **Current code.** `scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs:163-165` Claude default `{source:'git-subdir', url, path:'claude', ref: tag}`; `:256-270` `codexSource` returns `{source:'local', path:'./codex'}` when the URL is the identity's own (`:265`), right inside the tree and wrong on `main`, which has no `codex/`; `:222-237` the Copilot entry is the relative path `./copilot` because the object form's keys were "not stated on the pages read" (2026-09-20, `:29-35`). The reference now documents it: `{"source":"github","repo":"owner/repo","ref":"v1.0.0","path":"plugins/my-plugin"}` (cli-plugin-reference, read 2026-10-01). Cursor catalogs take only a relative directory path (`:22-28`), so `main` cannot carry a Cursor catalog that reaches a tag. **New exports in `scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs`:** `export const DEFAULT_BRANCH_CATALOG_PATHS = { claude: '.claude-plugin/marketplace.json', copilot: '.github/plugin/marketplace.json', codex: '.agents/plugins/marketplace.json' }` and `export function renderDefaultBranchCatalog(client, identity, version) → object`. It always addresses `identity.defaultGitUrl` / `identity.slug` at `releaseTag(identity, version)` and never reads `identity.distribution.sources` (those name where an organization's mirror serves roots; the default branch names this repository's own releases). `claude` → the `renderClaudeMarketplace` shape (`:169-190`: `name`, `owner{name,url}`, `description`, `version`, one plugin with `name`, `source`, `description`, `version`, `author`, `homepage`, `repository`, `license`, `keywords`) with `source: {source:'git-subdir', url: identity.defaultGitUrl, path:'claude', ref}` and no component paths (the release root's own manifest and default discovery carry them, `scripts/plugins/clients/claude.mjs:12-35`). `copilot` → the `renderCopilotMarketplace` shape (`:222-237`) with `source: {source:'github', repo: identity.slug, ref, path:'copilot'}` in that key order. `codex` → the `renderCodexMarketplace` shape (`:273-290`) with `source: {source:'git-subdir', url: identity.defaultGitUrl, path:'./codex', ref}`. `cursor` (or any other client) throws `catalogs: cursor catalogs accept only a directory path, so the default branch cannot address a release tag; Cursor serves a team marketplace from a distribution branch.` No `sha`: the distribution commit is unknown when the version commit lands. The header paragraph `:29-36` records the Copilot object form as documented (2026-10-01). **`scripts/generate-plugin-manifests.mjs`:** `marketplace` (`:475-496`) becomes `renderDefaultBranchCatalog('claude', catalogIdentity, version)` with `catalogIdentity = buildCatalogIdentity(pkg, identity)` imported from `./plugins/catalogs.mjs`; `MANIFESTS` (`:541-546`) gains `.github/plugin/marketplace.json` and `.agents/plugins/marketplace.json` rows, so `--check` verifies 6 files ("Verified 6 plugin manifest(s)"); `isPrivate` (`:261-279`), `TARBALL_ROOT`, `tarballPath`, `marketplacePrefix`, `marketplacePath` (`:385-413`, `:464-465`) are deleted with their comments, and the header paragraphs "TWO TREES" (`:110-117`) and "A PRIVATE PACKAGE HAS NO TARBALL" (`:119-123`) are replaced by one paragraph stating the rule above. `scripts/fork-identity.mjs` needs no edit: it already runs this generator (`:61`). **Assumed from `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md (u1-renovate-suffix)`:** `fork-identity.mjs --suffix <id>` writes suffix-aware patterns into the Renovate presets and leaves `package.json` `version` to the operator's `<X.Y.Z>-<id>.<n>`; the catalogs read that version through `releaseTag`, so no catalog code knows the suffix. **Codex README** `scripts/plugins/clients/codex.mjs:183-187` becomes: "Pin with `--ref`. Without it the client checks out the repository's default branch, whose `.agents/plugins/marketplace.json` names this root at the release tag of that branch's version — which can sit behind the newest tag, or ahead of a release still publishing." **Docs** `docs/plugins.md:267-277` ("The `--ref` is not optional …") becomes the same two facts in the page's voice; the add lines keep their `--ref` (`test/docsPages.test.ts:2392-2403` stays true). **CONTRIBUTING** `:209` row gains `.github/plugin/marketplace.json` and `.agents/plugins/marketplace.json`. **CHANGELOG** `### Changed` under `## [Unreleased]`: "**The default branch's catalogs install the full release root.** `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` named the npm package, which carries no hooks, no bundled runtime and unresolved placeholders, and Codex and the Copilot CLI fell back to it when a marketplace was added without a ref.
