**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **files, added:** the executing run's record (`vscode-catalog.md`).
- **interfaces, added:** **The rule (brief P7): whatever catalog the Copilot CLI or VS Code reads on the default branch resolves to the `copilot/` root, never the `claude/` root.** The Copilot CLI is settled by order: it searches `marketplace.json`, `.plugin/marketplace.json`, `.github/plugin/marketplace.json`, then `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` (this unit's own citation), so `.github/plugin/marketplace.json` answers it, and no default-branch catalog at a path it searches earlier may name another root. VS Code is not settled by any page ("which catalog file it reads: not stated", `vendor-facts.md`), so the rule is declared before the walk: (1) a maintainer step, scheduled with the early VS Code install (Execution order step 2): VS Code with `chat.plugins.marketplaces` naming a `file://` URI of a scratch clone of this unit's branch, adding the marketplace with no ref; the record names the VS Code version, the catalog file it read and the root it installed (the `copilot/` root carries `com.github.copilot/`); (2) `copilot/` → verdict `VS Code catalog: COPILOT`, done; (3) `claude/` → the unit adds a root-level `marketplace.json` rendering the Copilot catalog (`DEFAULT_BRANCH_CATALOG_PATHS.copilotRoot = 'marketplace.json'`, a seventh `MANIFESTS` row), re-walks, and keeps it only when VS Code then installs `copilot/` and the APM legs stay green (verdict `VS Code catalog: ROOT-FILE`); otherwise the file is removed and the verdict is `VS Code catalog: STOP` — the default-branch Claude catalog is never pointed at `copilot/` (Claude Code reads only that file), the unit records `Not done:` with the maintainer as owner, and `u3-rollout-guides` tells VS Code users to add the Copilot catalog's source; (4) a walk not performed → `Not done:` with its owner, and the rule moves to `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`'s VS Code walk, which then owns step (3).
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** the default branch's catalogs, **then** every catalog file at a path the Copilot CLI searches before `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json` names the `copilot` root (`test/ci/pluginManifests.test.ts`). **Given** the record, **then** it carries one verdict line `VS Code catalog: COPILOT`, `ROOT-FILE` or `STOP`, the VS Code version and the date; `ROOT-FILE` comes with `generate-plugin-manifests.mjs --check` printing "Verified 7 plugin manifest(s)".
- **edgeCases, added:** A root `marketplace.json` that changes APM's format detection → a red APM leg stops the root file, and the verdict is `STOP`. VS Code adding a `git-subdir` source it cannot resolve → the record says so; nothing wrong installs.

| `interfaces` | **Current.** Four corpus bodies carry one byte-identical sentence, pinned as `RUNNING_CLI_SENTENCE` in `test/corpus/cliCallForm.test.ts:32-33` at the call sites `content/skills/st-handoff/SKILL.md:23`, `content/skills/st-learn/SKILL.md:50`, `content/commands/st-debug.md:30` and `content/commands/st-work.md:160` (as the bullet `- **CLI calls.** …`): "Every `stamity <verb>` call in this file runs as `npx --no stamity <verb>`, which runs an installed copy — a `stamity` bin the project's own `package.json` declares, one in `node_modules/.bin` here or in a parent folder, or a global one — and never downloads a package; where npm refuses because no copy is installed, the call runs as `${STAMITY:CLI} <verb>`, the version this setup was generated with. Never `@latest`, and never `stamity <verb>` typed bare at the shell." `content/commands/st-quick.md:123-124` carries the token without the sentence. The plugin build stages the corpus once for all four clients (`scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs:458-464`, `cli: { packageName, version: releaseVersion, npmChannel }`), and `tokens.substitute` maps `${STAMITY:CLI}` to `pinnedCliPrefix(cli)` = `npx -y <pkg>@<ver>` (`npx --no` without an npm channel) (`scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs:50-62`, `:107-119`), so every plugin body names `npx --no stamity` first and the network form second, and no body names `runtime/locate.mjs` (0 hits in the four roots). The locator spawns the companion install when its version fits the plugin's range, else the bundled `runtime/dist/cli.js`, never PATH and never the network (`scripts/plugins/locate.mjs:12-32`, `:408-422`). A command's shell gets no plugin-root variable on the Copilot CLI (measured 1.0.87, `scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:31-41`) or on Codex (exported to hook processes only, `scripts/plugins/clients/codex.mjs:208-210`). **New exports in `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs`:** `RUNNING_CLI_SENTENCE` (the exact sentence above, token unresolved); `LOCATOR_CALL = 'node "<root>/runtime/locate.mjs" --'`; `pluginCliSentence(cli) → string`, returning exactly: "Every `stamity <verb>` call in this file runs first as `node "<root>/runtime/locate.mjs" -- <verb>`, through the runtime this plugin bundles: `<root>` is this plugin's root, the nearest folder above this file that holds `stamity-plugin.json` (where your client sets `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT`, `CURSOR_PLUGIN_ROOT` or `PLUGIN_ROOT` in the shell, that variable names the same folder), and the locator runs the repository's own stamity install when its version fits this plugin and the bundled copy otherwise, and never downloads a package. Where the locator cannot run, the two npm forms apply: `npx --no stamity <verb>`, which runs an installed copy and never downloads a package, and where npm refuses because no copy is installed, `<PINNED> <verb>`, the version this plugin was built with. Never `@latest`, and never `stamity <verb>` typed bare at the shell." with `<PINNED>` = `pinnedCliPrefix(cli)`. `substitute(body, cli)`: when `cli.runtime === 'bundled'`, it first replaces every occurrence of `RUNNING_CLI_SENTENCE` with `pluginCliSentence(cli)`, then maps `${STAMITY:CLI}` to `LOCATOR_CALL` instead of the pinned call; any other `runtime` value throws `Cannot render the CLI call: runtime must be "bundled" when it is given.`; without `runtime`, behaviour is byte-identical to today (the APM generator and every other caller keep the pinned call). The phrase "neither form" in each site's next sentence then reads as the two npm forms. **Generator:** `scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs:463` passes `cli: { packageName, version: releaseVersion, npmChannel, runtime: 'bundled' }`. No per-client staging: `<root>` is client-neutral because two of the four clients export no root variable to a shell. **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**Plugin skills run the runtime their plugin bundles.** In a plugin root, `st-learn`, `st-handoff`, `st-debug`, `st-work` and `st-quick` call the CLI through the root's `runtime/locate.mjs` first, which runs the repository's own install or the bundled copy and never downloads one; the `npx` forms are the fallback." **Follows:** `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs:107-119` (one pure substitution pass, unresolved tokens reported) and `test/ci/pluginModules.test.ts:426-437` (staging driven through `stageFor`). **Surface pins moved:** none. |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** a synthetic corpus body holding `RUNNING_CLI_SENTENCE` and a fenced line `${STAMITY:CLI} learn capture --title t`, **when** `substitute` runs with `{ packageName: PACKAGE, version: "1.11.0", runtime: "bundled" }`, **then** the text holds `pluginCliSentence(...)` once, the fenced line reads `node "<root>/runtime/locate.mjs" -- learn capture --title t`, `unresolved` is empty, and the string `npx -y` appears only inside the sentence's fallback clause (in `test/ci/pluginModules.test.ts`). **Given** the same body without `runtime`, **then** the output equals today's (`npx --no stamity` sentence, `npx -y <pkg>@1.11.0 learn capture`). **Given** `npmChannel: false` and `runtime: "bundled"`, **then** the sentence's last form is `npx --no <pkg>@1.11.0`. **Given** `runtime: "network"`, **then** `substitute` throws "runtime must be". **Given** the four corpus call sites, **then** each file contains `tokens.RUNNING_CLI_SENTENCE` byte-for-byte (so a corpus edit to the sentence fails here, not in a plugin user's session). **Given** each built root (claude, cursor, copilot, codex), **then** every body under the root containing `runtime/locate.mjs` or `npx --no stamity` names `runtime/locate.mjs` at a lower index than any `npx` (in each `test/ci/pluginPackages.<client>.test.ts`), and no body contains `RUNNING_CLI_SENTENCE` with its token resolved to the pinned call. **Given** the cursor oracle staged with `runtime: "bundled"` (`test/ci/pluginPackages.cursor.test.ts:197-206`), **then** its byte comparisons stay exact; **given** the copilot `cli` rule (`test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts:346-354`), **then** each moved line equals its native twin after replacing the plugin sentence with the native sentence and `LOCATOR_CALL` with the native pinned call — both re-pinned with `TEST CHANGE, justified: plan 016 file 2, u2-skills-bundled-runtime moves plugin bodies to the locator form (REQ-PLUGIN-042)`. **Given** a stub runtime whose `cli.js` exits 7 on `learn capture`, **when** the locator runs with argv `-- learn capture --title t`, `npm_config_registry=http://127.0.0.1:9` and a `PATH` holding only the Node binary's directory, **then** the stub receives exactly `learn capture --title t` and the locator exits 7 (in `test/ci/pluginLocate.test.ts`): the locator form needs neither npm nor a network. **Given** the repository route, **when** `npm run build && node dist/cli.js check` runs, **then** it exits 0 with no drift: `.claude/skills/st-learn/SKILL.md` and the other CLI-route bodies are byte-identical to before. |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **files, added:** `scripts/plugins/locate.mjs` (`parseVersion` `:96-105`; `satisfiesCaret` and its doc `:115-140`), `scripts/plugins/capability.mjs` (the `CARET_RANGE` doc `:66-76`), `test/ci/pluginLocate.test.ts` (beside `:286-299`).
- **interfaces, added:** **Brief F12: a fork's companion skew.** `satisfiesCaret` accepts only the identical prerelease under a prerelease range (`scripts/plugins/locate.mjs:136`), so a repository whose own install is `1.12.0-<suffix>.2` never uses it under a root at `1.12.0-<suffix>.1` and falls back to the bundled runtime. New rule: a prerelease of the form `<id>.<n>` (`<id>` one or more dot-separated identifiers, `<n>` a number without a leading zero) is split into `<id>` and `<n>`; a range `^X.Y.Z-<id>.<n>` accepts a companion with the same major (for `0.x`, the same minor; for `0.0.x`, the same triple — today's caret shapes), a prerelease of the same `<id>`, and `(triple, n)` at or ahead of the range's; every other prerelease range keeps today's exact equality; a released companion under a prerelease range, and a prerelease companion under a released range, stay refused. The doc comment lists the fifth shape (`^1.12.0-acme.1   the same major and id, at or ahead of (1.12.0, 1)`), and `scripts/plugins/capability.mjs:66-76` says the locator now orders same-id prereleases.
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** a root at `1.12.0-acme.1`, **then** companions at `1.12.0-acme.2`, `1.12.1-acme.1` and `1.13.0-acme.1` resolve `companion`, and companions at `1.12.0-acme.0`, `1.12.0-other.1`, `1.12.0` and `2.0.0-acme.1` resolve `bundled` (`test/ci/pluginLocate.test.ts`); **given** a root at `1.9.0-rc.1`, **then** `1.9.0-rc.2` resolves `companion` and `1.9.0` still `bundled` — the case at `:286-293` renamed with `TEST CHANGE, justified: REQ-PLUGIN-007 — same-id prereleases are ordered`.
- **edgeCases, added:** A prerelease without a numeric last part (`1.12.0-acme`) → exact equality, as today. A companion at `1.12.0-acme.10` under `^1.12.0-acme.9` → numeric, not string, order (accepted).

| `interfaces` | **Current.** `primitive(head, item)` = `composeFrontmatter(head, item.body)` (`scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:570-572`): bodies ship verbatim, tokens included — 36 occurrences in 11 files over 6 tokens (`CLI` ×12, `VERIFY_GATE_ALL` ×7, `VERIFY_GATE_TEST` ×6, `VERIFY_GATE_LINT` ×5, `VERIFY_GATE_TYPECHECK` ×5, `TEST_FRAMEWORK` ×1; `rg -no '\$\{STAMITY:[A-Z_]*\}' .apm` at `88fcfd32`), on the reasoning of `:92-99` and the maintainer decision of 2026-09-17 ("APM unchanged", `.stamity/inbox.md:17`). The plugin side: `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs` exports `TOKEN_PREFIX`, `CLI_TOKEN`, `pinnedCliPrefix({ packageName, version, npmChannel })` (`:50-62`), `CHARTER_REFERENCE_PHRASES` (eight tokens → phrases, `:70-79`, bound to `REPO_SUBSTITUTION_TOKENS` by `test/ci/pluginModules.test.ts`), and `substitute(body, cli)` → `{ text, unresolved }` over the bounded `TOKEN_PATTERN = /\$\{STAMITY:[^}\n]*\}/g` (`:95`, `:107-119`). `scripts/generate-plugin-packages.mjs:332-334`: `npmChannel = !(pkg.private === true \|\| pkg.private === 'true') \|\| (typeof pkg.publishConfig?.registry === 'string' && pkg.publishConfig.registry !== '')`. **New, tokens.mjs:** `export function substituteWith(body, values)` — `values: Readonly<Record<string, string>>`, token → replacement; the same bounded pattern, `$` literal (replacer function), unknown tokens left standing and listed once each in first-appearance order. `substitute(body, cli)` keeps its exact results by delegating: `substituteWith(body, { ...CHARTER_REFERENCE_PHRASES, ...(cliValue === null ? {} : { [CLI_TOKEN]: cliValue }) })`. **New, the generator:** it computes `npmChannel` as above and `const cli = { packageName, version, npmChannel }`, refusing once (exit 1) when `pinnedCliPrefix(cli)` throws; `APM_TOKEN_VALUES = { ...CHARTER_REFERENCE_PHRASES, [CLI_TOKEN]: pinnedCliPrefix(cli) }`; `primitive(head, item)` → `composeFrontmatter(head, resolveBody(item))` with `resolveBody(item) = substituteWith(item.body, APM_TOKEN_VALUES)`; any `unresolved` → `fail("<item.relativePath>: <tokens> is not resolvable in an APM body — a package is built once for every consumer, so a token here would reach the agent as a broken variable")` before anything is written. The APM bodies take the plain pinned `npx` call through `substituteWith`, never through `substitute`, so a plugin-root-only rendering of `${STAMITY:CLI}` (u2-skills-bundled-runtime) cannot reach them. Skill companion files (`references/`, `scripts/`, `agents/openai.yaml`) stay byte-verbatim (`scripts/plugins/corpusStage.mjs:11-19`'s opaque rule). `--check`: after the byte comparison, every rendered and every committed `.md` under `.apm/` is scanned with `TOKEN_PATTERN`; a hit adds the drift line `<path>: unresolved ${STAMITY:…} token(s): <list>`. The header paragraph `:92-99` is rewritten: bodies carry the plugin roots' charter-reference phrases (REQ-PLUGIN-004) and the CLI call pinned to this package and version; the 2026-09-17 decision is reversed by plan 016. Result: `.apm/skills/st-learn/SKILL.md`'s `${STAMITY:CLI} learn capture` → `npx -y @zomarit/stamity@<package.json version> learn capture`; `.apm/agents/stamity-test-runner.agent.md:27`'s `${STAMITY:VERIFY_GATE_TEST}` → "the Tests command listed under Verification gates in AGENTS.md"; a registry-less private fork renders `npx --no @acme/stamity@<version>`. The unterminated prose `${STAMITY:` at `content/agents/stamity-test-runner.md:79` stays (the bounded pattern does not match it). **Docs:** `docs/getting-started.md`'s APM section says the package carries no template token: gates and facts read as pointers into the consuming repository's `AGENTS.md`, CLI calls are pinned to the package's version. **Inbox:** the row at `.stamity/inbox.md:17` leaves with one line in the run record (the inbox header's retirement rule, `:3`). **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**The APM package carries no template token.** Gate and fact tokens read as the same pointers into `AGENTS.md` the plugin roots carry, and every CLI call is the pinned `npx -y @zomarit/stamity@<version>` call; `generate-apm-package.mjs --check` fails on any token left. The CLI calls name the package version, so `.apm/` moves with every release." **Follows:** `scripts/plugins/corpusStage.mjs:64-101` (`stageDirectory`: substitute bodies, refuse unresolved tokens by file, copy companions opaquely) |

### u2-apm-charter — carry the charter to APM-only consumers as its own small package, and document per target what reaches

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-apm-charter |
| `requirements` | REQ-APM-012, REQ-APM-009 |
| `files` | `scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs` (usage `:6-15`; the charter-gap paragraph `:161-180`; `USAGE` `:278`; argument parsing `:345-359`; a new `renderCharterPackage()` beside `renderPackage` `:626-713`); `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs` (a second generator call after the `apm/` one; README `## APM`; the `apm` block); `scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs` (`APM_KEYS` `:33`; `buildReleaseManifest` `:120`; `validateApm` `:215-224`); `test/ci/apmPackage.test.ts` (a new `describe("the charter package")`); `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts` (`:72`, `:109`; new cases); `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts` (`manifest.apm`; the README line); `test/ci/pluginDownstream.test.ts` (the fork's charter spec and CLI call); `docs/getting-started.md` (APM section: the APM-only flow and the per-target table); `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (`:708-711`); `test/docsPages.test.ts` (`REATTESTATION_DATE`); `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** The generator leaves the charter out by settled scope (`scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:161-170`). The charter template is `content/charter/stamity-charter.md` (frontmatter `description`, `invariants_version: 1.1.0`, `invariants_ratified: 2026-08-31`, `invariants_amended: 2026-09-27`; body tokens `LINTER`, `TEST_FRAMEWORK`, `CI_PROVIDER`, `MATURITY_TIER`, `CLI` at `:23-26`, the four gates at `:32-35`, `INVARIANTS_VERSION` at `:38`). `readCharterTemplate(contentRoot?)` (`src/content/charter.ts:452`) returns `{ frontmatter, invariants: { version, ratified, amended } \| null, body, … }`; `renderInvariantsVersion(invariants)` (`src/emit/substitution.ts:277-279`) renders `<version> · ratified <date> · last amended <date>`; `DETECTION_UNKNOWN = "unknown"` (`src/emit/substitution.ts:38`) is the engine's sentinel for a fact nothing detected, and the charter itself defines it ("The literal value `unknown` means detection found nothing — treat that item as unconfigured and report it", `content/charter/stamity-charter.md:20-21`). **Measured (probe 2026-10-01, apm-cli 0.32.0, a fixture package):** an instruction with no `applyTo` is deployed by `apm install` to `.claude/rules/<id>.md` with no frontmatter, to `.github/instructions/<id>.instructions.md` and `.cursor/rules/<id>.mdc` with `description` only, and to nothing for codex ("Instructions installed for codex. Run 'apm compile' to update AGENTS.md."); `apm compile --target codex` and `--target cursor` fold it into a new `AGENTS.md` under `## Global Instructions`; `--target copilot` writes no `AGENTS.md` while `.github/instructions/` is populated ("pass --force-instructions to write AGENTS.md"); `--target claude` writes no `CLAUDE.md` ("Claude Code reads .claude/rules/ directly"); compile never overwrites a hand-authored `AGENTS.md` ("Protected AGENTS.md: hand-authored file will not be overwritten"); a `.apm/context/*.context.md` primitive is neither deployed nor folded; per-dependency filters are `skill_subset` and `target_subset` only (`apm_cli/models/dependency/reference.py:104-105`), so a consumer cannot exclude one instruction of a package. **Hence the shape (see Concerns C1):** the charter is NOT an instruction of the main package, which every APM-backed repository installs and which would then deliver it twice; it is its own package, `apm-charter/` in the distribution tree, which APM-only consumers add and APM-backed repositories do not. **New, the generator:** `--charter` renders, under `--out-dir` (required with `--charter`), exactly two files: `apm.yml` = `name: <packageId>-charter`, `version`, `description: "The stamity charter as one always-on APM instruction: repository facts left unknown, the floor invariants, the touchpoint index."`, `author`, `license`, `type: instructions`; and `.apm/instructions/stamity-charter.instructions.md` = frontmatter `{ description: <charter frontmatter description> }` (no `applyTo`, see the edge case) over the charter body resolved with `substituteWith(body, { the four fact tokens and the four gate tokens: DETECTION_UNKNOWN, [INVARIANTS_VERSION_TOKEN]: renderInvariantsVersion(invariants), [CLI_TOKEN]: pinnedCliPrefix(cli) })` (`substituteWith` from u2-apm-placeholders); an unresolved token or a charter without invariants keys → exit 1 before writing. `--charter --check --out-dir <dir>` byte-compares the two files and reports any extra file as drift. The charter-gap paragraph `:161-180` is rewritten to state this. **New, the build:** after the `apm/` call, `runGenerator('generate-apm-package.mjs', ['--charter', '--out-dir', join(outDir, 'apm-charter')])`; `apm` gains `charterInstallSpec: `${identity.slug}/apm-charter#${tag}`` (FALLBACK: the fallback tag, the charter package pushed beside the main one). `scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs`: `APM_KEYS` = `['manifest', 'primitives', 'installSpec', 'charterInstallSpec']`, the builder copies it when present, and the validator checks it as a tree path **only when present**, so a `release.json` of an earlier release (no such key) still validates — the promote and rollback path of u2-channels-promote reads older manifests. README `## APM`: "APM-only repositories add the charter too: `apm install <slug>/apm-charter#<tag>`. A repository that also runs the CLI does not — the CLI writes the charter." **Measurement at execution** (clients from u2-apm-sparse-measure — 0.29.1 and the current client, 0.33.0 on 2026-10-06 — the charter package installed as a dependency through the declarative local route and, once a candidate tag exists, through the slim ref, a fresh consumer per target): per target the deployed path and its frontmatter; then `apm compile --target <t>` (and `--force-instructions` for copilot) in a consumer with no `AGENTS.md`, recording whether `AGENTS.md` or `CLAUDE.md` is written and carries `## Invariants`; then the same with a hand-authored `AGENTS.md`, recording "Protected". **Docs:** `docs/getting-started.md` gets the APM-only flow (`apm install`, then `apm compile`, with `--force-instructions` when copilot is a target) and a per-target table — claude: `.claude/rules/stamity-charter.md`, loaded every session (Claude Code loads a rule without `paths` at session start, `https://code.claude.com/docs/en/memory`, read on the run date); copilot: `.github/instructions/stamity-charter.instructions.md` without `applyTo` (what VS Code does with it, from `https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/copilot/customization/custom-instructions` read on the run date) and the compiled `AGENTS.md` with `--force-instructions`; cursor: an agent-requested rule plus the compiled `AGENTS.md`; codex: the compiled `AGENTS.md` only; and a "not delivered" line for anything the measurement shows missing. `docs/enterprise-forks.md:708-711` ("This package does not deliver stamity's charter…") is rewritten to point at the charter package. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**APM-only repositories can install the charter.** Each release's distribution carries `apm-charter/`, a one-instruction package holding the charter with its repository facts left `unknown`; `apm install zomarit/stamity/apm-charter#plugins/v<version>` adds it, and the getting-started page lists what each target loads. A repository that also runs the CLI leaves it out." **Follows:** `scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:626-713` (`renderPackage`: render everything into a map, write only after every refusal had its chance) |

### u2-channels-promote — release channels that move only through a verified promotion

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-channels-promote |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-035 |
| `files` | `scripts/distribution-identity.mjs` (`:21-25` defaults and `DISTRIBUTION_KEYS`; `:221-276` `resolveDistribution`), `scripts/promote-channel.mjs` (new), `.github/workflows/promote.yml` (new), `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs` (`renderReadme` `:376-438`: a `## Channels` section after `## APM`), `test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts` (`:88-99`, `:113-116`; new cases), `test/ci/promoteChannel.test.ts` (new), `test/ci/workflow.test.ts` (`:4141-4176` secrets list; `:4199-4241` writers list; a new `describe("promote.yml …")`), `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts` (a case beside `:535-584`), `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (a new H2 section `## Promote a release to a channel` after `## Release your fork` (`:904`) and before `## Roll the plugin out to your organization`, plus the page header's re-attestation line), `test/docsPages.test.ts` (one new case; `REATTESTATION_DATE` if the header moves), `.github/release-controls-checklist.md` (a new "Control 4 — channel promotion" section after `:129`), `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** `plugin-dist` is force-pushed to each release's orphan commit and `plugins/v<version>` is created once (`.github/workflows/release.yml:923-1003`; `fork-release.yml:671-751`, held byte-equal to it by `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`); the commit message is `plugins: v$VERSION from $GITHUB_SHA` and both git dates are `release.json`'s `sourceCommitDate` (`release.yml:969-970`), so the commit dates cannot measure a soak; the GitHub release is created under `v$VERSION` (`release.yml:1074`, `fork-release.yml:793`). `scripts/distribution-identity.mjs:25` `DISTRIBUTION_KEYS = ['branch', 'tagPattern', 'ownerEmail', 'sources']`; `:42` `REF_NAME = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._/-]*$/`; `:231-236` the branch check; `:272-275` result key order `branch, tagPattern, ownerEmail?, sources`. Each catalog entry pins `ref: plugins/v<version>` and the entry `version` (`scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs:158-165`, `:181`); Claude "detects updates by comparing versions" and "has no release-channel concept" (code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/host-marketplace, "Run release channels"). Rulesets today: `main-policy` (default branch) and `release-tags` (`refs/tags/v*`); nothing covers `plugin-dist`, channel branches or `refs/tags/plugins/v*` (`gh api` read, research return 2026-10-01). A workflow's `GITHUB_TOKEN` cannot be a ruleset bypass actor for `non_fast_forward` (field reports of HTTP 422 "Actor GitHub Actions integration must be part of the ruleset source or owner organization"; bypass actor types are `Integration`, `OrganizationAdmin`, `RepositoryRole`, `Team`, `DeployKey`, `User` — docs.github.com/en/rest/repos/rules; deploy keys became bypass actors on 2024-04-30). Environment required reviewers gate a `workflow_dispatch` job, and environment secrets are released only after approval; on Free, Pro and Team plans required reviewers work on public repositories only (docs.github.com deployments-and-environments). **New config** in `package.json` `stamity.distribution`: `channels` — an object whose keys are `stable` and optionally `canary`, each a branch name; default when absent `{ "stable": "plugin-stable" }`; and `soakDays` — an integer 0..90, default 0. `resolveDistribution` checks them after `tagPattern`: `DISTRIBUTION_KEYS` becomes `['branch', 'tagPattern', 'channels', 'soakDays', 'ownerEmail', 'sources']`; `channels` must be a plain object (`package.json stamity.distribution.channels must be an object keyed by channel.`), unknown keys refused through `refuseUnknownKeys(value, ['stable', 'canary'], 'channels')`, `stable` required when `channels` is given (`package.json stamity.distribution.channels.stable is required: a channel set always has a stable channel.`), each value passes the `branch` rule (`:232`) or throws `package.json stamity.distribution.channels.<name> must be a git branch name: …`, no value equals `branch` or another channel (`… must differ from stamity.distribution.branch and from every other channel.`), and no value matches the tag pattern's rendered shape (`tagPattern` with `<version>` → `\d+\.\d+\.\d+…`; `… must not look like a distribution tag.`); `soakDays` not an integer in 0..90 throws `package.json stamity.distribution.soakDays must be a whole number of days from 0 to 90.` The resolved object's key order becomes `branch, tagPattern, channels, soakDays, ownerEmail?, sources` (the catalog renderers read only `branch`, `tagPattern`, `ownerEmail` and `sources`, so no catalog byte moves). **`scripts/promote-channel.mjs`** (new; Node builtins plus `./distribution-identity.mjs`, `./plugins/catalogs.mjs`, `./plugins/releaseManifest.mjs`, `./plugins/version.mjs`). Usage: `node scripts/promote-channel.mjs --tag <tag> --channel <stable\|canary> --remote <git-url> [--verify-only] [--expect-commit <sha>] [--json]`. Exports for tests: `parseArguments(argv)`, `planPromotion({ identity, tag, channel, git, release, now }) → Promise<PromotionPlan>`, `pushPromotion(plan, git) → Promise<PushOutcome>`, where `git(args) → {status, stdout, stderr}` runs `git` with `shell: false` (injected), and `release` is `{ assetSha256(version, archive) → Promise<string\|null>, publishedAt(version) → Promise<string\|null> }` (the CLI binds it to `gh release download "v<version>" --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" --pattern '<archive>.sha256' --output -` and `gh release view "v<version>" --json publishedAt --jq .publishedAt`). `PromotionPlan` = `{ ok: boolean, tag, version, channel, branch, commit: string\|null, previous: { version: string, commit: string } \| null, rollback: boolean, soak: { requiredDays: number, publishedAt: string\|null, ageDays: number\|null } \| null, problems: string[] }`. Two refusals come before any read and exit 2: `channel` not configured (`--channel canary: stamity.distribution.channels names no canary branch.`), and `tag` not equal to `releaseTag(identity, v)` for a `PLUGIN_VERSION`-valid `v` (`<tag> is not a distribution tag of this identity (<tagPattern>).`). Then the checks, in order, each failure appending one problem (exit 1) and stopping later network reads: (1) `git ls-remote <remote> refs/tags/<tag>` names a commit (`<tag> does not exist on the remote.`); (2) after `git fetch --depth=2 <remote> refs/tags/<tag>`, the commit has no parent (`<tag> is not an orphan distribution commit.`); (3) its subject equals `plugins: v<version> from <40-hex>` (`<tag>'s commit message is not the release step's.`); (4) `git show <commit>:release.json` parses, `validateReleaseManifest` returns no defect, `version === <version>`, `distribution.tag === <tag>`, `distribution.branch === identity.distribution.branch` (each mismatch named); (5) for every `packages[]` entry the archive blob at `<commit>` hashes to `entry.sha256`, the `<archive>.sha256` blob reads `<sha256>  <archive>\n`, and `release.assetSha256` returns the same line (`<archive>: the release asset .sha256 differs from the tree.` or `… is missing on release v<version>.`); (6) with `soakDays > 0`, `release.publishedAt` is at least `soakDays` days before `now` (`soak: release v<version> was published <n> day(s) ago; the channel needs <soakDays>.`); (7) the channel head, when present, is an orphan whose subject has the release shape, read as `previous` (`<branch>'s head is not a distribution commit; refusing to move a branch with history.`); `rollback` is true when `previous.version` is semver-greater than `version`; the same commit is a no-op (`ok: true`, nothing pushed, message "already at v<version>"). `pushPromotion` runs `git push --force-with-lease=refs/heads/<branch>:<previous.commit or empty> <remote> <commit>:refs/heads/<branch>`; a refusal whose stderr carries `GH013`, `non-fast-forward`, `protected branch` or `Cannot force-push` exits 1 with: `A rule on refs/heads/<branch> refused the push. Give the promotion a bypass: store a write deploy key as the STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY secret of the plugin-promotion environment and add "Deploy keys" to that ruleset's bypass list. GITHUB_TOKEN cannot bypass a ruleset.` The remote URL is never printed (it may carry a token); every message names the branch and tag only. Exit codes: 0 promoted, verified or no-op; 1 a check or the push failed; 2 bad arguments or configuration. `--verify-only` stops after the plan; `--expect-commit` refuses (exit 1) when the planned commit differs from the one the verify job printed. **`.github/workflows/promote.yml`** (new): `name: Promote plugin channel`; `on: workflow_dispatch` with exactly two inputs, `tag` (string, required) and `channel` (choice: `stable`, `canary`); workflow `permissions: contents: read`; `concurrency: { group: promote-${{ inputs.channel }}, cancel-in-progress: false }`. Job `verify` (`permissions: contents: read`, `outputs: { ok, commit }` from step `plan`): SHA-pinned `actions/checkout` and `actions/setup-node` at the pins `release.yml` uses, then step `plan` runs `node scripts/promote-channel.mjs --tag "$TAG" --channel "$CHANNEL" --remote "https://github.com/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY.git" --verify-only --json` with `GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}` and inputs passed through `env:` (never interpolated into `run:`), writing the plan to the step summary and `ok` and `commit` to `$GITHUB_OUTPUT`. Job `promote` (`needs: verify`, `if: needs.verify.outputs.ok == 'true'`, `environment: plugin-promotion`, `permissions: contents: write`): checks out the repository at `${{ github.sha }}` and sets up Node with the same SHA-pinned actions, then runs the script with `--expect-commit "${{ needs.verify.outputs.commit }}"`; when the environment secret `STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY` is set it pushes over SSH (`git@github.com:$GITHUB_REPOSITORY.git`, key written to a `0600` temp file, `GIT_SSH_COMMAND="ssh -i <file> -o IdentitiesOnly=yes -o StrictHostKeyChecking=yes -o UserKnownHostsFile=<file holding GitHub's published ed25519 host key>"`), else over HTTPS with `GITHUB_TOKEN`. No `zomarit/stamity` literal: the identity comes from the checked-out `package.json`, so the same file serves the canonical repository and every fork. **Distribution README** (`renderReadme`): a `## Channels` section naming the channel branches from the identity, "the tree at `<tag>` reaches a channel only through the `Promote plugin channel` workflow (`.github/workflows/promote.yml`), which verifies the tag before it moves the branch", and "roll back by promoting the previous tag". **Docs:** the enterprise-forks section `## Promote a release to a channel` — its own H2, because `test/docsPages.test.ts:2300-2318` holds every `STAMITY_` name inside `## Release your fork` to the settings `fork-release.yml` reads — names the workflow, its two inputs, the `plugin-promotion` environment, `STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY` and the ruleset bypass, rollback, and the Team-plan limit; the release-controls checklist's Control 4 lists the environment's required reviewer and the channel ruleset (restrict creations, updates and deletions; block force pushes; "Deploy keys" the only bypass). **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**Release channels move only through a verified promotion.** `stamity.distribution.channels` names a `stable` branch (default `plugin-stable`) and an optional `canary`; the `Promote plugin channel` workflow checks the tag's commit, its `release.json`, every archive digest against the release assets and an optional `soakDays` before it moves a channel, behind an environment with required reviewers. Rollback is promoting the previous tag. `plugin-dist` still carries the newest release." **Follows:** `.github/workflows/release.yml:923-1003` (the push step's shape checks, remote read-first, orphan-head refusal) and `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:89-187` (argument sinks, exit 2 on usage). **Surface pins moved:** the closed secrets list (`test/ci/workflow.test.ts:4166-4174`) and the writers list (`:4228-4234`), each with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` line; no CLI verb, flag or `stamity config` key (the new keys live in `package.json` `stamity.distribution`, read by scripts only). |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** a fixture bare remote (`file://` URL) holding an orphan distribution commit tagged `plugins/v1.2.0` with subject `plugins: v1.2.0 from <40 hex>`, a valid `release.json` built with `buildReleaseManifest` and two archive blobs with matching `.sha256` blobs, and a release stub returning matching asset lines, **when** `planPromotion` then `pushPromotion` run for `stable`, **then** `git ls-remote <remote> refs/heads/plugin-stable` names the tag's commit and `plugin-dist` and every tag are unchanged (in `test/ci/promoteChannel.test.ts`); **when** run again, **then** it exits 0 with "already at v1.2.0" and pushes nothing. **Given** each defect in turn — a subject `plugins: v1.2.1 from …`, `release.json` `version` `1.2.1`, one archive byte changed, the stub's asset line differing, the tag absent, the commit having a parent — **then** the plan has `ok: false`, exactly one problem naming that check, and the remote's refs are byte-identical before and after (`git for-each-ref` compared). **Given** `soakDays: 3` and the stub's `publishedAt` one day before `now`, **then** the problem reads "published 1 day(s) ago; the channel needs 3"; with three days, **then** it promotes. **Given** the channel at `plugins/v1.3.0` and a promotion of `plugins/v1.2.0`, **then** the channel points at 1.2.0's commit and the plan carries `rollback: true` and `previous.version: "1.3.0"`. **Given** a channel head with a parent, **then** nothing is pushed and the problem names the branch. **Given** a remote whose `pre-receive` hook prints `GH013: Repository rule violations found` and exits 1, **then** `pushPromotion` exits 1 with the message naming `STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY` and "Deploy keys", and stderr does not contain the remote URL. **Given** `channels` naming `plugin-dist`, a value `-x`, a key `beta`, a missing `stable`, or `soakDays: 1.5`, **then** `resolveDistributionIdentity` throws naming that path (in `test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts`); the resolved-shape pins at `:88-99` move to `["branch","tagPattern","channels","soakDays","sources"]` with `channels: {stable:"plugin-stable"}` and `soakDays: 0` under a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` comment. **Given** `promote.yml`, **then** its dispatch inputs are exactly `tag` and `channel`, `promote` names `environment: plugin-promotion`, holds the only write grant and carries an `if:`, every action is SHA-pinned, and no line carries `zomarit/stamity` (in `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`). **Given** a full distribution build, **then** the README's `## Channels` section names `plugin-stable` and `.github/workflows/promote.yml` (in `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`). **Given** the enterprise-forks page, **then** its `## Promote a release to a channel` section names `STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY`, `plugin-promotion` and both inputs (in `test/docsPages.test.ts`). |
| `edgeCases` | A fork on a GitHub Team plan with a private repository gets no required reviewers: the `promote` job runs without approval, the verify checks still bind, and the docs section says so. A first promotion creates the channel branch (`--force-with-lease=refs/heads/<branch>:` expects it absent). Two dispatches for one channel queue on the `concurrency` group and the second one's lease protects against a race. A release asset missing because the release step partly failed fails the promotion, never the reverse. `canary` dispatched where no canary is configured exits 2 before any network read. Windows: the tests drive git through `execFileSync`; the `pre-receive` hook is a `#!/bin/sh` script that Git for Windows runs through its bundled shell — if the Windows leg cannot run it, that one case is skipped there with the reason written in the test, never marked passed. |
| `depends_on` | u2-main-catalogs (the CHANGELOG writer order and `test/docsPages.test.ts`), u2-apm-slim-package (both edit `renderReadme` in `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs`), u2-release-integrity (the root digests and `scripts/plugins/rootDigest.mjs`) |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/ci/promoteChannel.test.ts test/ci/distributionIdentity.test.ts test/ci/workflow.test.ts test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts && npm run lint && npm run typecheck` |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **files, added:** `docs/plugins.md` (`:224-225` only), `scripts/plugins/rootDigest.mjs` (read only; `u2-release-integrity`'s).
- **interfaces, added:** **Inbox rows 444 and 445.** `verify` declares `outputs: { ok: ${{ steps.plan.outputs.ok }}, commit: ${{ steps.plan.outputs.commit }} }`; its step `plan` runs the script with `--verify-only --json` under `set -o pipefail` into `plan.json` and writes `ok` and `commit` from it to `$GITHUB_OUTPUT` with one `node -e` line (a failed check exits 1, so the job fails and `promote` is skipped). `promote` checks out the repository itself at `${{ github.sha }}` (SHA-pinned `actions/checkout`, `persist-credentials: false`) and sets up Node (SHA-pinned `actions/setup-node`, the pins `release.yml` uses) before running the script with `--expect-commit "${{ needs.verify.outputs.commit }}"`; jobs share no workspace. A new `test/ci/workflow.test.ts` case over every workflow: each `needs.<job>.outputs.<name>` it references is declared in that job's `outputs`, and each job whose step runs `node scripts/…` has a checkout and a `setup-node` step before it. **Root digests (the brief's S4; this file's decision S18).** A check (5b) after the archive check: for a schema-2 `release.json` (`u2-release-integrity`), every `packages[]` entry's root at `<commit>` — `git ls-tree -r -z <commit> -- <path>`, refusing a `120000` or `160000` entry (`<path>: the root holds a symbolic link or a submodule`), blobs read with `git cat-file --batch` — hashes under sha256-tree-v1 (`rootDigest`, `scripts/plugins/rootDigest.mjs`) to `entry.rootSha256` (`<client> root: the tree at <tag> hashes to <x> and release.json says <y>.`); a schema-1 `release.json` → `<tag>'s release.json is schema 1, which records no root digest; channels start at the first schema-2 release.` (decision S18). **Rulesets restrict creation too.** Control 4 and the docs section ask for a ruleset on each channel branch with **Restrict creations**, **Restrict updates** and **Restrict deletions** and force pushes blocked, with "Deploy keys" as the only bypass ("Restrict creations" and "Restrict updates" are separate rules: updates do not cover creation, GitHub rulesets page, read 2026-10-06); a channel without that ruleset can be created or moved by any writer, and the docs name it as the unsupported case. The GH013 message drops its last alternative. **Cursor's team marketplace.** `docs/plugins.md:224-225` "pointed at your mirror of the distribution branch" → "pointed at your mirror of the stable channel branch (`plugin-stable` by default; `plugin-dist` moves to every release without a promotion)".
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** the fixture remote of this unit with one byte of one file under `cursor/` changed in the tag's tree (the manifest unchanged), **then** the plan has `ok: false`, exactly one problem naming `cursor`, and the remote's refs are byte-identical (`test/ci/promoteChannel.test.ts`). **Given** a tag whose `release.json` is schema 1, **then** one problem naming schema 1 and no ref moves. **Given** `promote.yml`, **then** `verify.outputs` declares `ok` and `commit`, `promote`'s `if:` reads `needs.verify.outputs.ok`, and `promote` has its own checkout and `setup-node` steps before the script (`test/ci/workflow.test.ts`). **Given** the workflow scan above over every file in `.github/workflows/`, **then** it finds no undeclared output read. **Given** `docs/plugins.md`, **then** the Cursor team-marketplace paragraph names the channel branch and not "the distribution branch" (`test/docsPages.test.ts`).
- **edgeCases, added:** A root that `git ls-tree` lists with an executable bit → hashed by content like any blob (the digest covers bytes and paths only). A first promotion under a creation-restricted ruleset without `STAMITY_PROMOTE_DEPLOY_KEY` → the push is refused and the GH013 message names the deploy key.

### u2-admin-templates — managed-settings templates per channel and per tag, for Claude Code and Copilot

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-admin-templates |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-036 (and, at Prove, the spec delta's MODIFIED REQ-PLUGIN-029) |
| `files` | `scripts/plugins/managed-settings.mjs` (whole module: `:1-37` header and key list, `:95-112` renderer; new renderer and `adminTemplates`), `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs` (header `:24-27`; `:77` `CLAUDE_MANAGED_SETTINGS`; `:244-249` `installNote`; the Copilot section of `clientRoutes` `:291-304`; `:561-570` the write), `test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts` (`:80-145` and new cases), `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts` (`:586-648`), `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (`## Roll the plugin out to your organization` `:1023-1086` only, plus the header line if `u2-channels-promote` has not moved it), `test/docsPages.test.ts` (`:2320-2337`), `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** `renderClaudeManagedSettings(identity, { ref, minimumVersion = MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION })` (`scripts/plugins/managed-settings.mjs:100-112`) returns `extraKnownMarketplaces: {<name>: {source: {source:'github', repo:<slug>, ref}}}`, `enabledPlugins: {"<name>@<name>": true}`, `strictKnownMarketplaces: [<same source>]`, `requiredMinimumVersion` (`MIN_CLAUDE_VERSION = '2.1.277'`, `:21`); `MANAGED_SETTINGS_KEYS` (`:24-37`) fixes that order; `REF_NAME` (`:44`) mirrors the identity's rule and is pinned to it (`test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts:197-205`). The builder writes only `admin/claude-managed-settings.json`, at the release tag, when Claude is built (`scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:561-570`), and the Claude README section names it (`:244-249`). **Vendor shapes (read 2026-10-01).** Claude: a managed `extraKnownMarketplaces` entry is `{ "source": {…}, "autoUpdate": true }` — `autoUpdate` sits beside `source`, and a managed value locks the user's toggle (code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/org, "Require a marketplace" and "Set update policy"); allowlist matching is exact on `repo`, `ref` and `path` (code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference); `requiredMinimumVersion` is managed-only, invalid values are ignored (code.claude.com/docs/en/managed-settings). Copilot (`.github-private` repository → `copilot/managed-settings.json` on its default branch; GA 2026-07-01; legacy `.github/copilot/settings.json` still read): `enabledPlugins {"PLUGIN@MARKETPLACE": bool}`; `extraKnownMarketplaces {<name>: {source, autoUpdate?}}` with `source` `{source:"github", repo:"OWNER/REPO", ref?, path?}`, `{source:"git", url, ref?, path?}` or `{source:"directory", path}`; `strictKnownMarketplaces` an array of source objects, `[]` = lockdown, ref matching undocumented; no `requiredMinimumVersion` key (docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/enterprise-administrators/enterprise-managed-settings). **New in `managed-settings.mjs`.** `renderClaudeManagedSettings(identity, { ref, autoUpdate = false, strict = false, sha, minimumVersion = managedFloor({ autoUpdate, strict, sha }) })` returns, in this key order: `extraKnownMarketplaces: {<name>: autoUpdate ? {source, autoUpdate: true} : {source}}`, `enabledPlugins`, `requiredMinimumVersion`, then `strictKnownMarketplaces: [source]` only when `strict` (the order the spec delta's REQ-PLUGIN-029 text fixes: the opt-in key last). `MANAGED_SETTINGS_KEYS` becomes `['extraKnownMarketplaces', 'enabledPlugins', 'requiredMinimumVersion', 'strictKnownMarketplaces']`. `autoUpdate` and `strict` must be booleans (`managed-settings: autoUpdate must be a boolean.`). New `renderCopilotManagedSettings(identity, { ref, autoUpdate = false, strict = false })` returns `extraKnownMarketplaces` (same entry shape), `enabledPlugins`, and `strictKnownMarketplaces` only when `strict`; `COPILOT_MANAGED_SETTINGS_KEYS = ['extraKnownMarketplaces', 'enabledPlugins', 'strictKnownMarketplaces']`; it never writes a key the Copilot reference does not document. New `adminTemplates(identity, { tag, clients }) → { path: string, document: object }[]`, pure: for each of `claude` and `copilot` that is in `clients`, in that order, for each variant in order `tag` (ref = `tag`, `autoUpdate: false`), `stable` (ref = `identity.distribution.channels.stable`, `autoUpdate: true`), `canary` (only when configured; ref = its branch, `autoUpdate: true`), it yields the plain file and then the strict one: `admin/<client>/managed-settings.<variant>.json` and `admin/<client>/managed-settings.<variant>.strict.json`. Eight files for the canonical identity (no canary), twelve with a canary. The opt-in is the admin's choice of the `.strict` file: no build switch and no config key, so the canonical tree serves organizations that want the allowlist and those that do not. **Builder:** `:561-570` writes every `adminTemplates(...)` document with `jsonDocument`; `CLAUDE_MANAGED_SETTINGS` (`:77`) is replaced by the function; the Claude `installNote` (`:247-249`) names `admin/claude/managed-settings.stable.json` and says "the `.strict` file of the same name also admits only this marketplace; `tag` pins this release"; the Copilot section gains an `installNote`: "An enterprise rolls this plugin out by committing `admin/copilot/managed-settings.stable.json` to its `.github-private` repository as `copilot/managed-settings.json` (see the fork guide, "Roll the plugin out to your organization")." The legacy path `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` stays for one compatibility window (decision S3 of file 2) as a byte copy of `admin/claude/managed-settings.tag.strict.json` (the same four keys, the allowlist now last), written by the builder beside `adminTemplates`'s output, and goes at the next major release. **Docs** `docs/enterprise-forks.md:1023-1086`: the JSON block becomes the Claude stable template rendered for this repository (`ref: "plugin-stable"`, `autoUpdate: true`, `requiredMinimumVersion`), the key list explains `autoUpdate` and says the allowlist is in the `.strict` files; the opening paragraph ("one file declares the marketplace, allows only that one, and turns the plugin on") no longer claims the allowlist by default. The rest of the section is file 3's (u3-rollout-guides). **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**The managed-settings templates follow a channel, and the allowlist is opt-in.** The distribution tree carries Claude Code and Copilot templates under `admin/claude/` and `admin/copilot/`, one per channel with `autoUpdate: true` and one pinned to the release tag, each with a `.strict` twin that adds `strictKnownMarketplaces`. `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` stays as a copy of `admin/claude/managed-settings.tag.strict.json` until the next major release." **Follows:** `scripts/plugins/managed-settings.mjs:100-112` (one closure builds the declared source and the allowlist entry, so they cannot differ). **Surface pins moved:** `test/docsPages.test.ts:2320-2337` (four keys → the keys the shown block's options produce). |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** the canonical identity, **when** `renderClaudeManagedSettings(identity, { ref: "plugin-stable", autoUpdate: true })` runs, **then** its keys are exactly `extraKnownMarketplaces`, `enabledPlugins`, `requiredMinimumVersion` in that order, the entry is `{source:{source:"github",repo:"zomarit/stamity",ref:"plugin-stable"},autoUpdate:true}` (key order `source`, `autoUpdate`), `requiredMinimumVersion` equals `managedFloor({ autoUpdate: true })` and the README names its reason, and there is no `strictKnownMarketplaces`. **Given** `strict: true` for each ref in `plugins/v1.9.0`, `plugin-stable`, `plugins/v2.0.0-rc.1`, **then** the last key is `strictKnownMarketplaces`, it holds exactly one entry, and that entry deep-equals the declared `source` (the property `test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts:101-115` holds today, kept). **Given** the tag variant, **then** `ref` is `plugins/v<version>` and the entry has no `autoUpdate` key. **Given** `renderCopilotManagedSettings(identity, { ref: "plugin-stable", autoUpdate: true })`, **then** its keys are `extraKnownMarketplaces`, `enabledPlugins` only, the entry's source is `{source:"github",repo,ref}`, and no key outside `COPILOT_MANAGED_SETTINGS_KEYS` appears; with `strict: true` the allowlist entry equals the declared source. **Given** the acme fork identity of `test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts:57-65`, **then** the strict tag variant's bytes equal the literal block at `:117-145` with `requiredMinimumVersion` moved above `strictKnownMarketplaces` (re-pinned with `TEST CHANGE, justified: the spec's REQ-PLUGIN-029 text puts the opt-in key last`). **Given** `adminTemplates` for the default identity and `clients` of all four, **then** it returns exactly the 8 paths `admin/claude/managed-settings.{tag,tag.strict,stable,stable.strict}.json` then the same 4 under `admin/copilot/`; with `channels.canary` configured, 12; with `clients: ["cursor"]`, none. **Given** a full distribution build, **then** every one of those files exists with bytes equal to `jsonDocument(document)`, `admin/claude-managed-settings.json` exists and equals `admin/claude/managed-settings.tag.strict.json` byte for byte, a second build with `--client claude,copilot` writes identical bytes for the files it writes, the leak gate over the `admin/` folder reports "0 hits", and a `--client cursor` build has no `admin/` (in `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`, replacing `:586-648`). **Given** the enterprise-forks rollout section, **then** its first `json` block equals `renderClaudeManagedSettings` for the canonical identity with the `ref` and `autoUpdate` read from the block (`test/docsPages.test.ts:2320-2337` re-pinned). |
| `edgeCases` | A channel template points at a branch that no promotion has created yet: the client cannot reach the marketplace; the README line says to promote before rolling a channel template out. An organization that serves a mirror edits `repo` in the declared source and, in a `.strict` file, in the allowlist entry together, as the guide already says (`docs/enterprise-forks.md:1078-1085`). A fork with a renamed plugin gets its own marketplace key and plugin id in every file (the renderer reads `identity.name`). Copilot's allowlist ref matching is undocumented: the `.strict` Copilot files carry the same source object, and the live check needs a Copilot Business or Enterprise organization, recorded `Not done:` in REQ-PLUGIN-044. Two marketplaces with one name cannot both be registered by a user (Claude, host-marketplace page); a user receives one channel's file, so the shared name `stamity` keeps the plugin id `stamity@stamity` stable when an admin moves a group between channels. |
| `depends_on` | u2-channels-promote (the resolved `channels`), u2-release-integrity (both edit the publish job's stamp and asset steps) |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts && npm run lint && npm run typecheck` |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **files, added:** `.github/workflows/release.yml` (a step after "Stamp the release manifest with the distribution commit" `:1015-1034`; the asset loop `:1066-1072`), `.github/workflows/fork-release.yml` (the same pair), `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`, `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`.
- **interfaces, added:** **Copilot, re-read 2026-10-06:** GA 2026-07-01 at `copilot/managed-settings.json` in the enterprise's `.github-private` repository, still reading `.github/copilot/settings.json`; `strictKnownMarketplaces` added 2026-06-25; covers VS Code and the Copilot CLI on Business and Enterprise; extended to the Copilot app and the cloud agent on 2026-07-27 (`vendor-facts.md`). The Copilot `github` source documents `repo`, `ref` and `path` and no `sha`, so no Copilot template carries one. **The Claude tag variant pins the commit.** `extraKnownMarketplaces.<name>` takes `sha` beside `source`, which "pins" and "skips updates even if `autoUpdate` is `true`" (https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference, read 2026-10-06). `renderClaudeManagedSettings` gains `sha` (40 lowercase hex, `managed-settings: sha must be a 40-character lowercase commit sha.`; with `autoUpdate: true` → `managed-settings: a sha pins the marketplace, so autoUpdate would never move it; pass one or the other.`), placed after `source` in the entry; the `.strict` twin's allowlist entry stays the `source` object alone. A tree cannot name its own commit, so the tree's tag templates carry no `sha`: a new step **"Stamp the Claude tag templates with the distribution commit"**, right after the manifest stamp in both workflows (`run` byte-equal; the fork's copy of the manifest stamp comes from file 1's `u1-npm-dist-tag`, r3 S3.1 — if it has not landed, this unit adds it too and the parity list names both), writes `plugins/managed-settings-claude-tag.json` and `plugins/managed-settings-claude-tag.strict.json` from the tree's two tag templates with `sha` = `PLUGINS_COMMIT`, and the asset loop (`release.yml:1066-1072`) attaches `plugins/managed-settings-*.json`. **Precedence and strict matching, said in the README and the docs section:** a managed entry replaces a project entry of the same name in Claude Code and the two do not merge (https://code.claude.com/docs/en/plugins/org, read 2026-10-06), so a repository's `--declare` of `stamity` at another ref is overridden where the organization manages `stamity`; a `.strict` file admits only its exact `repo`, `ref` and `path` (settings reference), so a project declaration of another ref is not admitted; Copilot's same-name precedence is undocumented, and the README says so. **The floor per variant.** `export const MANAGED_KEY_FLOORS = { strictKnownMarketplaces: { version: '2.1.277', reason: 'the first release that enforces an invalid strictKnownMarketplaces as an empty allowlist', citation }, autoUpdate: <read at execution>, sha: <read at execution> }` and `export function managedFloor({ autoUpdate, strict, sha })` = the highest floor among the keys the variant writes, starting from the Claude root's `CLIENT_FLOOR` (2.1.224, the archive-source floor, `scripts/plugins/clients/claude.mjs:126-130`); a key whose introducing release no page states keeps 2.1.277 with the reason "unstated; the template floor of 1.11.0". `renderClaudeManagedSettings`' `minimumVersion` defaults to `managedFloor(...)`. The README prints, per template, its `requiredMinimumVersion` and the reason, beside the root's 2.1.224 and its reason (brief C1: one release showed two floors with no reason).
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** `renderClaudeManagedSettings(identity, { ref: "plugins/v1.12.0", sha: "<40 hex>" })`, **then** the entry is `{ source: {…}, sha: "<40 hex>" }` in that key order and no `autoUpdate`; **given** `sha` with `autoUpdate: true`, **then** it throws naming both; **given** a malformed `sha`, **then** it throws (`test/ci/managedSettings.test.ts`). **Given** the stamp step's program extracted from `release.yml` and run over a fixture `plugins/admin/claude/` with a commit, **then** `managed-settings-claude-tag.json` deep-equals `renderClaudeManagedSettings(identity, { ref: tag, sha })` and the tree's own template is unchanged (`test/ci/workflow.test.ts`); **given** `fork-release.yml`, **then** the step's `run` equals `release.yml`'s (`test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`). **Given** `managedFloor` for each variant, **then** a `.strict` variant reads 2.1.277, and each other variant's value and reason equal the table's, each with a citation and an access date.
- **edgeCases, added:** An admin who rolls out the tree's tag template rather than the stamped asset → pinned by tag only; the README says the asset copy also pins the commit. A Claude Code older than the release that introduced entry-level `sha` → the floor keeps it from starting, rather than ignoring the pin silently.

detail: string } \| { kind: "read"; lock: ApmLock; refusedPaths: readonly string[] }`; `interface ApmLock { lockfileVersion: string \| null; apmVersion: string \| null; dependencies: readonly ApmLockDependency[] }`; `interface ApmLockDependency { repoUrl: string \| null; name: string \| null; packageType: string \| null; resolvedRef: string \| null; resolvedCommit: string \| null; deployedFiles: readonly string[] }` (every field `readonly`; repository-relative POSIX paths, files and folder claims as the lock lists them, unsafe entries in `refusedPaths`); `parseApmLock(text)` (pure, never throws), `readApmLock(rootDir)`, `apmDeployedPaths(read)` (the union of every dependency's `deployedFiles`). **This unit's additive extension to that module** (decision S14; file 1's fields, read states and functions keep their shapes, and its test cases pass unedited): `ApmLockDependency` gains `readonly virtualPath: string \| null` and `readonly version: string \| null`, read from the dependency row's `virtual_path` and `version` keys the way file 1 reads its other scalars (another type → `null`); when a dependency carries no `deployed_files`, `parseApmLock` fills its `deployedFiles` from the `value` of the top-level `deployments[]` rows (`{ kind, target, value, runtime, scope, owners, active_owner, content_hash }`) the lock attributes to it through `active_owner`/`owners`, spelled as the current client's lock spells them (0.33.0, measured 2026-10-06: a local dependency's rows name its absolute `local_path`; the unit transcribes a remote-ref lock as a second fixture at intake), each value through file 1's path rules (unsafe → `refusedPaths`). apm-cli 0.32.0 and 0.33.0 write `deployed_files` as a legacy view kept "one cycle" (`apm_cli/core/deployment_ledger.py:279-335` in 0.33.0); probe 2026-10-01 shows both present, plus `name` and `version` on each dependency row. **Current.** `InstallMode = "generated" \| "plugin-backed"`, stored at `manifest.plugin.mode` (`src/types/manifest.ts:228-231`, `:293-298`), validated by `collectEnumError` (`src/manifest/manifest.ts:570-573`); The duplicates row passes outright when no client records a plugin (`src/cli/commands/check.ts:996-999`); its `apm` source matches `apm.yml` dependency lines by the package's two identities and swallows a YAML error (`src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:605-686`; inbox rows `.stamity/inbox.md:67`, `:68`). APM 0.32.0 deploys to the same paths the CLI writes (`.claude/agents/<id>.md`, `.github/prompts/<id>.prompt.md`, …, `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:109-132`, re-observed by the probe) and skips a path it does not manage ("Skipping <path> — local file exists (not managed by APM). `MANIFEST_VERSION` does not move (an additive value on the `plugin` field's own precedent, `src/types/manifest.ts:373-382`; an engine at 1.11.0 refuses the unknown value at `collectEnumError`, so a downgrade fails loudly). `collectPluginErrors` refuses `clients` under `apm-backed` ("`plugin.clients` is not used under `apm-backed`; the targets in apm.yml decide which clients receive APM's classes"). `pluginOwnedClasses`: `if (readInstallMode(manifest) === "apm-backed") return new Set(APM_DELIVERED_CLASSES[tool]);` before today's logic. **New, `src/apm/layout.ts`** (pure, imports `src/types/core.ts` only, `PLAN_MAP` wave 1): `export const APM_DELIVERED_CLASSES: Readonly<Record<Tool, readonly ("agent" \| "skill" \| "command" \| "rule")[]>> = { claude: ["agent", "skill", "command", "rule"], copilot: ["agent", "skill", "command", "rule"], cursor: ["agent", "skill", "command", "rule"], codex: ["agent", "skill"] }` (APM's codex profile carries no command class and compiles instructions only on `apm compile`); `export const APM_CHARTER_ID = "stamity-charter"`; `export function classifyApmPath(path: string): { cls: "agent" \| "skill" \| "command" \| "rule" \| "charter"; id: string; readers: readonly Tool[] } \| null` over the table `.claude/agents/<id>.md` agent/claude, `.claude/commands/<id>.md` command/claude, `.claude/rules/<id>.md` rule/claude, `.claude/skills/<id>/…` skill/claude, `.github/agents/<id>.agent.md` agent/copilot, `.github/prompts/<id>.prompt.md` command/copilot, `.github/instructions/<id>.instructions.md` rule/copilot, `.cursor/agents/<id>.md` agent/cursor, `.cursor/commands/<id>.md` command/cursor, `.cursor/rules/<id>.mdc` rule/cursor, `.codex/agents/<id>.toml` agent/codex, `.agents/skills/<id>/…` skill/[cursor, copilot, codex]; a rule whose id is `APM_CHARTER_ID` is class `charter`; anything else → `null`. A test holds the table equal to `TARGET_LAYOUT` in `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:109-132` (the smoke stays dependency-free, so the two are twins held equal, as `DEFAULT_CLI_PACKAGE_NAME` is, `src/cli/kit/packageName.ts:31-42`). **New, `src/apm/deliveries.ts`** (pure, wave 3): `export interface ApmDelivery { tool: Tool; id: string; cls: string; apmPaths: string[]; cliPaths: string[]; dependency: string }`; `export function findDeliveredTwice({ tools, ledger, dependencies }): ApmDelivery[]` — the CLI delivers a ledger row to its own `adapter` (a row with `artifactId === "charter"` is `(charter, APM_CHARTER_ID)`, otherwise `classifyApmPath(row.path)`); APM delivers a deployed path to every selected tool among its readers; a finding is a selected tool and an id both deliver; `dependency` is `name`, else `repoUrl` plus `"/" + virtualPath` when `virtualPath` is set, else `"an unnamed APM dependency"` (file 1 reads a missing `repo_url` as `null`); `export function apmCoverageGaps({ tools, dependencies, isPackage }): { tool: Tool; missing: string[] }[]` — under `apm-backed`, a selected tool and a class of `APM_DELIVERED_CLASSES[tool]` that no deployed path of a dependency `isPackage` accepts delivers; `export function isPackageDependency(dep, { repositorySlug, packageId, shippedIds }): boolean` — `repoUrl` normalized equals the slug (case-insensitive; a `null` `repoUrl` never does), or `name` is `packageId` or `<packageId>-charter`, or a deployed path classifies to an id the corpus ships (u2-apm-floor-current reads it). **New, emission.** `src/emit/ownership.ts` `export function projectionRowOwners(manifest, readers, row: { artifactType: string; artifactId: string }, apmRuleSkillIds: ReadonlySet<string>): Tool[]` — under `apm-backed`: `readers` minus each tool whose `APM_DELIVERED_CLASSES` has `skill` when the row is a `skill` row or a `rule` row whose id is in `apmRuleSkillIds`; otherwise exactly `sharedProjectionOwners(manifest, readers)`. `src/emit/planner.ts:851-865`: `owners = file.origin === "pack" ? projectionReaders : projectionRowOwners(ctx.manifest, projectionReaders, file, apmRuleSkillIds)`, where `apmRuleSkillIds` is empty outside `apm-backed` and otherwise `demotedRuleIds("copilot", <reachable rule items>.map(ruleDeliveryInputOf), RULE_DELIVERY_DEFAULT)` — the predicate the APM generator ships its rule-skills by (`scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:485-489`), so codex keeps its other demoted rules as `.agents/skills/stamity-<id>/` (APM's codex profile delivers no rules) and drops the two APM ships as skills. Net effect, tools all four under `apm-backed`: the CLI writes `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.override.md`, `.claude/settings.json`, the review gate, the hook configuration and scripts of all four clients, MCP documents, `.codex/config.toml`, codex's nine touchpoints (`.agents/skills/st-<id>/`, APM delivers codex no commands) and its rule-skills outside the APM set, and `.stamity/` state; no file of the four classes for claude, copilot or cursor, and no `.codex/agents/`. **New, entry and switch.** `init --install-mode <mode>` (`new Option(…).choices(["generated", "apm-backed"])`, default `generated`) passes `plugin: { mode: "apm-backed" }` to `applyInit` and prints "install mode apm-backed — APM delivers agents, skills, commands and rules; this setup writes the charter, hooks, MCP wiring and state". `config set installMode <generated \| apm-backed>` (a `KEY_SPECS` row: `choices: ["generated", "apm-backed"]`, `read: manifest.plugin?.mode ?? null`, `resolve: readInstallMode`, `apply` sets `draft.plugin = raw === "apm-backed" ? { mode: "apm-backed" } : undefined` and refuses when the current mode is `plugin-backed`: "this setup is plugin-backed; run clean, then init"). Order for a generated repository: `config set installMode apm-backed`, `sync` (reclaims the CLI's copies), then `apm install`, then `check`. The refusal remedy u1-foreign-paths prints names `init --install-mode apm-backed`. **New, the duplicates row** (`plugin-duplicates`, `src/cli/commands/check.ts:991-1015`; the id stays — `check --json` consumers and `docs/troubleshooting.md` key on it): it runs in every mode. Inputs: `readApmLock`, `apm.yml` parsed with `yaml`. Findings: (1) today's plugin findings for recorded clients, severity unchanged (`warn` under `generated`, `fail` under `plugin-backed` — the spec delta's suggested default (a) for REQ-PLUGIN-019); their `apm` source now reads the lock when it is readable — a recorded client's carried class (`agent`, `skill`, `command`) delivered by any lock path → finding, so `acme/stamity-mirror/apm#plugins/v1.12.0` is found (inbox `:67`) — and falls back to today's `apm.yml` line match only when no lock exists; (2) `findDeliveredTwice` findings, `fail` in every mode, one line each: `<tool>: <id> (<cls>) arrives twice — APM <dependency> at <apmPaths>, this setup at <cliPaths> — <remedy>`; remedy under `generated`: "switch with `<packageCommand("config set installMode apm-backed")>` then `<packageCommand("sync")>`, or remove <dependency> from apm.yml and run apm install"; under `apm-backed`: "remove <dependency> from apm.yml and run apm install; this setup writes <cls>"; for the charter: "remove the charter package from apm.yml; this setup writes the charter"; (3) an unparseable `apm.yml` or an unreadable lock, `fail` in every mode, naming the file and the first line of the error (inbox `:68`); (4) under `apm-backed`: no lock → `fail` "apm-backed, but apm.lock.yaml is absent: run apm install"; each `apmCoverageGaps` row → `fail` "<tool>: APM delivers no <classes>; add <tool> to the targets in apm.yml and run apm install". The row's status is the worst finding's. With no plugin record, no `apm.yml`, no lock, no APM sidecar and no plugin declaration, the detail stays byte-identical: "no client records a plugin, so nothing can duplicate". **New, the rest.** `pluginOwnedLines` prints `apm-owned` instead of `plugin-owned` under `apm-backed` (the JSON `pluginOwned` keeps its shape). `clean` under `apm-backed` closes with "APM's files stay: remove the dependency from apm.yml and run apm install". **Boundaries:** `PLAN_MAP` rows `"src/apm/layout.ts": { unit: "u2-apm-backed-mode", wave: 1 }` and `"src/apm/deliveries.ts": { unit: "u2-apm-backed-mode", wave: 3 }` (it imports `layout.ts` and the ledger and lock types; wave 3 sits above file 1’s wave-2 `src/detect/apmLock.ts`, another unit’s module, and below its importers `plugin/probe.ts`, wave 14, and `check.ts`, wave 15). **Docs:** `docs/troubleshooting.md`'s `plugin-duplicates` row says it runs in every mode, names the APM findings and their remedies, and that an unparseable `apm.yml` or lock fails; `docs/getting-started.md` gains "APM and the CLI together" (the mode, the two commands, the order). **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**An `apm-backed` install mode.** A repository that installs the APM package and also runs the CLI sets it with `init --install-mode apm-backed` or `config set installMode apm-backed`; the CLI then writes only what APM does not carry — the charter, hooks, MCP wiring and state, and Codex's touchpoints and rules — and `check` fails, in every mode, any agent, skill, command, rule or charter that reaches a client from both the CLI and an APM dependency, read from `apm.lock.yaml`." **Follows:** `src/manifest/manifest.ts:1155-1182` (one resolver decides what the recorded mode means) and `src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:822-869` (`collectPluginDuplicates`: repository-level reads once, then pure per-client answers) |

**given** the same with `clients`, **then** validation names `plugin.clients`. **Given** `parseApmLock` over the transcribed 0.33.0 lock with only its `deployments[]` rows and over the same lock with `deployed_files`, **then** `apmDeployedPaths` is equal for both and each dependency's `virtualPath` and `version` read as the lock writes them (`null` when absent); under `generated` and `plugin-backed`, **then** results equal today's (`test/emit/ownership.test.ts`, every branch of `projectionRowOwners`, the `src/emit/` 100% floor at `vitest.config.ts:123-128`). **Given** tools `[claude, copilot]`, mode `apm-backed` and a lock deploying the package, **when** emission is planned (`test/emit/apmBacked.test.ts`), **then** the plan writes `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/settings.json`, `.github/hooks/stamity.json`, the hook scripts and `.stamity/` state, and 0 paths under `.claude/{agents,commands,rules,skills}/`, `.github/{agents,prompts,instructions}/` and `.agents/skills/`. **Given** tools all four under `apm-backed`, **then** `.agents/skills/st-work/SKILL.md`, `.agents/skills/st-work/agents/openai.yaml` and `.agents/skills/stamity-testing/SKILL.md` are planned and owned by codex alone, and `.agents/skills/stamity-ai-evals/`, `.agents/skills/stamity-question-protocol/` and `.codex/agents/` are not planned. **Given** `findDeliveredTwice` over a cursor ledger row `.agents/skills/st-work/SKILL.md` and a lock path `.cursor/commands/st-work.md`, **then** one finding `{ tool: "cursor", id: "st-work" }`; **given** the `apm-backed` plan's ledger and the package's lock, **then** none; **given** a lock path `.claude/rules/stamity-charter.md` and a claude charter ledger row, **then** one `charter` finding. **Given** mode `generated`, tools `[claude, cursor]` and that lock, **when** `check` runs, **then** it exits 1 and the `plugin-duplicates` detail names `cursor`, `st-work` and the source `apm`; **given** mode `apm-backed`, **then** the row passes and `check` exits 0. **Given** an `apm.yml` that does not parse, or a lock that does not, **then** `check` exits 1 and the row names that file. **Given** a lock whose dependency is `acme/stamity-mirror` deploying `.claude/agents/stamity-reviewer.md` and a claude plugin record carrying `agent`, **then** the row reports source `apm` for claude. **Given** mode `apm-backed`, tools `[claude, cursor]` and a lock deploying only `.claude/**`, **then** the row fails "cursor: APM delivers no agent, skill, command, rule". **Given** no plugin record, no `apm.yml`, no lock, no APM sidecar and no plugin declaration, **then** the row's detail equals "no client records a plugin, so nothing can duplicate" byte for byte. **Given** `init -y --tools claude --install-mode apm-backed` in a fixture holding a lock, **then** exit 0 and `.stamity/manifest.json` records `plugin.mode` `apm-backed`; A lock `.agents/skills/<id>` directory entry beside its files → classified once. Ledger and lock paths are compared as POSIX strings, never through `node:path` (the Windows leg; `.stamity/learnings/the-local-test-gate-is-weaker-than-ci.md`). A tool deselected later → coverage reads the selected tools only. `apm-backed` with codex plus cursor or copilot → codex's touchpoints in `.agents/skills/` are also listed by cursor and copilot next to APM's commands: a cross-read, not a second route, so the row stays green and the capability matrix's residue section names it (Concerns C8). `plugin setup` on an `apm-backed` repository → today's refusal on an existing manifest. |

- **files, added:** `src/content/ruleDelivery.ts` (new export beside `demotedRuleIds` `:143-164`);

  (the `plugin-duplicates` row `:107`).

  `project-relative`, `target-relative`, `uri` (`apm_cli/core/deployment_state.py:16-21`; rows at
  `core/deployment_ledger.py:656-673`): MCP servers are `kind: uri`, `target: mcp`, `value: <server>`, owned by `.`; a
  `target-relative` value is relative to a user-scope root. So the fallback reads only rows with `kind:
  project-relative` and `scope: project`, and attributes a row to the dependency whose unique key (`repo_url`, plus
  `/virtual_path`) or `local_path` equals `active_owner` or appears in `owners` (M1: a local dependency's rows name its
  absolute `local_path`); rows owned by `.` are the consumer's own. `ApmLockDependency` also gains `readonly localPath:
  string \| null` (`local_path`); `ApmLock` gains `readonly mcpTargetServers: Readonly<Record<string, readonly
  string[]>>` (top-level `mcp_target_servers`; absent → the `uri`/`mcp` rows grouped by `runtime`) and `readonly
  mcpProvenance: Readonly<Record<string, string>>` (`mcp_config_provenance`; a list value joined with `, `,
  `deps/lockfile.py:160-167`). Same additive rule as S14: file 1's fields, read states and test cases do not move.
  **(2) Hook placements, read from APM's sidecar.** `src/apm/deliveries.ts` gains `export const APM_HOOK_SIDECAR =
  "apm-hooks.json"`, `export const SHARED_HOOK_FILES = { claude: ".claude/settings.json", cursor: ".cursor/hooks.json",
  codex: ".codex/hooks.json" } as const` (the sidecar sits in the same folder) and `export function
  apmHookPlacements(input: { tool: "claude" \| "cursor" \| "codex"; file: string; configText: string \| null;
  sidecarText: string \| null }): { placements: ApmHookPlacement[]; unreadable: string \| null }` with
  `ApmHookPlacement = { tool; file; event: string; source: string; summary: string; present: boolean }` — each sidecar
  entry under an event is matched to one native entry of `hooks.<event>` by APM's own content key (the entry without
  `_apm_source`, JSON with keys sorted at every depth; one native entry per sidecar entry,
  `apm_cli/integration/hook_ownership.py:18-45`); `summary` = the matcher (or `*`) and the first word of the first
  command, at most 60 characters; `present: false` when nothing matches. It also gains `export const APM_MCP_DOCUMENTS =
  { claude: { tool: "claude", document: ".mcp.json" }, cursor: { tool: "cursor", document: ".cursor/mcp.json" }, codex:
  { tool: "codex", document: ".codex/config.toml" }, copilot: { tool: "copilot", document: ".github/mcp.json" } }` (the
  lock's runtime names, M1 and M3; another runtime is kept with `tool` and `document` `null`), `export interface
  ApmMcpPlacement { runtime: string; tool: Tool \| null; document: string \| null; server: string; source: string }` and
  `export function apmMcpPlacements(lock: ApmLock): ApmMcpPlacement[]` — one row per runtime and server of
  `lock.mcpTargetServers`, `source` = `lock.mcpProvenance[server]`, else `"apm.yml"` (the consumer declared it itself).
  **(3) The duplicates row widens to hooks, MCP and plugin declarations (REQ-APM-018).** `findDeliveredTwice` takes `hooks?: { placements: readonly
  ApmHookPlacement[]; engineEntryKeys: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlySet<string>> }` (file → content keys of the entries

  selected: readonly string[] }`, and returns, beside
  today's findings, `{ cls: "hook", id: <event>, tool, apmPaths: [file], cliPaths: [file], dependency: source }` for a
  placement whose command runs a script under `.stamity/generated/hooks/` or whose content key is an engine entry's in
  the same file, and `{ cls: "mcp", id: <server>, tool, apmPaths: [APM's document], cliPaths: [this setup's document,
  `src/mcp/emit.ts:174-180`] }` for a placement whose server `manifest.mcp?.servers` also selects for that tool; both
  `fail` in every mode (S6's `apm` rule); remedies "stop that package delivering hooks (apm deny <source>) or remove it
