  `collectPluginDuplicates` (`src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:891`) reads the three hook files and sidecars (regular
  files only) and the lock once. **Declarations (brief C14; r1 measured `check` passing on fx `c14`):** `export function
  pluginDeclarationsIn(doc: unknown, identity: { pluginId: string; repositorySlug: string \| null }): string[]` in
  `probe.ts` returns the matched key paths — an `enabledPlugins` key `<pluginId>@<marketplace>` with a truthy value,
  and an `extraKnownMarketplaces` entry whose key is `pluginId` or whose `source.repo` equals `repositorySlug`

### u2-apm-floor-current — test the 0.29.1 floor and the current client, and fail `check` below the floor

| Field | Content |
|---|---|

### u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode — every hook decides from its own payload, and the Copilot root loads in VS Code

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-040 |
| `files` | `scripts/qa/hook-payload-probe.mjs` (new), `test/fixtures/hooks/payloads/` (new: one scrubbed payload file per client and event, `index.json`, `README.md`), `src/hooks/model.ts` (new tables after `:190`), `src/hooks/portableRunner.ts` (`buildPortableHookRunner` `:118-209`), `src/hooks/scripts.ts` (a new generated snippet beside `READ_STDIN` `:604-623`, used by the four Claude-layout scripts at their payload read: `:1073`, `:1702`, `:1925`, `:2362`), `scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs` (`renderReadme` `:267-269`), `test/hooks/portableRunner.test.ts`, `test/hooks/scripts.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts` (`:550`), `test/corpus/__snapshots__/emissionGoldens.test.ts.snap` (regenerated), `.stamity/generated/hooks/claude/*` (the dogfood, regenerated by `sync`), `CHANGELOG.md`; the executing run's record (`hook-measurement.md`, `vscode-load.md`) through plan 014's records route |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** Core hook rows carry no matcher (`src/emit/hooksInfra.ts:450-465`); only user and pack hooks may (`:485-491`, `src/hooks/userHooks.ts:351-368`). Copilot's file keys PascalCase event names under `version: 1` with each row's `matcher` (`src/adapters/copilot.ts:925-934`); the runner it launches reads `row.matcher` nowhere (`src/hooks/portableRunner.ts:138-209`). VS Code's Local hooks ignore matchers ("Local parses nested commands but ignores matcher values, so every command for the event runs") and read `.github/hooks/*.json` in a native form (PascalCase, no `version`), a Copilot form (numeric `version`, camelCase) and `.claude/settings*.json` only with `chat.useClaudeHooks`; the Copilot session target runs "the same SDK hook implementation as Copilot CLI" (code.visualstudio.com/docs/agent-customization/hooks and its reference, approved 2026-09-30). Two other clients run this repository's `.claude/settings.json` hooks beside their own: Cursor loads them by default ("Include Third-Party Plugins, Skills, and Other Configs … on by default", "All matching hooks from every source run", Claude `PreToolUse`/`Stop`/`SubagentStop`/`SessionStart` mapped to Cursor events, cursor.com/docs/reference/third-party-hooks), and the Copilot CLI reads `.claude/settings.json` hooks as a repository source (docs.github.com hooks reference; CLI changelog 1.0.12). So in a repository set up for Claude plus Cursor or Copilot, the Claude guard, session-start, tamper-notice and review-gate scripts also run inside the other client, on that client's payload. Documented payload keys: Cursor's common fields include `cursor_version`, `conversation_id`, `workspace_roots` (cursor.com/docs/hooks); Copilot's both formats carry `timestamp` (docs.github.com hooks reference); Claude's common fields carry neither (code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks). The Copilot root README says "VS Code is not on it (read 2026-09-20)" (`scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:267-269`, pinned at `test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts:550`). VS Code is not installed on the maintainer's machine today (`/usr/local/bin/code` resolves into `Cursor.app`). **New harness `scripts/qa/hook-payload-probe.mjs`** (follows `scripts/qa/hook-runs.mjs:1-60`: one fixture per client from `createFixture`, the hook's own JSONL as the instrument, a missing client is `not-run` with its reason, every quoted line through `scripts/qa/redact.mjs`). Usage: `node scripts/qa/hook-payload-probe.mjs --client <claude\|copilot\|cursor\|codex\|vscode> [--mode observe\|block-once] [--out <dir>]`. It writes into a scratch fixture a `probe.mjs` that appends `{ label, receivedAt, stdin, envNames }` (`envNames` = sorted environment variable NAMES, never values) to `hook-events.jsonl` and exits 0 silently; in `block-once` it holds the first completion event once with the client's documented continue shape (`{"decision":"block","reason":"Reply with exactly HOOK-CONTINUED"}` for Claude, Codex and Copilot; `{"followup_message":"Reply with exactly HOOK-CONTINUED"}` for Cursor) and records whether a second completion event and the marker followed. It registers the probe on every event both this unit and `u2-review-gate-all` read: SessionStart, PreToolUse, Stop/agentStop/stop, SubagentStop/subagentStop, TaskCompleted (Claude), SessionEnd; plus two variants measuring the cross-client imports — a Cursor fixture with only `.claude/settings.json`, and a Copilot fixture with `.github/hooks/` plus `.claude/settings.json`. Commands (flags read from `--help` on this machine): `claude -p "<prompt>" --output-format stream-json --verbose --include-hook-events --no-session-persistence` (once with `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TODO_TOOLS=1`); `COPILOT_ALLOW_ALL=true copilot -p "<prompt>" --allow-all-tools --no-auto-update`; `agent --trust -p "<prompt>" --output-format stream-json`; Codex and VS Code are maintainer steps (below). The prompt asks the agent to delegate one README read to a project subagent named `probe` whose scripted final text is `verdict: approve` / `confidence: high`, then reply done. **Committed fixtures** `test/fixtures/hooks/payloads/<client>/<event>.json`: one payload per client and event, with the values of `session_id`, `sessionId`, `conversation_id`, `generation_id`, `prompt_id`, `turn_id`, `agent_id`, `agentId`, `transcript_path`, `transcriptPath`, `agent_transcript_path`, `cwd`, `workspace_roots`, `user_email`, `model`, `model_id`, `scratchpad_dir` and `timestamp` replaced by `"<redacted:string>"`, and any free-text field kept only when it equals the scripted reply; `index.json` records per client the version, the date, the command, and per event `fired`, `continuedAfterHold` and the key list; the README carries each file's `shasum -a 256` (the convention of `test/fixtures/plugins/README.md:1-20`). **Maintainer steps, recorded with their outcome:** Codex — add `[projects."<fixture absolute path>"] trust_level = "trusted"` to `~/.codex/config.toml`, run `codex exec --dangerously-bypass-hook-trust --json "<prompt>"` in the fixture, then remove the entry (the trust confound of `.stamity/learnings/codex-hooks-default-on-and-codex-exec-ran-none.md`); VS Code — install the current stable VS Code and GitHub Copilot Chat, open the VS Code fixture with the VS Code binary (not `code`), trust it, and send the prompt once with Session Target "Local" and once with "Copilot". A step not performed is `Not done:` with the maintainer as owner, never a pass. **New tables in `src/hooks/model.ts`:** `export const FOREIGN_INVOCATION_SIGNATURES: readonly { client: Tool; payloadKey?: string; envName?: string; citation: { url: string; accessDate: string }; measured: string }[]` — seeded with the Cursor row `{ client: "cursor", payloadKey: "cursor_version", citation: { url: "https://cursor.com/docs/hooks", accessDate: "2026-10-06" } }` only when the captured Cursor payloads — its own hooks and the imported `.claude/settings.json` ones — all carry the key and, for Copilot, the key or environment name the fixtures show in every Copilot payload and in no Claude payload (none chosen without that evidence); and `export const HOOK_EVENT_ALIASES: Readonly<Record<CanonicalHookEvent, readonly string[]>>` — each canonical event's wire names across the four clients (e.g. `stop`: `Stop`, `agentStop`, `stop`; `user_prompt_submit`: `UserPromptSubmit`, `userPromptSubmitted`, `beforeSubmitPrompt`), cited per name. **Runner self-filter** (`buildPortableHookRunner`, after `input` is built at `:162`): (a) when `row.matcher` is set and `row.event` is `pre_tool_use` or `post_tool_use`, the runner tests `new RegExp("^(?:" + row.matcher + ")$", "i")` against `input.tool_name` (`*` and the empty string match every tool); no match → exit 0 with no stdout, except Cursor `pre_tool_use`, which writes `{"permission":"allow"}` as the runner already does for a silent child (`:209`); an invalid matcher regex → the runner's own fault path (`safeError("Invalid hook matcher")`); (b) when the payload names its own event (`payload.hook_event_name` or `payload.hookEventName`, read before the runner overwrites it at `:162`) and that name is not in `HOOK_EVENT_ALIASES[row.event]`, exit 0 with no stdout. **Claude-script self-filter** (`src/hooks/scripts.ts`): a generated snippet `FOREIGN_INVOCATION` beside `READ_STDIN` defines `invokedByAnotherClient(payload)` over the JSON-embedded `FOREIGN_INVOCATION_SIGNATURES`; each of the four Claude-layout scripts calls it right after `readPayload()` and, on a match whose client is in `SELECTED_TOOLS`, writes `stamity: skipped — <client> runs its own copy of this hook` to stderr and exits 0 without deciding; a match whose client is not selected runs the script as today; the snippet is emitted only for `tool === "claude"` (the one client whose configuration other clients import). **README** `scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:267-269` becomes: "VS Code loads this root as an agent plugin (Agent Plugins 1.0, generally available 2026-08-12; `chat.plugins.enabled`, on by default since 1.116) and also picks up a Copilot CLI install from `~/.copilot/installed-plugins/`; its skills appear as `/stamity:<id>`. VS Code's Local hooks ignore matchers, so every hook here decides from its own payload (loaded on VS Code <version>, <date>)." — version and date from the walk, or "not walked; see the route proofs" if the walk is `Not done:`. **VS Code walk (`vscode-load.md`):** build the distribution (`node scripts/build-plugin-runtime.mjs` then `node scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs --out dist/plugins --runtime <runtime>`), install the Copilot root through `copilot plugin marketplace add ./dist/plugins` and `copilot plugin install stamity@stamity`, open a plugin-backed fixture in VS Code, and record: the Agent Plugins view lists stamity at the version; Configure Skills lists `/stamity:st-learn`; the session-start hook ran; in a repository-mode fixture a user hook with matcher `Edit` wrote no line for a read tool call; and which catalog VS Code reads when `zomarit/stamity` is added with no ref (the observation REQ-PLUGIN-034 leaves to execution). **CHANGELOG** `### Fixed`: "**Hooks decide from their own payload.** A hook with a matcher no longer runs on every tool where the client ignores matchers (VS Code), and the Claude hooks stop acting when Cursor or the Copilot CLI runs them from `.claude/settings.json` beside their own." **Follows:** `scripts/qa/hook-runs.mjs:1-60` (harness posture), `test/fixtures/plugins/README.md:1-20` (vendored facts with digests), `src/hooks/model.ts:92-121` (guarantees as cited data). **Surface pins moved:** none. **Regenerate in the same change:** `npx vitest run test/corpus/emissionGoldens.test.ts --update` (the file first, `--update` last — learning `vitest-update-flag-takes-an-optional-value`) and, for the dogfood, `npm run build && node dist/cli.js sync`. |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** the runner built for copilot and a row `{event:"pre_tool_use", matcher:"Edit\|Write", command:[node, child]}`, **when** it receives a payload with `toolName: "bash"`, **then** it exits 0, writes nothing to stdout and never spawns the child (the child's marker file is absent); **with** `toolName: "edit"`, **then** the child runs once (in `test/hooks/portableRunner.test.ts`). **Given** the cursor runner and a non-matching `pre_tool_use` payload, **then** stdout is exactly `{"permission":"allow"}`. **Given** a `stop` row and a payload whose `hook_event_name` is `PreToolUse`, **then** exit 0 and no stdout. **Given** each captured Cursor fixture and each captured Copilot-import fixture, **when** the four generated Claude-layout scripts run on it, **then** each exits 0 with nothing on stdout and the stderr line names the client; **given** each captured Claude fixture, **then** no script reports itself skipped and the guard and gate behave exactly as before (in `test/hooks/scripts.test.ts`). **Given** every captured Claude payload, **then** none carries a `payloadKey` and the capturing environment named none of the `envName`s in `FOREIGN_INVOCATION_SIGNATURES` (the table cannot disarm Claude's own hooks on the evidence held). **Given** `test/fixtures/hooks/payloads/README.md`, **then** every listed digest equals `shasum -a 256` of its file. **Given** the Copilot root's `com.github.copilot/hooks/hooks.json`, **then** every key is in VS Code's documented event list (SessionStart, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PostToolUse, PreCompact, SubagentStart, SubagentStop, Stop). **Given** the Copilot root README, **then** it no longer matches `/VS\s*Code is not on it/` (`test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts:550` re-pinned with `TEST CHANGE, justified:`). `judgment: maintainer` · **Given** VS Code with the root installed, **when** a session starts and a tool runs, **then** the record names the VS Code version, the session target, the listed skills, the session-start hook line and the filtered matcher hook; a walk not performed is `Not done:` with its owner. |
| `edgeCases` | No Copilot signature with evidence → no Copilot row; the Claude hooks may still run twice under the Copilot CLI, and the record states whether the CLI rejected the imported block (its inline hooks are "strict: any item-level validation error rejects the whole hooks field" and the block carries `TaskCompleted` and `ConfigChange`). A user hook matcher written in Claude tool names (`Bash`) never matches VS Code's own tool names: the hook does not run there, which is what its matcher asked for; the docs (file 3) say matchers are client vocabulary. A payload that is not an object or has no tool name: the runner's existing fault and empty-payload paths apply before the filter. The fixtures never hold a value of an identity field (the redaction list above) — a captured line with an unredacted email or path fails the probe before it writes. Windows: the runner change is plain JavaScript inside the generated script; the probe harness runs on the maintainer's machine only and is not part of the suite. |
| `depends_on` | u2-skills-bundled-runtime (both edit `test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts`), u2-repo-file-waste (the adapters, `src/emit/skillsProjection.ts`, the golden snapshot and the capability matrix) |
| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/hooks/portableRunner.test.ts test/hooks/scripts.test.ts test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts test/corpus/emissionGoldens.test.ts && npm run build && node dist/cli.js check && npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test` |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **interfaces, added:** **Vendor-confirmed double run.** Cursor runs every matching hook from every source, in the order Enterprise, Team, `.cursor/hooks.json`, user, `.claude/settings.local.json`, `.claude/settings.json`, `~/.claude/settings.json` (https://cursor.com/docs/reference/third-party-hooks, read 2026-10-06), so a Claude-plus-Cursor repository fires each stamity hook twice in Cursor: once from Cursor's own wiring and once from the Claude wiring. **The skip, scoped:** a Claude-layout script invoked by another client exits 0 without acting only when that client is among the manifest's selected tools — its own wiring runs the same hook; the generated snippet embeds the selection at sync as `SELECTED_TOOLS` (the Claude scripts re-render when the selection changes); a foreign client that is not selected runs the hook as today, because the import is then the only stamity hook it has. The stderr line stays `stamity: skipped — <client> runs its own copy of this hook`. **Signature first:** the probe's Cursor variants become two — a fixture with only `.claude/settings.json`, and a Claude-plus-Cursor fixture (`init --tools claude,cursor`) — and each records, per event, how many times each stamity hook ran and the payload keys and environment names of every run; `index.json` adds per client `claudeProjectDirSet: boolean` (r3 C9.8: whether Cursor or the Copilot CLI exports `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` to an imported hook). The seeded `cursor_version` row stays only if every captured Cursor payload, its own and the imported ones, carries the key and no captured Claude payload does — the file's evidence rule, unchanged; when Cursor hands an imported Claude hook a Claude-shaped payload with no Cursor key, the environment names decide, and with no signature at all the self-filter is not built for Cursor and the double run is a capability-matrix residue row.
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** the Claude-plus-Cursor probe record, **then** it names, per event, the run count of each stamity hook and the client version. **Given** a captured Cursor payload and a generated Claude script whose `SELECTED_TOOLS` holds `cursor`, **then** the script exits 0 with the skip line; **given** the same payload and `SELECTED_TOOLS` without `cursor`, **then** the script acts as it does for a Claude payload (`test/hooks/scripts.test.ts`).
- **edgeCases, added:** Node missing → the script never starts, so this filter cannot help; that launch tail is file 1's `u1-node-missing-hint` (r3 C9.8). The IDE's Third-Party Imports switched off → Cursor runs only its own wiring; the skip never fires there.

### u2-review-gate-all — the review gate holds a run's completion on every client that can hold it

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-review-gate-all |

| `files` | `src/hooks/model.ts` (`CLIENT_EXTENSION_EVENTS` `:143-190`; new `REVIEW_GATE_WIRING` table), `src/hooks/scripts.ts` (`:1957-2010` gate constants; `ReviewGateScriptOptions` `:2032-2045`; `buildReviewGateScript` `:2090-2930`), `src/adapters/claude.ts` (`:237-255` `REVIEW_GATE_EVENTS`; `:828-845` `buildReviewGate`; `:906-937` wiring in `buildSettingsJson`), `src/adapters/copilot.ts` (rows `:313-321`; `buildCopilotHooksJson` `:925-934`), `src/adapters/cursor.ts` (the hooks rows `:485-499` and `buildHooksJson`), `src/adapters/codex.ts` (the hooks rows and the `.codex/hooks.json` builder `buildHooksJson` `:803-830`, whose entries carry `command` and `commandWindows`), `src/emit/capabilityMatrix.ts` (the `review-gate` cap per client), `docs/capability-matrix.md` (regenerated), `src/cli/commands/check.ts` (new probe `review-gate`; `runDoctor` `:1040-1070`), `docs/troubleshooting.md` (a `review-gate` row in the section "What `check` prints"), `test/hooks/scripts.test.ts`, `test/adapters/copilot.test.ts`, `test/adapters/cursor.test.ts`, `test/adapters/codex.test.ts`, `test/adapters/claude.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts` (`:474-520`), `test/ci/pluginPackages.claude.test.ts` (`:445-470`), `test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginPackages.cursor.test.ts`, `test/ci/pluginPackages.codex.test.ts` (each root's hook configuration), `test/corpus/__snapshots__/emissionGoldens.test.ts.snap` (regenerated), `.claude/settings.json` and `.stamity/generated/hooks/claude/*` (the dogfood, regenerated by `sync`), `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** The gate holds only `TaskCompleted` (`REVIEW_GATE_COMPLETION_EVENTS = ["TaskCompleted"]`, `src/hooks/scripts.ts:1984`) and counts rounds on `SubagentStop`, wired by the Claude adapter alone from the two Claude rows of `CLIENT_EXTENSION_EVENTS` (`src/adapters/claude.ts:253-255`, `:935-937`); it reads the run from `session_id`/`run_id` (`:2737`), the finishing agent from `agent_type`/`subagent_type` (`:2738`), the event from `hook_event_name` (`:2739`), the verdict from `last_assistant_message`/`lastAssistantMessage` (`:1998`); a hold exits 2 with the message on stderr (`:2922-2940`, `BLOCK_EXIT` = `BLOCKING_EXIT_CODE`); fault paths open the gate (`:2847-2885`). **What the docs say against that (2026-10-01, re-read 2026-10-06):** `TaskCompleted` fires only through the TaskUpdate tool or when an agent-team teammate finishes, and the task tools are off by default on models newer than Opus 4.7 since 2.1.233 unless `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TODO_TOOLS=1` (code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks; tools-reference; changelog 2.1.233; the default from a secondary source on 2026-10-06; latest client 2.1.291), so on the models this project runs the gate likely never holds — measured by `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`'s fixtures; the `TaskCompleted` note (`src/hooks/model.ts:180`) says its message reaches "the operator rather than … the model", and the page says stderr is "fed back to the model"; the `SubagentStop` note (`:187`) says exit 2 shows a message "only the operator sees", and the page says a block "keeps the subagent running and delivers reason to the subagent as its next instruction". Each client's completion event and how it continues the agent (docs, 2026-10-01): Claude `Stop` — exit 2 or `{"decision":"block","reason"}`, guard `stop_hook_active`, cap 8 consecutive; Copilot CLI `agentStop`/`Stop` — JSON `{"decision":"block","reason"}` only (exit 2 is a warning), guard `stop_hook_active`, cap 8; `subagentStop`/`SubagentStop` carries `agentType`/`agentName` and `response` (camelCase) or `agent_type` and `last_assistant_message` (PascalCase); Codex `Stop` — exit 2 or `decision:"block"` becomes "a new continuation prompt", `SubagentStop` carries `agent_type`, `last_assistant_message`, `session_id` = the parent's; Cursor `stop` — `{"followup_message":"…"}` is submitted as the next user message, `loop_count` input, per-hook `loop_limit` (default 5), and `subagentStop` carries `subagent_type`, `summary`, `status` but no full final text; VS Code Local `Stop`/`SubagentStop` carry no final-message field. **Step 1 — read the measurement.** From `test/fixtures/hooks/payloads/index.json` (written by `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`), for each client: (a) did the completion event fire headless, (b) did a hold continue the agent (`continuedAfterHold`), (c) does the subagent-stop payload carry the finishing agent's id and its final text, (d) does the run key match between the two events. A client is wired only when (a), (b), (c) and (d) all hold; otherwise it is prose-only with the failing letter as its reason. **New data in `src/hooks/model.ts`:** `export interface ReviewGateWiring { tool: Tool; completionEvents: readonly string[]; roundEvents: readonly string[]; holdOutput: "exit-2" \| "json-decision-block" \| "cursor-followup"; runKeyFields: readonly string[]; agentFields: readonly string[]; messageFields: readonly string[]; measured: { clientVersion: string; date: string; fixture: string } }` and `export const REVIEW_GATE_WIRING: readonly ReviewGateWiring[]` plus `export const REVIEW_GATE_PROSE_ONLY: readonly { tool: Tool; reason: string; measured: { clientVersion: string; date: string } \| null }[]`, together covering each of the four tools exactly once (asserted like `CLIENT_HOOK_GUARANTEES`). Expected rows, each confirmed or overturned by the fixtures: claude `completionEvents: ["Stop", "TaskCompleted"]`, `roundEvents: ["SubagentStop"]`, `holdOutput: "exit-2"`; copilot `["Stop"]` / `["SubagentStop"]` (PascalCase keys, so the payload is snake_case) / `"json-decision-block"`; codex `["Stop"]` / `["SubagentStop"]` / `"exit-2"` (prose-only if `codex exec` ran no hook even with the folder trusted); cursor prose-only unless `subagentStop.summary` carries the reviewer's `verdict:` line. The two Claude rows of `CLIENT_EXTENSION_EVENTS` get notes that say what the page says (`TaskCompleted` stderr reaches the model; a `SubagentStop` block keeps the subagent running), and the gate keeps never holding on a round event. **Gate script:** `ReviewGateScriptOptions` gains `wiring: ReviewGateWiring`; the generated constants `COMPLETION_EVENTS`, `MESSAGE_FIELDS` and the field lists of `:2737-2739` come from it (`conversation_id` joins the run-key fields for Cursor); a hold writes per `holdOutput` — `exit-2`: the message on stderr and exit 2, as today; `json-decision-block`: `{"decision":"block","reason":<message>}` on stdout and exit 0; `cursor-followup`: `{"followup_message":<message>}` on stdout and exit 0. A continuation cap: each run's state entry gains `holds` (consecutive holds since its last recorded round), a recorded round resets it to 0, and at `HOLD_CAP = 3` the gate releases with reason code `CONTINUATION_CAP` and the message "Review round <n> of <cap> is still open after 3 held completions; the run ends here as BLOCKED_FAILURE with the open findings attached." — bounding every client whatever its own cap (Codex documents none). `REVIEW_GATE_STATE_SCHEMA` moves to a new value and a state file of the old schema reads as `STATE_ABSENT` for the run (the gate opens once, then records anew). The `layout` option (`generated`/`container`) is unchanged. **Adapters.** Claude: `REVIEW_GATE_EVENTS` derives from the wiring row (completion plus round events); the `Stop` entry joins any canonical `Stop` rows in `buildSettingsJson` rather than replacing them; the comment at `:240-252` ("`Stop` would gate every session") is rewritten: the gate holds only a run with a recorded round and no approval, and at most `HOLD_CAP` times. Copilot, Codex, Cursor (where wired): each emits `.stamity/generated/hooks/<tool>/stamity-review-gate.mjs` from `buildReviewGateScript` with its wiring and wires it directly (`node <path>`, not through the portable runner, whose event set is the six canonical events, `src/hooks/portableRunner.ts:130`) on its completion and round events — Copilot under the PascalCase keys `Stop`/`SubagentStop` in `.github/hooks/stamity.json`, Codex under `Stop`/`SubagentStop` in `.codex/hooks.json` (with `commandWindows` beside `command`, as `buildHooksJson` writes every entry), Cursor under `stop`/`subagentStop` in `.cursor/hooks.json` with `loop_limit: 3`; each row's owner is `<tool>-review-gate`, and that id joins the adapter's `HOOK_INFRA_ARTIFACT_IDS` (`src/adapters/copilot.ts:270-273`, `cursor.ts:280`, `codex.ts:292`) so a plugin that carries hooks owns it. The plugin roots need no client-module edit: `.stamity/generated/hooks/<tool>/` rows land in `hooks/` by the existing prefix rules (`scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:104`, `cursor.mjs:78`, `codex.mjs:92`), and the hook configs are re-addressed as today. **check:** a new probe `const id = "review-gate"` placed after `apm-client` (`u2-apm-floor-current`) and before `plugin-reach` and `pack-reach`; status `pass`; detail one line per selected tool: `claude: enforced on Stop, TaskCompleted (measured Claude Code <the version measured at execution>, <date>)` or `cursor: prose ladder — <reason>`. **Capability matrix:** each client's `review-gate` cap renders from the table (`docs/capability-matrix.md:151` today names Claude only). **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**The review gate holds a run's completion on every client that can hold it.** It rides Claude Code's `Stop` as well as `TaskCompleted`, and the Copilot CLI's and Codex's `Stop` where measured, and stops holding after three held completions with no new round; `check` names, per client, whether the gate or the prose ladder applies." **Follows:** `src/hooks/model.ts:92-121` (guarantees as data rendered into docs and adapters) and `src/hooks/scripts.ts:2090` (one generated gate body, parameterized by the wiring adapter). **Surface pins moved:** `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts:478-530` (fifteen rows at `d10db029`, `pack-reach` included: the title's count and the array become the list it finds plus `review-gate`, seventeen after `apm-client`, with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` line; inbox row 501) and the doctor table of `docs/troubleshooting.md` (set-equal to the probes, `test/docsPages.test.ts:2755-2785`). **Regenerate in the same change:** `npx vitest run test/corpus/emissionGoldens.test.ts --update` (the file first, `--update` last), `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`, and, for the dogfood, `npm run build && node dist/cli.js sync`. |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** each wired client's captured subagent-stop fixture carrying the scripted `verdict: request-changes` text, **when** the generated gate runs on it, **then** the state file records round 1 for that run key and the gate does not hold; **given** that client's captured completion fixture with the same run key, **then** the gate holds in the client's form — exit 2 with the message on stderr (claude, codex), stdout `{"decision":"block","reason":…}` with exit 0 (copilot), stdout `{"followup_message":…}` with exit 0 (cursor, if wired); **given** a later round with `verdict: approve` and `confidence: high`, **then** the completion passes with exit 0 and empty stdout (in `test/hooks/scripts.test.ts`). **Given** three consecutive held completions with no new round, **then** the fourth exits 0 with reason code `CONTINUATION_CAP` on stderr. **Given** a completion for a run with no recorded round, **then** exit 0, empty stdout, and the state file unchanged. **Given** a state file of the previous schema, **then** the gate opens once with `STATE_ABSENT` and records the next round under the new schema. **Given** `REVIEW_GATE_WIRING` and `REVIEW_GATE_PROSE_ONLY`, **then** the four tools appear exactly once across the two, and each wiring row's `measured.fixture` names a file under `test/fixtures/hooks/payloads/` that exists. **Given** a sync for each tool, **then** a wired tool's hook configuration names the gate script on exactly its `completionEvents` and `roundEvents` and nowhere else, and a prose-only tool's names it nowhere (in `test/adapters/<tool>.test.ts`); the Claude root's `hooks/hooks.json` carries the gate on `Stop`, `TaskCompleted` and `SubagentStop` (`test/ci/pluginPackages.claude.test.ts:445-470` re-pinned with justification). **Given** `check` on a repository selecting all four tools, **then** the `review-gate` row passes and names each tool once with "enforced on" or "prose ladder"; the doctor list pin and the troubleshooting table both carry the new row. **Given** `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`, **then** `docs/capability-matrix.md` shows the gate per client and `git diff --exit-code` passes after regeneration. |
| `edgeCases` | Claude's auto permission mode hands a subagent's report back through the SubagentHandback tool, and `last_assistant_message` "is not the delivered report" (code.claude.com hooks, SubagentStop): the verdict then reads as unrecorded and the gate holds to `HOLD_CAP` — a follow-up row; the fixture records whether the reviewer's text arrived. A non-work session never has a recorded round, so `Stop` never holds it. A resumed session with a stale open round is held at most `HOLD_CAP` times. Cursor's imported `.claude/settings.json` `SubagentStop` no longer double-counts a round, because `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode` made the Claude script skip a Cursor payload. Codex `exec` running no project hook even when trusted → Codex is prose-only with that reason, and the TUI result, if the maintainer runs it, is recorded beside it. Windows: the gate's lock and rename budgets are unchanged; the new output forms are stdout writes. |

| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/hooks/scripts.test.ts test/adapters/claude.test.ts test/adapters/copilot.test.ts test/adapters/cursor.test.ts test/adapters/codex.test.ts test/cli/commands/check.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts test/ci/pluginPackages.claude.test.ts test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts test/ci/pluginPackages.cursor.test.ts test/ci/pluginPackages.codex.test.ts test/corpus/emissionGoldens.test.ts && node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs && git diff --exit-code docs/capability-matrix.md && npm run build && node dist/cli.js check && npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test` |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **interfaces, added:** **Vendor refresh (read 2026-10-06).** `TaskCompleted` fires only "through the TaskUpdate tool" or when an agent-team teammate finishes (https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks); the task tools are off by default since 2.1.233 for newer models and `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TODO_TOOLS=1` turns them on (a secondary source); Claude Code's latest is 2.1.291 (2026-10-06). The probe runs on the client version installed at execution and records it; every "measured Claude Code <v>" in the `check` row and the matrix is that version. **Hook-file ownership (file 0).** File 0 owns `.claude/settings.json` and the hook files per entry, so the gate's new `Stop`/`SubagentStop`/`stop`/`subagentStop` entries in `.claude/settings.json`, `.cursor/hooks.json`, `.codex/hooks.json` and `.github/hooks/stamity.json` are engine entries recognised by their `.stamity/generated/hooks/` script path, and an owner's own completion hooks survive `sync` and `clean`. Adding entries to `.codex/hooks.json` changes the file Codex hashes for trust, so Codex asks to re-trust once, at the sync that adds them (https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/hooks, `vendor-facts.md`).
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** a `.cursor/hooks.json` holding an owner's `stop` entry, **when** the gate is wired and the repository then cleaned, **then** the owner's entry is byte-identical after both (`test/adapters/cursor.test.ts`, with file 0's per-entry reducer).

The Cursor adapter emits one `.cursor/rules/<id>.mdc` per admitted rule in generated mode (`src/adapters/cursor.ts:418-424`, `buildMdcRule` `:573-610`). Vendor facts (2026-10-01): project rules must be `.mdc` and a plain `.md` in `.cursor/rules` is ignored (cursor.com/docs/rules); the Cursor CLI reads `.cursor/rules`, `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md`, and loads plugin skills, commands, subagents, MCP and (since 2026-08-11) hooks — plugin rules in the CLI are never named (cursor.com/docs/cli/using, cli/changelog); cloud agents read committed `.cursor/rules/*.mdc` (cursor.com/docs/cloud-agent/best-practices); Bugbot reads the root `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` "and any additional files found while traversing upward from changed files", "Cursor project rules (`*.mdc` files in `.cursor/rules/`) do not apply to Bugbot runs", and "Each rule is truncated at 30,000 characters … The combined rules … are capped at 100,000 characters" (cursor.com/docs/bugbot). Two corpus rules carry the `review` tag: `stamity-security-patterns` (6,658 bytes) and `stamity-testing` (4,481 bytes). **Container (`cursor.mjs`).** `place` returns `null` for `.cursor/rules/…` (repository-owned) and for `.cursor/BUGBOT.md`; `DECLARED_CLASSES` gains `rule: { status: 'repository-owned', reason: RULE_REASON }` with `RULE_REASON = 'Cursor rules are written by stamity plugin setup into the repository as .cursor/rules/*.mdc, which the IDE, the CLI and cloud agents all read; plugin rules are not named on the CLI pages (cursor.com/docs/cli/using, read 2026-10-01), and one copy keeps the IDE from loading each rule twice'`; `buildManifest` drops the `rules` field; the README's "does not carry" list gains "rules — <RULE_REASON>". **Capability:** `CARRIABLE_CLASSES.cursor` becomes `["agent", "skill", "command", "hooks"]`. **Manifest:** `pluginOwnedClasses(manifest, tool)` (`:1168-1183`) also drops any recorded class not in `CARRIABLE_CLASSES[tool]` (import from `../plugins/capabilityFile.ts`, wave 2 below `src/manifest/manifest.ts`'s wave 4 in `test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts:348`, `:611`), so a repository set up on a 1.11.0 Cursor root, whose manifest records `rule`, gets `.cursor/rules/` written by its next `sync` with no re-setup; the record itself is left as it is (validation still accepts it, `:533`). **Bugbot (`src/adapters/cursor.ts`).** New constant `CURSOR_BUGBOT_PATH = ".cursor/BUGBOT.md"`, `BUGBOT_RULE_MAX_CHARS = 30_000` (cited above) and a row, in both install modes, when at least one admitted rule carries the tag `review`: content `wrapInManagedBlock(inner, CURSOR_BUGBOT_PATH, ctx.engineVersion)` (the helper the Claude bridge uses, `src/adapters/claude.ts:715`; `src/merge/managedBlocks.ts:387`), so a team's own Bugbot text outside the block survives `sync` and `clean`; `inner` = the line `# Review rules for Bugbot`, one sentence ("Generated by stamity from this repository's review rules. Bugbot reads this file and not `.cursor/rules/`."), then per review rule in catalog walk order `## <description>`, for a glob-scoped rule the line `Applies to files matching: <globs joined by ", ">`, and the rule body rendered with the same `render()` the `.mdc` rows use (`:395-403`); owner `{ adapter: "cursor", artifactId: "cursor-bugbot", artifactType: "infra" }` (an infra row, so a plugin-backed setup keeps it). When `[...content].length > BUGBOT_RULE_MAX_CHARS` the row carries `sourceRefusal` with the message `.cursor/BUGBOT.md would be <n> characters, and Bugbot truncates a rule at 30,000 (cursor.com/docs/bugbot, read 2026-10-01); narrow the selected review rules or shorten them.`, so `sync` and `check` exit 1 and nothing is written (the pattern of `src/adapters/codex.ts:478-486`). No admitted review rule → no row (an absent file, never an empty block). **Docs:** `docs/plugins.md:35` row `rule` reads `repository-owned` for Cursor, and the reasons list gains the Cursor line. **CHANGELOG** `### Changed`: "**Cursor's rules stay in the repository under a plugin, and Bugbot gets them too.** A plugin-backed Cursor setup writes `.cursor/rules/*.mdc`, which the IDE, the CLI and cloud agents read, and the Cursor plugin root no longer carries them; a repository set up earlier picks them up on its next `sync`. Both install modes write `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` from the rules tagged `review`, because Cursor's Bugbot page says project rules in `.cursor/rules/` do not apply to Bugbot runs and does not mention plugins." **Follows:** `scripts/plugins/clients/claude.mjs:82-83`, `:114-117` (a class declared `repository-owned` with its reason, its rows dropped by `place`) and `src/adapters/claude.ts:715` (a managed-block markdown row). **Surface pins moved:** `test/plugins/capabilityFile.test.ts:455` (the cursor list) and `test/ci/pluginPackages.cursor.test.ts:518` (five carried classes → four), each with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` line. **Regenerate in the same change:** `npx vitest run test/corpus/emissionGoldens.test.ts --update` (the file first, `--update` last) and `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`; the dogfood selects Claude only (`.stamity/manifest.json` `tools: ["claude"]`), so no `sync` is owed. |

the rules describe block at `:283-330` is replaced by these assertions, and the fork-patch case at `:589-617` moves to the repository emission: a fork `.customize.yaml` patch changes the `.cursor/rules/stamity-patch-rule.mdc` head that `plugin setup --client cursor` writes). **Given** `plugin setup --client cursor` from a root built after this unit, **then** `.cursor/rules/` holds one `.mdc` per admitted Cursor rule with the bytes the generated mode writes, and the ledger records each (in `test/cli/commands/pluginSetup.test.ts`). **Given** a manifest recording `{ cursor: { classes: ["agent","skill","command","rule","hooks"] } }` under `plugin-backed`, **when** `pluginOwnedClasses(manifest, "cursor")` runs, **then** it returns `agent, skill, command, hooks`; **when** `sync` runs, **then** `.cursor/rules/` is written. **Given** generated and plugin-backed setups selecting Cursor, **then** `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` holds one managed block whose `##` headings are exactly the descriptions of the admitted `review`-tagged rules, each glob-scoped rule's globs follow its heading, and `[...content].length <= 30000` (in `test/adapters/cursor.test.ts`). **Given** a fixture review rule whose body pushes the file past 30,000 characters, **then** the row carries `sourceRefusal` naming 30,000 and `check` exits 1 with nothing written. **Given** a selection that admits no `review` rule, **then** no `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` row is emitted. **Given** an existing `.cursor/BUGBOT.md` with user text, **when** `sync` then `clean -y` run, **then** the user text is byte-identical after both and only the block was added and then removed. **Given** `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`, **then** the Cursor container row shows `rule` repository-owned and `git diff --exit-code` passes after regeneration. |
| `edgeCases` | A team-marketplace user on an old Cursor root and a new CLI: the old root still carries rules and the repository now carries them too, so the IDE may load each rule twice until the root updates — the `plugin-duplicates` row reports nothing for it (rules are no longer plugin-owned), and the CHANGELOG bullet says to update the plugin with the CLI. A nested `BUGBOT.md` in a subfolder is the team's own and untouched. A rule's description holding a newline or `#`: headings collapse whitespace like `yamlScalar` does (`src/adapters/copilot.ts:892-894`) and escape a leading `#`. The 100,000-character combined cap counts team and dashboard rules this engine cannot see; only the per-rule 30,000 limit is enforced here, and the block's sentence names the combined cap. Windows: content paths are POSIX in the ledger; the `.mdc` rows already pass the Windows leg. |

The unit's wording follows: documented for `.cursor/rules`, unmentioned for plugins. **Cloud agents (SECURITY).** Team-marketplace plugins do not load in Cursor cloud agents (Cursor forum 170280, staff, 2026-09-02, read 2026-10-06; workaround: commit `.cursor/skills/` and `.cursor/hooks.json`), and cloud agents skip `sessionStart`, `sessionEnd`, `beforeMCPExecution` and `afterMCPExecution` even from a committed `.cursor/hooks.json` (https://cursor.com/docs/hooks, read 2026-10-06). So a plugin-backed Cursor repository, where `plugin setup` writes no `.cursor/hooks.json` (measured 2026-10-06, r5), runs no stamity hook in a cloud agent — the subagent guard and the MCP guard included — and a generated one runs its committed hooks there except the session-start index and the MCP guard. `export const PLUGIN_ROUTE_GAPS: readonly { tool: Tool; surface: string; gap: string; citations: readonly { url: string; accessDate: string }[] }[]` holds one row (`cursor`, "Cursor cloud agents", the gap above for both modes), rendered as `## Plugin route gaps` after `## Plugin containers`. `runDoctor` passes the `plugin-runtime` row through `withCursorCloudNote(row, manifest)`: when the manifest records a plugin-backed Cursor client, the detail gains the line `cursor: Cursor cloud agents load no team-marketplace plugin (Cursor forum 170280, 2026-09-02), so a cloud agent here runs no stamity hook — the subagent and MCP guards included; the generated route commits .cursor/hooks.json for agents that need them`, and the row's status is unchanged (decision S27).
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** a manifest recording a plugin-backed Cursor client, **when** `check --json` runs, **then** the `plugin-runtime` row's detail holds the cloud-agent line and its status equals the status computed without it; **given** generated mode with Cursor, **then** no such line (`test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`). **Given** `renderCapabilityMatrix()`, **then** `## Plugin route gaps` holds the Cursor row with both sources and access dates, and `node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs` regenerates the page (`test/emit/capabilityMatrix.test.ts`).
- **edgeCases, added:** A Cursor cloud agent in a generated repository → its `subagentStart` guard runs (not on the skipped list), its MCP guard does not; the matrix row says both.

Copilot CLI — "Agents and skills use first-found-wins precedence … the agent or skill in the plugin is silently ignored" (docs.github.com cli-plugin-reference); a personal agent outranks the project one. class: PluginOwnedClass; id: string; cause: ProjectCopyCause; path: string; reaches: boolean; precedence: "project-wins" \| "bare-name" \| "undocumented"; reason?: string }`, with `reaches: true` when the ledger records the copy's path and `false` with `reason: "not written yet; run sync"` otherwise, and `reason` naming the client's rule when `precedence` is `undocumented`. `duplicatesForClient` skips a ledger row whose `<artifactType>:<artifactId>` is a reach entry's key for that tool. **check:** new probe `const id = "plugin-reach"` after `review-gate` (from `u2-review-gate-all`) and before `pack-reach`: `pass` with "no plugin client recorded" or "no customized artifact under a plugin-owned class"; **given** the same patch in a copilot-recorded repository, **then** the shared `.agents/skills/` copy of that skill holds it, owned by copilot. **Given** `models.pins` setting a class `stamity-reviewer` uses, **then** each recorded plugin client that carries agents (claude, copilot, cursor) gets that agent at project level with the pinned `model:`, and an agent whose class is unpinned gets none. **Given** an installed pack skill, **then** all four clients get it under a plugin, as Claude alone did before. **Given** a fork-layer patch only, **then** no copy is written. **Given** an overlay-free corpus, **then** `buildContentIndex`'s items deep-equal today's (no `patchedBy` key appears). **Given** the repository above, **when** `check` runs, **then** `plugin-duplicates` passes and `plugin-reach` lists `claude: agent stamity-reviewer (user-override) … bare-name`; **given** cursor recorded with a model pin, **then** `plugin-reach` warns naming "Cursor documents no order"; **given** `plugin status --json`, **then** `reach` holds the same entries with `reaches: true` (in `test/cli/commands/check.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/plugin.test.ts`). **Given** an override added after the last sync, **then** its entry reads `reaches: false` with "run sync" and the row warns. **Given** `PLUGIN_COPY_PRECEDENCE`, **then** it holds exactly one row per (tool, class) of `CARRIABLE_CLASSES`, each with a citation dated 2026-10-01 or later. **Given** `npx vitest run test/emit/ownership.test.ts --coverage`, **then** `src/emit/ownership.ts` stays at 100% statements, branches, functions and lines. |
| `edgeCases` | A user override that adds a new id the plugin does not carry is plain repository content and was never filtered; it still gets a reach entry only if its class is plugin-owned, with `precedence` for a name no plugin copy shares reported as `project-wins`. A user's personal copy (`~/.claude/skills/<id>`, a personal Copilot agent) outranks the project copy on those clients; the note says so and `check` cannot see it. Removing an override: the next `sync` stops emitting the copy and reclaims it through the ledger, so `plugin-duplicates` cannot flag a stale copy. A Claude bare name used by both the project copy and the plugin's namespaced copy: the corpus dispatches by bare name, so the project copy runs; whether the model, invoking a skill on its own judgement, picks `st-x` or `stamity:st-x` is undocumented, recorded by the measurement. Hooks are not reach entries: an accepted user or pack hook under a plugin that owns hooks stays the sync warning it is today (`src/emit/hooksInfra.ts:500-511`). |

- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** generated mode and a user override of `stamity-reviewer`, **then** `validate --json`'s row has `emits: true` and one `reaches: true` entry per selected tool. **Given** a plugin-backed Claude fixture carrying `agent` and the override, before `sync`, **then** `reach` is `[{ tool: "claude", reaches: false, reason: "not written yet; run sync" }]` and `emits: false`; after `sync`, **then** `reaches: true` with the `bare-name` rule as `reason`. **Given** `apm-backed` with tools `[claude]`, a lock deploying the package and the override, **then** `reaches: false` with the APM reason, and the human output prints that line (`test/cli/commands/validate.test.ts`). **Given** an installed pack skill in a plugin-backed repository, **then** every client gets the pack's copy (regression of `efc7b79d`).

### u2-declare — `plugin setup --declare` writes the project's plugin declaration as ledger-owned keys

anything else → `USAGE` "--declare takes a channel (stable, canary) or a distribution tag such as plugins/v1.12.0."; `slug === null` → `CONFIG_ERROR` "this package names no github.com repository, so there is no marketplace to declare."; `marketplace` = the package name without its scope (the catalog's `name`, `scripts/plugins/catalogs.mjs:309`); `export async function existingDeclarations(rootDir, marketplace): Promise<string[]>` — the files (`.claude/settings.json`, `.github/copilot/settings.json`) whose parsed `extraKnownMarketplaces` already has the key `marketplace` (an `enabledPlugins` entry alone, the client's own install record, is not a declaration). **Setup:** `PluginSetupInput` gains `declaration?: PluginDeclaration`; `runSetup` resolves `--declare`, refuses before planning when `existingDeclarations` is non-empty (`VALIDATION_ERROR` "This repository already declares the <m> marketplace in <file>; remove that entry or run plugin setup without --declare.", exit 1, nothing written) and when no selected client is `claude` or `copilot` (`CONFIG_ERROR` "--declare writes the Claude Code and Copilot declarations; Cursor has no committed declaration (its plugins are set in the team marketplace dashboard, and .cursor/cli.json takes only permissions), so set up claude or copilot."); `planPluginSetup` returns `plugin: { mode: "plugin-backed", clients, declaration }`. **Manifest:** `PluginConfig` gains `declaration?: { marketplace: string; repo: string; ref: string; kind: "channel" \| "tag"; sha?: string; shaVersion?: string }`; the validator refuses unknown keys, a `marketplace` outside `/^[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9.-]*[a-z0-9])?$/`, a `repo` not `owner/repo`, a `ref` failing the ref-name rule; Both files carry the same `source` and `ref`, so the Copilot CLI reading both cannot see two refs; only `.claude/settings.json` carries a `sha`. The Copilot plugin container drops the path (`place` → `null`, repository-owned). **Output:** `runSetup` prints one line per declared file and, for Claude, the next step "each contributor runs `claude plugin install <name>@<m> --scope project` once: Claude Code does not install a plugin whose catalog entry is an external source from project settings"; the JSON result gains `declared: { file: string; marketplace: string; ref: string }[]`. **Docs:** `docs/plugins.md:152-163` replaces the hand-written declaration with `plugin setup --declare`. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**`plugin setup --declare <channel\|tag>` declares the plugin for a repository's contributors.** It writes `extraKnownMarketplaces` and `enabledPlugins` into `.claude/settings.json` and `.github/copilot/settings.json` as keys the ledger owns one by one, so `clean` removes exactly those and keeps every key another tool wrote." **Follows:** `src/cli/commands/plugin/setup.ts:85-153` (refuse before planning), `src/manifest/claudeSettings.ts` (per-entry co-ownership on file 0's core) and `src/cli/kit/packageName.ts:187-224` (running-package identity, cached). **Surface pins moved:** `docs/cli-reference.md` (regenerated by `node scripts/generate-docs.mjs`; byte-gated by its suite) and `test/architecture/boundaries.test.ts` `PLAN_MAP` (two rows); no new verb, so `src/cli.ts` counts, `ADVERTISED` and the two `test/docsPages.test.ts` verb arrays do not move. |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** a fresh repository and plugin roots for claude and copilot, **when** `plugin setup --client claude,copilot --declare stable -y` runs, **then** `.claude/settings.json` and `.github/copilot/settings.json` each carry `extraKnownMarketplaces.stamity.source` = `{source:"github", repo:"<slug>", ref:"plugin-stable"}` and `enabledPlugins["stamity@stamity"] = true`, and the ledger rows of both files record those two pointers (in `test/cli/commands/pluginSetup.test.ts`). **Given** `--declare tag` on an engine at 1.12.0 (or `--declare plugins/v1.12.0`), **then** the declared `ref` is `plugins/v1.12.0`. **Given** `.claude/settings.json` already holding `extraKnownMarketplaces.stamity` (any value), **then** the command exits 1 naming `.claude/settings.json` and `git status --porcelain` shows no change. **Given** `.claude/settings.json` holding only `enabledPlugins["stamity@stamity"]: true` written by the client, **then** setup succeeds, the key is untouched and unrecorded, and a later `clean -y` leaves it in place. **Given** a declared setup and a foreign key `extraKnownMarketplaces.other` added afterwards, **when** `clean -y` runs, **then** `extraKnownMarketplaces` holds only `other`, `enabledPlugins` holds only foreign entries, no `.bak` exists, and `.github/copilot/settings.json`, which the engine created and which holds nothing foreign, is gone (in `test/merge/settingsKeyOwnership.test.ts`). **Given** `--declare canary` with no canary configured, **then** exit 1 naming `stamity.distribution.channels.canary`; **given** `--declare main`, **then** exit 2 naming the accepted forms; **given** `plugin status --declare stable`, **then** exit 2. **Given** `--client cursor --declare stable`, **then** exit 1 with the message naming that Cursor has no committed declaration and "set up claude or copilot". **Given** a manifest whose `plugin.declaration.ref` is `plugins/../x`, **then** manifest validation fails naming `plugin.declaration.ref`. **Given** `distributionRefs()` and `scripts/distribution-identity.mjs`, **then** the default channel and tag pattern are equal. **Given** `node scripts/generate-docs.mjs`, **then** `docs/cli-reference.md` lists `--declare <ref>` and `git diff --exit-code` passes after regeneration. |
| `edgeCases` | Claude Code still needs one `plugin install … --scope project` per contributor (external source); setup prints it, and the route guides (file 3) say so. An untrusted folder ignores the Claude declaration silently (vendor page); nothing for the engine to do. A later channel change on an existing setup is not this flag's: `plugin setup` refuses an existing manifest, so it is clean-then-setup until a follow-up adds a config route (inbox row). A fork with a renamed plugin declares its own marketplace name and repository (both read from the running package). A `.github/copilot/settings.json` that already exists with foreign keys (`hooks`, `disableAllHooks`) keeps them; one that is not a JSON object is a collision, as for the Claude file. Windows: settings files are written through the same atomic writer; pointers are strings, no path separators. |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **Replaces:** the sentence beginning "**From `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md (u1-settings-key-ownership)`:**" through "it is neither recorded nor removed." → "**From `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-00.md (u0-settings-ownership)`:** `LedgerEntry.coOwned?: CoOwnership` with `members` (an RFC 6901 pointer of depth 1 or 2 → the sha256 of the member's canonical JSON), `elements`, `preexisting` and `createdFile`; the core `src/manifest/coOwnedJson.ts` (`CoOwnedJsonSpec` with `members: MemberSpec[]` — `{ pointer, foreign: "collide" \| "yield", structural? }` — and `elements`, `planCoOwnedJson`, `reduceCoOwnedJson`, `predictCoOwnedMerge`, `materializeCoOwned`); `claudeSettingsSpec(extraMembers)`; the lane registry. **This unit passes** its two pointers as `claudeSettingsSpec([{ pointer: "/extraKnownMarketplaces/<m>", foreign: "yield" }, { pointer: "/enabledPlugins/<name>@<m>", foreign: "yield" }])` when declared, and registers a `.github/copilot/settings.json` lane on the same core with a spec holding the same two members and no elements. A declared member that already holds an equal value no ledger row wrote stays foreign: it is neither recorded nor removed (the core's equal-foreign rule)."
- **files, added:** `src/manifest/ownedPaths.ts` (`.github/copilot/settings.json` joins `OWNED_PATHS.exact`; `version` rises).
- **interfaces, added:** the path is co-owned per pointer through `coOwnedReclaimReducers`, so a forged row never reaches the whole-file branch; the planner golden of file 0 fails until the path is in the bound.
- **files, added:** `src/cli/commands/config.ts` (a `KEY_SPECS` row `plugin.declarationSha` after `:755`), `docs/configuration.md` (regenerated), `test/cli/commands/config.test.ts`, `test/cli/docs/configReference.test.ts`, `src/cli/commands/sync/engine.ts` (the manifest write that records a moved tag), `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs` (the Copilot `update` line `:299`), `scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs` (`:209-211`, `:231-233`), `test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts` (`:539`), `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`.
- **interfaces, added:** **A tag declaration follows the engine version.** `--declare tag` declares `releaseTag(identity, <engine version>)`; `--declare plugins/v<v>` is accepted only when `<v>` is the engine's own version (`USAGE` "--declare plugins/v<v>: a tag declaration follows this engine (<engine version>); use --declare tag, or a channel."). `PluginConfig.declaration` becomes `{ marketplace: string; repo: string; ref: string; kind: "channel" \| "tag"; sha?: string; shaVersion?: string }` (the validator refuses `sha` unless `kind` is `tag`, a `sha` that is not 40 lowercase hex, and `sha` without `shaVersion`). `sync` and `upgrade` render a `tag` declaration at the running engine's version and write that `ref` into the manifest they write, so the CLI pin's update moves it in both files; `renovate/plugins.json` is not extended to these files, because a ref bumped alone reads as drift under per-entry ownership and the next `sync` restores it (r5 P8.4). **The commit pin is operator input** (principle 6: the distribution commit exists only after the release pushes, so no engine can carry its own): `plugin setup --declare tag --declare-sha <sha>` and `config set plugin.declarationSha <sha>` (`none` clears it) record `sha` with `shaVersion` = the engine version; `.claude/settings.json`'s entry carries `sha` beside `source` while `shaVersion` equals the engine's version; `.github/copilot/settings.json` carries none (its `github` source documents no `sha`), so the two files share `source` and `ref` and differ only by Claude's `sha`. A sync by another engine version renders the new tag without `sha`, clears the record and warns `declaration-sha-stale` (a `u2-json-contract` registry code, severity `warning`): "the declared plugins/v<old> was pinned to <sha>; plugins/v<new> is not pinned yet — copy its distribution commit from that release's notes: <pinned call> config set plugin.declarationSha <sha>". **Refusals.** `existingDeclarations` also reports an `enabledPlugins` key `<plugin>@<other>` whose marketplace differs (the plugin enabled through another marketplace, r5 P8.3): "This repository already enables <plugin> from the <other> marketplace in <file>; remove it or run plugin setup without --declare." A setup with no `claude` and no `copilot` → `CONFIG_ERROR` "--declare writes the Claude Code and Copilot declarations; Cursor has no committed declaration (its plugins are set in the team marketplace dashboard, and .cursor/cli.json takes only permissions), so set up claude or copilot." A `claude,cursor` (or `copilot,cursor`) setup → declares for the others and prints `cursor: no committed declaration exists for Cursor; roll the plugin out through a team marketplace`; the JSON gains `notDeclared: [{ client: "cursor", reason }]`. **Lines printed after a declaration:** the existing Claude per-contributor install; for Copilot, "the Copilot CLI installs a declared plugin itself; update it with `copilot plugin update <plugin>@<marketplace>`" (Copilot CLI 1.0.89 `plugin update --help`: "Plugin name (plugin-name@marketplace-name)", r5 P9.4); and one precedence line, "a managed extraKnownMarketplaces.<marketplace> entry replaces this one in Claude Code, and a .strict managed file admits no other ref". The distribution README's Copilot `update` (`scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:299`) and the Copilot root README (`scripts/plugins/clients/copilot.mjs:209-211`, `:231-233`) print `copilot plugin update stamity@stamity` from the identity, keeping the bare name only for a direct install.
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** an engine at 1.12.0 and `plugin setup --client claude,copilot --declare tag --declare-sha <40 hex>`, **then** both files declare `ref: "plugins/v1.12.0"`, only `.claude/settings.json`'s entry carries `sha`, and the manifest records `kind: "tag"`, `sha` and `shaVersion: "1.12.0"` (`test/cli/commands/pluginSetup.test.ts`). **Given** that repository synced by an engine at 1.13.0, **then** both files declare `plugins/v1.13.0`, no `sha` is written, the manifest's `sha` is cleared, and `sync --json`'s `warnings` holds `declaration-sha-stale`; **given** `config set plugin.declarationSha <hex>` and a sync, **then** `sha` returns with `shaVersion: "1.13.0"` (`test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts`, `test/cli/commands/config.test.ts`). **Given** `--declare plugins/v1.11.0` on a 1.12.0 engine, **then** exit 2 naming `--declare tag`. **Given** `.claude/settings.json` enabling `stamity@acme-mirror`, **then** exit 1 naming that file and `acme-mirror`. **Given** `--client cursor --declare stable`, **then** exit 1 and the message says Cursor has no committed declaration; **given** `--client claude,cursor --declare stable`, **then** Claude's declaration is written and the output and the JSON name Cursor as not declared. **Given** a Copilot declaration, **then** the output names `copilot plugin update stamity@stamity`; **given** a distribution build, **then** its README's Copilot update line reads `copilot plugin update stamity@stamity` (`test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`; `test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts:539` re-pinned with `TEST CHANGE, justified: Copilot CLI 1.0.89 names plugin-name@marketplace-name`).
- **edgeCases, added:** A channel declaration → no `sha` (a channel moves by design); `--declare-sha` with a channel is a usage error. A pinned tag whose operator never re-pins after an upgrade → the warning repeats on every sync until a `sha` is set or cleared with `none`. A repository whose organization manages `stamity` at a channel → the managed entry wins in Claude Code; the precedence line says so at setup.

### u2-apm-renovate-preset — a Renovate preset for the APM refs, retargeted by `fork-identity.mjs`

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-apm-renovate-preset |
| `requirements` | REQ-APM-015 |
| `files` | `renovate/apm.json` (new); `scripts/fork-identity.mjs` (header `:1-37`; `plan()` `:216-252`); `test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts` (`TARGETS` `:35`; new cases); `test/ci/renovateApmPreset.test.ts` (new); `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (`:162-164`; the APM Renovate rules and the paragraph after them `:728-771` at `d10db029`); `docs/getting-started.md` (APM section, one line); `test/docsPages.test.ts` (`REATTESTATION_DATE`); `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Renovate's `apm` manager** (renovatebot/renovate `main`, `lib/modules/manager/apm/extract.ts`, read 2026-10-01): an entry `owner/repo/path#ref` gives `depName = "owner/repo/path"` (the text before `#`) and, on github.com, `packageName = "owner/repo"` (the first two segments) with datasource `github-tags`; a 40-hex ref followed by a `# <tag>` comment is a pinned digest; only string entries are read (object entries are dropped). So the rules match `matchDepNames` — `matchPackageNames` would match the source-tree and the slim dependency alike. **Assumed from `docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md (u1-renovate-suffix)`:** `scripts/fork-identity.mjs` accepts `--suffix <id>` (`[a-z0-9]+`) and passes it into `plan(options)` as `options.suffix` (`string \| undefined`). **New, `renovate/apm.json` (canonical, strict — default 2):** `{ "$schema": "https://docs.renovatebot.com/renovate-schema.json", "description": "APM pins of stamity. Renovate's apm manager reads only the string form owner/repo[/path]#<ref> and drops object entries silently; it runs apm install and commits every file that install changed, so apm must be on its PATH. Pass GITHUB_APM_PAT through Renovate's secrets (never a config value) and set APM_NO_SCRIPTS=1 so the install runs no lifecycle script.", "packageRules": [ { "description": "The APM packages in a release's distribution tree: refs are plugins/v<version> tags.", "matchManagers": ["apm"], "matchDepNames": ["zomarit/stamity/apm", "zomarit/stamity/apm-charter"], "versioning": "regex:^plugins/v(?<major>\\d+)\\.(?<minor>\\d+)\\.(?<patch>\\d+)$", "pinDigests": true, "groupName": "stamity" }, { "description": "The source tree's APM package: refs are v<version> tags.", "matchManagers": ["apm"], "matchDepNames": ["zomarit/stamity"], "versioning": "semver", "pinDigests": true, "groupName": "stamity" } ] }` (FALLBACK: rule 0's dep names are `zomarit/stamity` with `matchCurrentValue: "/^apm\\/v/"` and the regex prefix `apm/v`). **New, the fork retarget** (`plan()` gains entries in the `retargetPreset` style, `scripts/fork-identity.mjs:197-214`, parse-replace-reparse): rule 0 `matchDepNames` → `["<owner>/<repo>/apm", "<owner>/<repo>/apm-charter"]`, rule 1 → `["<owner>/<repo>"]`; with `--suffix <id>`: rule 0 `versioning` → `regex:^plugins/v(?<major>\\d+)\\.(?<minor>\\d+)\\.(?<patch>\\d+)-<id>\\.(?<build>\\d+)$` (built by `tagVersioningTemplate({ prefix: "plugins/v", suffix: <id>, required: true })`, exported by `scripts/fork-identity.mjs` in file 1's u1-renovate-suffix), rule 1 gains `"ignoreUnstable": false` and `"allowedVersions": "/^v?\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+-<id>\\.\\d+$/"` — the suffix is required in both, because a fork imported by plan 014's recipe carries upstream's `v*` tags (`refs/tags/v*`), which an optional suffix would offer to its consumers. `TARGETS` and the header move from two presets to three. **Docs:** `docs/enterprise-forks.md:163-164` names three presets; `:726-760` points at `renovate/apm.json` (`"extends": ["github><owner>/<repo>//renovate/apm.json"]`) instead of the hand-written rules, keeps the measured facts (apm on Renovate's `PATH`, `GITHUB_APM_PAT`, the lockfile error without them) and states the string-form limit; `docs/getting-started.md`'s APM section names the preset. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**`renovate/apm.json` keeps APM pins current.** It orders the distribution's `plugins/v<version>` refs and the source tree's `v<version>` tags, pins digests, groups the APM packages with the CLI pin of `renovate/companion.json` into one pull request, and `fork-identity.mjs` retargets it — with `--suffix`, to the fork's own suffixed tags only." **Follows:** `renovate/plugins.json:1-18` (a preset carrying the identity as data that `fork-identity.mjs` retargets) |

**Amended 2026-10-06 (the updated brief; read at `d10db029`; sentences of the table above that this amendment replaces are already replaced in place):**
- **files, added:** `renovate/companion.json` (one key, `groupName`); `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (`:162-164`; the two
  Renovate rules and the paragraph after them `:728-771` at `d10db029`).
- **interfaces, added:** **Brief A2.** (a) `APM_NO_SCRIPTS=1`: apm-cli 0.32.0 and 0.33.0 then run no lifecycle script —
  policy, user or project `lifecycle:` — for that invocation (`apm_cli/core/lifecycle_scripts.py:29`, `:545-561`); it
  does not stop hooks or MCP servers from deploying (those are executables; `u2-apm-deliveries` reports them).
  (b) `GITHUB_APM_PAT` as a redacted secret: Renovate's `env` option is top-level only and needs the admin's
  `allowedEnv`, and a raw value in `customEnvVariables` may be logged, so the guide shows the self-hosted admin's
  `config.js`: `secrets: { GITHUB_APM_PAT: process.env.GITHUB_APM_PAT }` and `customEnvVariables: { APM_NO_SCRIPTS: "1",
  GITHUB_APM_PAT: "{{ secrets.GITHUB_APM_PAT }}" }` (Renovate `docs/usage/self-hosted-configuration.md`,
  `customEnvVariables` and `secrets`, read 2026-10-06); the preset itself sets no environment. (c) Grouping: both rules'
  `groupName` becomes `"stamity"` and `renovate/companion.json`'s rule gains the same `groupName`, so a repository
  holding the CLI pin and the slim ref (`apm-backed`) gets one pull request when one run sees both updates; the guide
  adds, for such a repository only, `{ "matchDepNames": ["@<scope>/<package>", "<owner>/<repo>/apm"], "groupName":
  "stamity", "minimumGroupSize": 2 }`, which holds the pull request until both are visible (Renovate's
  `minimumGroupSize`, `docs/usage/configuration-options.md`, read 2026-10-06). The shipped presets carry no
  `minimumGroupSize`: a repository with one of the two pins would never get its update. (d) String form only: the
  description and the guide say an entry in the object form — the only form that carries per-dependency `targets`
  (u2-apm-charter's A1d sentence) — is never updated, and Renovate commits every file `apm install` changed, not just
  the lock (`lib/modules/manager/apm/artifacts.ts:48-77`, read 2026-10-06). (e) The guide's unsuffixed sample (`:757`)
  goes with the hand-written rules; the replacement text tells a fork to run `fork-identity.mjs --suffix <id>`, which
  makes its copy of `renovate/apm.json` require the suffix (the canonical preset refuses suffixed tags).
- **testCriteria, added:** **Given** `renovate/apm.json`, **then** its `description` contains `APM_NO_SCRIPTS=1`,
  `secrets` and `string form`, both rules carry `groupName: "stamity"`, and neither carries `minimumGroupSize`;
  **given** `renovate/companion.json`, **then** its rule carries `groupName: "stamity"`; **given** the guide, **then**
  its grouping snippet parses as JSON with `minimumGroupSize: 2` and two `matchDepNames`, and its Renovate section names
  `customEnvVariables`, `secrets`, `APM_NO_SCRIPTS=1` and `--suffix` (`test/ci/renovateApmPreset.test.ts`). **Given**
  `fork-identity.mjs --repository https://github.com/acme-corp/stamity-fork`, **then** `renovate/companion.json` keeps
  `groupName: "stamity"` and only its package name moves (`test/ci/forkIdentityScript.test.ts`).
- **edgeCases, added:** A generated-mode repository with only the CLI pin → the shared group holds one update and is
  never blocked. An admin who puts the token straight into `customEnvVariables` → Renovate may log it; the guide shows
  only the `secrets` form. A Renovate deployment that cannot set environment variables (a hosted app) → no `apm` on its
  image anyway (the 2026-09-22 rehearsal's lockfile error), so the guide names self-hosted Renovate as the supported
  route.
- **Replaces:** "APM pins of stamity. Renovate's apm manager reads only the string form owner/repo[/path]#<ref> and drops object entries silently; it runs apm install, so apm must be on its PATH, with GITHUB_APM_PAT for a private repository." → "APM pins of stamity. Renovate's apm manager reads only the string form owner/repo[/path]#<ref> and drops object entries silently; it runs apm install and commits every file that install changed, so apm must be on its PATH. Pass GITHUB_APM_PAT through Renovate's secrets (never a config value) and set APM_NO_SCRIPTS=1 so the install runs no lifecycle script." (the preset's `description` value)
- **Replaces:** "stamity APM packages" → "stamity" (the `groupName` value of both rules; the text occurs twice in the cell)

### u2-apm-coexistence-fork — prove the APM package beside another APM package, and smoke a fork's published slim ref

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-apm-coexistence-fork |
| `requirements` | REQ-APM-016 |
| `files` | `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (the `apm-install` matrix `:386-396`: a fourth row; role-gated steps after `:429-450`; the comments `:361-377`, `:404-406`); `scripts/apm-coexistence-smoke.mjs` (new); `test/fixtures/apm/coexist-package/apm.yml` and `test/fixtures/apm/coexist-package/.apm/{agents/acme-auditor.agent.md,prompts/acme-report.prompt.md,instructions/acme-style.instructions.md,skills/acme-notes/SKILL.md}` (new); `test/ci/apmCoexistence.test.ts` (new); `.github/workflows/fork-release.yml` (a new job after `publish`, `:399-801`); `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts` (`:170` job list; new cases); `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (**Release your fork** → "Consume the release" `:1002-1006`); `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** No leg installs this package beside another one; `fork-release.yml` checks the APM package only with `generate-apm-package.mjs --check` (`:315-321`) and prints a pin hint in the dry-run summary (`:842`); its job list is pinned as `["probe", "gates", "publish", "dry-run-summary"]` (`test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts:170`); the gates job outputs `plugins_tag` (`fork-release.yml:174-182`, written at `:356-372`). `release.yml` keeps third-party interpreters out of credentialed jobs: "No job that runs third-party build code holds any of them" (`:670-672`). **New, the smoke** `node scripts/apm-coexistence-smoke.mjs --apm <bin> [--cli <dist/cli.js>] [--slim <dir>] [--work <dir>] [--keep] [--json]` (plain ESM over Node built-ins, like `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs`; exports its pure verdict functions): sources are `--slim` (default: a clean export of this checkout's root `apm.yml` + `.apm/`, byte-identical to the release's `apm/` copy by u2-apm-slim-package's test) and `test/fixtures/apm/coexist-package` (ids `acme-auditor`, `acme-report`, `acme-style` with `applyTo: "**/*.md"`, `acme-notes` — none starting `st-` or `stamity-`). **Consumer A, `apm-backed`:** `git init`; `apm.yml` with both local dependencies and `targets: [claude, copilot, cursor, codex]`; `apm install`; assert exit 0, both lock rows typed `apm_package`, their `deployed_files` disjoint, every listed file present; then `node <cli> init -y --tools claude,copilot,cursor,codex --install-mode apm-backed`; then `node <cli> check --json`: `ok` true, the `plugin-duplicates` row `pass`, drift clean, and no ledger path inside either dependency's deployed set. **Consumer B, `generated`:** `git init`; `node <cli> init -y --tools claude,cursor` first; then both dependencies with `targets: [claude, cursor]` and `apm install` (apm skips the files the CLI wrote, "local file exists (not managed by APM)"); then `check --json`: `ok` false and the `plugin-duplicates` row `fail`, its detail naming `cursor`, `st-work` and the stamity dependency. Exit 0 all held; 1 an assertion failed (each listed); 2 apm or the CLI could not run (the split of `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:56-68`). **New, CI:** matrix row `{ apm: '0.33.0', role: coexistence }`; steps `if: matrix.role == 'coexistence'`: "Install" `npm ci`, "Build" `npm run build`, "APM coexistence smoke" `node scripts/apm-coexistence-smoke.mjs --apm "$RUNNER_TEMP/apm-venv/bin/apm" --cli dist/cli.js`; the route smoke step gains `&& matrix.role != 'coexistence'`; the no-`npm ci` comment (`:404-406`) says only the coexistence row installs dependencies, because it drives the CLI. The aggregator already needs `apm-install` (no new job, no `all-ci-checks` pin moves). **New, fork release** — a job of its own rather than a step in `publish` (which holds write credentials; see Concerns C12): `apm-route: { name: "apm route smoke (published slim ref, apm ${{ matrix.apm }})", needs: [gates, publish], if: "needs.publish.result == 'success'", runs-on: ubuntu-latest, timeout-minutes: 15, permissions: { contents: read }, strategy: { fail-fast: false, matrix: { apm: ['0.29.1', '0.33.0'] } } }` with steps: checkout depth 1 `persist-credentials: false` (for the smoke script only), setup-node `22.22.2`, setup-python `3.13` (the SHAs `ci.yml` pins), then "Smoke the published slim ref" with `env: { APM: ${{ matrix.apm }}, GITHUB_APM_PAT: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}, SPEC: ${{ github.repository }}/apm#${{ needs.gates.outputs.plugins_tag }} }` running `python -m venv "$RUNNER_TEMP/apm-venv"`, `pip install --quiet "apm-cli==${APM}"`, `node scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs --apm "$RUNNER_TEMP/apm-venv/bin/apm" --source "$SPEC" --targets claude,copilot,cursor,codex`, then appending `apm install $SPEC` under `### APM install spec` to `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` (`GITHUB_APM_PAT` is APM's documented token variable; the job token is read-only and scoped to the fork). The dry-run summary (`:842`, already rewritten by u2-apm-slim-package) also prints the spec it would test. FALLBACK: `SPEC` names the APM tag. **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**The APM route is proved beside another APM package, and a fork release smoke-tests its own slim ref.** A CI leg installs this package next to a fixture package, then proves the APM-backed mode green and the generated mode red with its duplicates row; `fork-release.yml` installs the fork's freshly published `<owner>/<repo>/apm#plugins/v<version>` at apm-cli 0.29.1 and 0.33.0 and prints the spec." **Follows:** `scripts/apm-install-smoke.mjs:548-745` (a throwaway consumer, a deployed-tree verdict, exit 0/1/2) and `.github/workflows/release.yml:554-623` (`apm-route`: a credential-free job running the third-party client) |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** the smoke's exported verdict functions and fixture locks and envelopes (`test/ci/apmCoexistence.test.ts`), **then** two dependencies with one shared deployed path → a failed assertion naming that path; consumer A's envelope with `plugin-duplicates` `warn` → failed; consumer B's envelope with the row `pass` → failed; the expected shapes → passed. **Given** `STAMITY_APM_BIN` armed after `npm run build`, **when** the real smoke runs, **then** it exits 0 (`describe.skipIf`, like `test/ci/apmInstall.test.ts:527`). **Given** `ci.yml` parsed, **then** the `apm-install` matrix holds a `coexistence` row at `0.33.0`, its three gated steps exist in order, and the route smoke step does not run on it. **Given** `fork-release.yml` parsed, **then** the job list is `["probe", "gates", "publish", "apm-route", "dry-run-summary"]` (re-pin `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts:170`, `TEST CHANGE, justified:`), `apm-route.needs` is `["gates", "publish"]`, its permissions are exactly `{ contents: read }`, its matrix is `["0.29.1", "0.33.0"]`, its `SPEC` contains `/apm#` and `needs.gates.outputs.plugins_tag`, and no step of it references any secret other than `GITHUB_TOKEN`. **Given** the "Push plugin distribution" step pair, **then** `release.yml` and `fork-release.yml` still hold it byte-equal (the existing test). |
| `edgeCases` | A future apm that overwrites instead of skipping in consumer B → `check` still fails (drift plus the duplicates row); the smoke asserts only the row's failure. A fork whose distribution lives in another repository → `GITHUB_TOKEN` cannot read it; the job fails visibly and the guide names a fine-grained read token as `GITHUB_APM_PAT` (repository secret) as the remedy. A re-run of a published release → the smoke reruns harmlessly. The 0.29.1 leg on a slim ref → covered by u2-apm-sparse-measure's criterion (c). |
| `depends_on` | u2-apm-backed-mode, u2-apm-floor-current, u2-apm-deliveries, u2-apm-slim-package, u2-apm-renovate-preset, docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-01.md (u1-npm-dist-tag), docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-00.md |
