A command line commander rejects (exit 2) → stdout stays empty, as today. A `wrote[].warning` naming a `.bak` inside the repository → relative in the document, absolute in the human line (unchanged). A dry run → `changedFiles` predicts emission and reclaim only, as the reference says. A producer added later without a registry code → `typecheck` fails (`WarningCode` is a closed union). A consumer reading a 1.11.0 document → no `schemaVersion`, which the reference says to read as schema 1. A `clean` that empties `.stamity/` → one `deleted` row per file the sweep removed, none for the folder. |

### u2-release-integrity — `release.json` schema 2 states npm's integrity, a digest per client root and the engine's owned paths, and both release workflows check the registry after publishing

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `id` | u2-release-integrity |
| `requirements` | REQ-PLUGIN-054, REQ-PLUGIN-011 (amended) |
| `files` | `scripts/plugins/rootDigest.mjs` (new), `scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs` (`RELEASE_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION` `:17`; key lists `:19-33`; `buildReleaseManifest` `:83-122`; `unsupportedKeys` `:125`; `validateRuntime` `:155`; `validatePackages` `:169`; `validateReleaseManifest` `:235-257`), `scripts/plugins/runtime.mjs` (`buildRuntimeManifest` `:419-427`; `buildRuntime` `:435-460`), `scripts/build-plugin-runtime.mjs` (header step 4 `:24-26`), `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs` (header `:21-22`; the archive loop `:519-545`; the manifest `:573-596`; `renderReadme` `:341`), `src/index.ts` (one export, only if file 0 does not export its bound there), `.github/workflows/release.yml` ("Verify plugin distribution digest" `:841-886`; "Publish to npm with provenance" `:898-899`; a new step after it), `.github/workflows/fork-release.yml` ("Verify plugin distribution digest" `:475`; "Publish to the registry" `:644-666`; a new step after it), `docs/enterprise-forks.md` (`### Consume the release`, one paragraph), `test/ci/rootDigest.test.ts` (new), `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts` (`:85-86`, `:171`; new cases), `test/ci/pluginRuntime.test.ts` (`:492-496`; the stub package's export), `test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts` (the stub `RUNTIME.json`; `:274-321`), `test/ci/pluginDownstream.test.ts` (its stub runtime, re-read at intake), `test/ci/workflow.test.ts`, `test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts` (the parity case `:478-490`; a new case), `test/docsPages.test.ts`, `CHANGELOG.md` |
| `interfaces` | **Current.** `release.json` is schema 1 (`scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs:17`): `runtime` = `package, version, nodeFloor, tarballSha256` (`:31`), `packages[]` = `client, path, archive, sha256, bytes, clientFloor` (`:32`). The digests cover the archives; the unarchived roots that marketplaces install from `plugin-dist` (`claude/`, `cursor/`, `copilot/`, `codex/`) have no digest anywhere (r4 SEC-2). The validator refuses an unknown key (`:125`, applied at `:237`) and any `schemaVersion` but 1 (`:238`), so every new field is a consumer-visible change. `RUNTIME.json` = `package, version, nodeFloor, tarballSha256, dependencies` (`scripts/plugins/runtime.mjs:419-427`). The fork's publish computes the tarball's sha512 integrity for its idempotency check and discards it (`.github/workflows/fork-release.yml:656`). Canonical: the tarball digest is checked against the gates output (`release.yml:801-818`), the archives against `release.json` (`:841-886`), then `npm publish "./$TARBALL"` (`:899`), and the same `$TARBALL` is attached (`:1059`); no step compares the registry's `dist.integrity` with anything, and no page states that the asset is the registry tarball byte for byte (r3 S5.2). File 0 adds the engine's owned-path bound, printed as `ownedPaths` by `check --json`. **New, `scripts/plugins/rootDigest.mjs`** (Node builtins only): `export const ROOT_DIGEST_ALGORITHM = 'sha256-tree-v1'` · `export function rootDigestLines(files)` — `files: { path: string, bytes: Uint8Array }[]` with root-relative POSIX paths; returns, sorted by `path` (JS `<`, equal to byte order on the ASCII paths it admits), one line `<sha256 hex of bytes>  <path>\n` per file (the `sha256sum` line); throws `root digest: <path> …` for a duplicate or empty path, a leading `/`, a `\`, a `..` segment, a line break or a character outside printable ASCII · `export function rootDigest(files)` = sha256 hex of `rootDigestLines(files)` · `export function rootDigestOfDir(dir)` over every regular file under `dir`, refusing anything else (`root digest: <path> is not a regular file; a client root carries regular files only`). A consumer recomputes it with `cd <root> && find . -type f \| sed 's\|^\./\|\|' \| LC_ALL=C sort \| while IFS= read -r f; do sha256sum "$f"; done \| sha256sum`, which the README prints. **New `RUNTIME.json`:** `buildRuntimeManifest({ manifest, tarballSha256, tarballIntegrity, ownedPaths, dependencies })`, key order `package, version, nodeFloor, tarballSha256, tarballIntegrity, ownedPaths, dependencies`; `tarballIntegrity` = `'sha512-' + base64(sha512(<tarball bytes>))`, the string npm reports as `dist.integrity`; `ownedPaths` = the bound the extracted engine exports, read by `readOwnedPaths(root)`: `spawnSync(process.execPath, ['--input-type=module', '-e', <import process.argv[1], print JSON.stringify of file 0's bound>, pathToFileURL(join(root, 'dist', 'index.js')).href], { shell: false })`, shape-checked as `{ exact: string[], roots: string[] }` (the shape `check --json` prints; file 0 names the export, and when it does not export the bound from `src/index.ts` this unit adds that one export). **New `release.json`, schema 2:** `RELEASE_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION = 2`; `export const SUPPORTED_RELEASE_MANIFEST_SCHEMAS = [1, 2]`; schema-2 key lists — top level `schemaVersion, version, sourceCommit, sourceCommitDate, distribution, runtime, packages, catalogs, apm, ownedPaths`; `runtime` `package, version, nodeFloor, tarballSha256, tarballIntegrity`; `packages[]` `client, path, archive, sha256, bytes, rootSha256, clientFloor`. `buildReleaseManifest` writes schema 2 in that order. `validateReleaseManifest(value)` judges by the declared `schemaVersion`: 1 → today's rules, the schema-2 keys reported "is not a supported key"; 2 → also `runtime.tarballIntegrity: must be npm's sha512 integrity string (sha512- followed by 88 base64 characters)` (`/^sha512-[A-Za-z0-9+/]{86}==$/`), `packages[<i>].rootSha256: must be the 64-character lowercase hex digest of the client root (sha256-tree-v1)`, `ownedPaths: must be an object carrying exact and roots`, and `ownedPaths.exact` / `ownedPaths.roots: must be a non-empty, sorted array of distinct repository-relative POSIX paths` (no leading `/`, no `\`, no `..`; every `roots` entry ends with `/`, no `exact` entry does); any other value → `schemaVersion: must be 1 or 2`. One defect, one message, as today. **Builder** (`scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs`): each `packages[client]` gains `rootSha256: rootDigestOfDir(rootDir)`, computed in the archive loop (`:519-545`) over the files the archive takes; `runtime.tarballIntegrity` and `ownedPaths` come from `RUNTIME.json` beside `tarballSha256` (`:577-583`); the README's integrity paragraph gains "The release asset `<tarball>` is the npm tarball byte for byte: its sha512 is the registry's `dist.integrity`, recorded as `runtime.tarballIntegrity`. Each `packages[].rootSha256` is the sha256-tree-v1 digest of that client's directory: <the one-liner above>." **Workflows:** (1) "Verify plugin distribution digest" (`release.yml:841-886`, held byte-equal at `fork-release.yml:475`) gains `TARBALL_SHA256: ${{ needs.gates.outputs.tarball_sha256 }}` in `env` (both workflows have that output) and, for a schema-2 manifest, checks `runtime.tarballSha256` against it, `runtime.tarballIntegrity` against the sha512 of the verified `$TARBALL`, and each `packages[]` entry's `rootSha256` against the digest of `plugins/<path>/` computed inline by the same algorithm (`::error::<client> root hashes to <x> and the verified manifest says <y>. Refusing to publish.`); a test runs the step's program over a fixture tree and holds it equal to `rootDigestOfDir`. (2) A new step **"Verify the registry tarball"** directly after the publish step, its `run` byte-equal in both workflows (the fork's `env` also carries the publish step's `NODE_AUTH_TOKEN` and `REGISTRY`): `EXPECTED` = `plugins/release.json`'s `runtime.tarballIntegrity`; `npm view "$PACKAGE_NAME@$VERSION" dist.integrity ${REGISTRY:+--registry "$REGISTRY"}` asked up to 12 times, 10 s apart, until it answers; on a mismatch or no answer `::error::the registry serves $PACKAGE_NAME@$VERSION with integrity <a>, and release.json records <b>: the release asset is not the registry tarball. Nothing further is published.` and exit 1 — before "Push plugin distribution" and "Create GitHub release"; then `_hasShrinkwrap` is read the same way, and anything but `true` prints `::warning::the registry does not flag $PACKAGE_NAME@$VERSION as shrinkwrapped, so npm resolves its dependency tree fresh on install`. (3) The canonical "Publish to npm with provenance" (`release.yml:898-899`) becomes idempotent like the fork's (`fork-release.yml:655-665`): it computes the integrity, skips the publish when the registry already serves it ("already published with this tarball's integrity; nothing to publish (an idempotent re-run)"), refuses another tarball under the version, and otherwise runs `npm publish "./$TARBALL" --provenance --access public` — so a re-run after a step-(2) failure reaches the remaining steps. **Docs** `docs/enterprise-forks.md` `### Consume the release`: the byte-identity rule, the two fields, and that an install from the asset matches a lock resolved from the registry (equal `integrity`). **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**`release.json` names npm's integrity and a digest per client root.** Schema 2 adds `runtime.tarballIntegrity` (the sha512 integrity npm records for the tarball, which is the release asset byte for byte), `packages[].rootSha256` (a digest of each unarchived client root marketplaces install) and `ownedPaths` (the paths this release's engine may write or remove in a repository); both release workflows check the registry's integrity after publishing. The validator still reads schema 1." **Follows:** `scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs:83-122` (one fixed key order, builder and validator apart) and `.github/workflows/release.yml:841-886` (inline verification in a job that checks nothing out). **Surface pins moved:** `test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts:85-86` (schema 1 → 2) and `:171` (the stale-schema case now sets `schemaVersion = 3`), `test/ci/pluginRuntime.test.ts:492` (the `RUNTIME.json` key list), each with a `TEST CHANGE, justified:` line. |
| `testCriteria` | **Given** a two-file tree `a.txt` = `a\n`, `dir/b.txt` = `b\n`, **when** `rootDigest` runs, **then** it equals the sha256 of `<sha256("a\n")>  a.txt\n<sha256("b\n")>  dir/b.txt\n`, computed in the test with `node:crypto`; **given** the same files in reverse input order, **then** the same digest; **given** a symbolic link in the tree (skipped where the platform cannot create one, with the reason in the test), **then** `rootDigestOfDir` throws naming it; **given** a path with a non-ASCII character, **then** it throws (`test/ci/rootDigest.test.ts`, new). **Given** a distribution build, **then** `release.json` validates as schema 2, each `packages[].rootSha256` equals `rootDigestOfDir(<out>/<client>)` and equals `rootDigest` over the archive's own entries with the `<client>/` prefix dropped, `runtime.tarballIntegrity` equals the stub `RUNTIME.json`'s, and `ownedPaths` deep-equals the stub's (`test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts`). **Given** the schema-1 manifest of `plugins/v1.11.0` transcribed as a fixture, **then** `validateReleaseManifest` returns `[]`; **given** it with `ownedPaths` added, **then** exactly one message, `ownedPaths: is not a supported key`; **given** a schema-2 manifest missing `runtime.tarballIntegrity`, with `tarballIntegrity: "sha256-x"`, without one `rootSha256`, or with `ownedPaths.roots: ["a"]`, **then** exactly one message each, naming that path (`test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts`). **Given** `buildRuntime` over the test's stub tarball, **then** `RUNTIME.json`'s keys are `package, version, nodeFloor, tarballSha256, tarballIntegrity, ownedPaths, dependencies` and `tarballIntegrity` equals `sha512-` plus the base64 sha512 of the tarball (`test/ci/pluginRuntime.test.ts:492-496`, re-pinned). **Given** `release.yml`, **then** its publish job runs "Verify the registry tarball" after "Publish to npm with provenance" and before "Push plugin distribution", the step reads `dist.integrity` and `runtime.tarballIntegrity`, and the publish step skips when the registry already holds the integrity (`test/ci/workflow.test.ts`); **given** the "Verify plugin distribution digest" program extracted from the YAML and run over a fixture `plugins/` tree with a schema-2 manifest, **then** it exits 0, and with one root byte changed it exits 1 naming that client. **Given** `fork-release.yml`, **then** "Verify the registry tarball" follows "Publish to the registry" and its `run` equals `release.yml`'s byte for byte (`test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts`, a new case beside `:478-490`; the existing case keeps comparing `run` and `env` of its three steps). **Given** a distribution build's `README.md`, **then** it states the byte-identity rule and the root-digest command; **given** `docs/enterprise-forks.md`, **then** `### Consume the release` names `runtime.tarballIntegrity` (`test/docsPages.test.ts`). |
| `edgeCases` | A registry that has not served the version yet → the step retries for 120 s, then fails; the re-run skips the idempotent publish and checks again. A fork release re-run → the publish step skips (same integrity) and the check passes. A consumer validating with a 1.11.0 copy of the validator → refuses schema 2 (unknown keys), which the CHANGELOG names. A promotion or rollback reading a schema-1 tag → `u2-channels-promote` refuses it (decision S18). The APM folders (`apm/`, `apm-charter/`) → no root digest: they are not client roots, and `release.json`'s attestation (the brief's S1; file 1's decision S19) covers the file. A root that gains an empty folder → no change (folders are not hashed); a mode change (an executable bit) → no change, as the archive's bytes do not carry it either. Windows → the tests compute digests through `node:crypto`; the shell one-liner is documentation for POSIX hosts. |

| `verify` | `npx vitest run test/ci/rootDigest.test.ts test/ci/releaseManifest.test.ts test/ci/pluginRuntime.test.ts test/ci/pluginDistribution.test.ts test/ci/pluginDownstream.test.ts test/ci/workflow.test.ts test/ci/forkReleaseWorkflow.test.ts test/docsPages.test.ts` · `npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test` |

a result that differs from (a)–(e) above is written as found and stops the unit for the maintainer. |
| `edgeCases` | A registry that does not set `_hasShrinkwrap` (GitHub Packages is unmeasured) → npm resolves fresh, as today; `u2-release-integrity`'s post-publish step warns by name. A transitive security fix published after a release → reaches consumers with the next release only (npm recommends shrinkwrap for command-line tools, docs.npmjs.com/cli/v11/configuring-npm/npm-shrinkwrap-json, read 2026-10-06); Dependabot already watches `package-lock.json`. An install from the release asset or `npx --package <file>` → the shrinkwrap is ignored (measured); the consumer's own lock governs. A Dependabot bump of a production dependency → the next pack carries it, with no separate step. pnpm or yarn consumers → whether they read a dependency's shrinkwrap is unverified; the docs (file 3) say npm only. An interrupted local pack → the `finally` removes the file; `.gitignore` keeps a leftover out of commits, and the next `pack-release` refuses until it is gone. Windows → npm's `st.cmd` shim runs `dist/st.js`, so the deprecation line prints there too. `--json` runs through `st` → the line goes to stderr; stdout keeps its one document. |

Xcode, which reads prompt files and no skills, no longer lists them." **Follows:** `src/adapters/cursor.ts:468-476` (touchpoints as shared rows) and `scripts/plugins/clients/cursor.mjs:63-73` (a command-class skill row in a plugin root). **Surface pins moved:** the cross-client snapshot (regenerated: `npx vitest run test/emit/crossClientGoldens.test.ts --update`, file first, `--update` last), `docs/capability-matrix.md` (`node scripts/generate-capability-matrix.mjs`). |

**given** that repository synced and `cursor` then deselected, **when** `sync` runs, **then** the nine files stay byte-identical. **Given** a fixture whose ledger holds 1.11.0's nine prompt rows, **when** `sync` runs, **then** the nine prompt files are deleted, every other file under `.github/` is byte-identical, and the nine skills exist (`test/cli/commands/syncEngine.test.ts`). **Given** the Copilot plugin root built by `generate-plugin-packages.mjs`, **then** the touchpoints sit at `skills/st-<id>/SKILL.md`, its capability file counts nine under `command`, no `com.github.copilot/commands/` exists, and the binary leg's `copilot skill list --json` names `st-work` (`test/ci/pluginPackages.copilot.test.ts`, re-pinned with `TEST CHANGE, justified: REQ-FLOW-050`). **Given** the init panel for Copilot, **then** its command step names `.agents/skills` and `/st-<id>`, and no step names `.github/prompts` (`test/cli/commands/initPanel.test.ts`). **Given** `renderCapabilityMatrix()`, **then** Copilot's `command-surface` row names Xcode and both pages with access date 2026-10-06 (`test/emit/capabilityMatrix.test.ts`). **Given** every selection of the four clients, **then** no plan writes both a `.github/prompts/st-<id>.prompt.md` and an `.agents/skills/st-<id>/SKILL.md` (a property case in `test/emit/touchpointSkills.test.ts`). **Given** the cross-client goldens, **then** the refresh-ledger entry names, per selection with Copilot, the nine removed prompt files and the owner added to the skills. |
| `edgeCases` | An installed pack's command → a shared skill too (it rode `buildPromptFile` before); a `model_class` pin on it no longer reaches Copilot, which the cap says. The user's own files in `.github/prompts/` (no ledger row) → untouched. A user override of a touchpoint → lands in the shared skill, so it reaches all three readers, as on Cursor. An edited legacy prompt file → the sweep keeps it and reports it, and VS Code lists that touchpoint twice until it goes. Plugin-backed Copilot → the command class is plugin-owned, so the rows drop as before and the root carries them under `skills/`. Claude plus Copilot → whether VS Code also lists `.claude/commands/` is the census row `u2-repo-file-waste` adds; a `yes` there is a residue row, since Claude Code needs its commands. A personal or plugin skill named `st-work` in the Copilot CLI → the repository copy wins (first loaded), as intended. |

Move both together — the apm.yml ref to <slug>/apm#plugins/v<generatedBy>, or upgrade --to <v1>"; another major → `fail` with the same text plus "the two refuse each other's state". **New, the row** (`async function checkApmDeliveries(rootDir: string, manifest: SetupManifest \| null, engine: EngineRegistry): Promise<DoctorCheck>` in `src/cli/commands/check.ts`, `const id = "apm-deliveries"`, wired through `guarded` (`:2053`), placed after `apm-client` and before `pack-reach`): in every install mode it reads `readApmLock(rootDir)`, `apm.yml` (`yaml`), and for each of `SHARED_HOOK_FILES` (u2-apm-backed-mode) the file and its sidecar, each only after `lstat` proves a regular file (a link or another type → that file "cannot be read (not a regular file)"); it never writes. stop that package delivering hooks to cursor (apm deny <source>, or drop cursor from its targets), then apm install" (`(from APM <source>)` only when `apmHookPlacements` attributes the entry; `no-command`: "entry <n> of <event> has no command"); (3) **`warn`**: an `apmHookPlacements` row with `present: false` — "<sidecar>: APM records a <event> entry of <source> that <file> no longer holds — run apm install"; (4) **`warn`**: any hook placement, Copilot hook file or MCP placement from a source that is not the stamity package (`isPackageDependency`, u2-apm-backed-mode) while `executableGateOn` is false — "APM deployed hooks or MCP servers of <sources> with no executables gate in apm.yml; APM deploys every package's hooks and servers until apm.yml carries one — add executables: { allow: {} } and approve the packages you keep (apm approve <package>)"; (5) one listing line per source (no status of its own): "<source>: hooks <event> [<summary>] in <file>, …; Copilot hook file <path>; MCP <server> for <tools>" — `summary` is `apmHookPlacements`' matcher plus the command's first word, never the whole command; (6) **`warn`**, `apm-backed` only: each `collectApmReachGaps` row — "<path>: overrides <type> <id>, which APM delivers to <tools> under apm-backed — no client there receives this override; change it in the fork layer (docs/customization.md), or switch to generated"; (7) `apm-backed` only: the `apmVersionSkew` line for the stamity dependency (`isPackageDependency`, not the charter package) against `manifest.generatedBy`. When `collectApmReachGaps` throws (the corpus cannot be read), part (6) reads `not evaluated: <first line of the error>` (`warn`), as `pack-reach` does. The sidecars, the lock and `apm.yml` shape only the wording and the attribution: no write, refusal or deletion follows from them (principle 5). **New, `validate`:** `export async function collectApmReachGaps(rootDir: string, engine: EngineRegistry, manifest: SetupManifest \| null): Promise<ApmReachGap[]>` — `apmReachGaps` over the customization outcomes `collectValidateFindings` (`:281`) already yields (replaced and patched rows, `type`, `id`, `path`, `emits`) and `apmRuleSkillIds` (u2-apm-backed-mode) of the reachable rule items; under `apm-backed` the human report prints each gap with row (6)'s text and `--json` carries `apmUnreached: ApmReachGap[]`; in other modes the key is absent (no shape change there). **Docs:** `docs/troubleshooting.md` gains the `apm-deliveries` row (**Can fail.** the two fail lines and their remedies; the warns; "the row reads, never writes"), the can-fail sentence moves from eight to nine names, and the sample gains `ok    apm-deliveries       no APM install in this repository`; `docs/getting-started.md`'s APM section and `docs/enterprise-forks.md:791-813` gain: "APM deploys every package's hooks and MCP servers unless `apm.yml` carries an `executables` block. With `executables: { allow: {} }` apm-cli 0.32.0 and 0.33.0 skip a package's hooks but still write its MCP servers; the deprecated `allowExecutables: {}` stops both (measured 2026-10-06). `check`'s `apm-deliveries` row lists what was placed either way." **CHANGELOG** `### Added`: "**`check` names what APM places beside this setup.** A new `apm-deliveries` row lists, per APM package, the hook entries it merged into `.claude/settings.json`, `.cursor/hooks.json` and `.codex/hooks.json` and the MCP servers it configured; it fails when `.cursor/hooks.json` holds an event Cursor rejects — which turns off every hook in the file, this setup's guards included — and when a server lands in `.codex/config.toml`, which `sync` rewrites whole. Under `apm-backed` it also names overrides APM's classes keep from reaching a client, and an APM package whose version differs from the CLI's." **Follows:** `src/cli/commands/check.ts:1182-1224` (`checkPackReach`: a row that passes with a note when its subject is absent and warns `not evaluated` when an input cannot be built) and `src/cli/commands/plugin/probe.ts:891` (`collectPluginDuplicates`: repository reads once, then pure answers) |

**when** `check --json` runs, **then** the `apm-deliveries` row warns with the same line; **given** mode `generated`, **then** the JSON has no `apmUnreached` key (`test/cli/commands/validate.test.ts`). **Given** the troubleshooting page, **then** `test/docsPages.test.ts`'s probe-row set check (`:2755-2785`) passes and the can-fail sentence names `apm-deliveries`. |
| `edgeCases` | A sidecar or hook file that does not parse → that file's line says "cannot be read (<first line of the parser error>)" (`warn`), and its entries are not attributed. A native entry that itself carries `_apm_source` (an older client wrote markers in place) → counted as a placement of that source. A sidecar source the lock no longer lists (a dependency removed while its entries stayed) → listed as "<source> (not in apm.lock.yaml)" with row (4)'s gate rule. Cursor not selected → no Cursor line (no guard of this setup lives there); codex not selected → the TOML server is listed, not failed. APM rewrites a hook file with Python's `json.dump(indent=2)` (`hook_integrator.py:1555`), escaping non-ASCII text as `\uXXXX` → attribution compares parsed values, so it holds; APM also replaces a hook file it cannot parse with one holding only its own entries (`:1263-1271`) → this setup's keys go missing and the drift gate reports them, `sync` restores them. Identical entries from APM and from this setup → u2-apm-backed-mode's duplicates row fails them; this row lists only what APM's sidecar claims. A future client that renames the sidecar → nothing is attributed and APM's entries look foreign; the coexistence leg (u2-apm-coexistence-fork, consumer A2) asserts the listing at the current client, so a rename turns CI red. A remote dependency's `_apm_source` spelling (canonical dependency string, `hook_ownership.py:131-136`) differs from the lock's `repo_url` → matched through `name`, `repoUrl`, `repoUrl/virtualPath` and `localPath` in that order; unmatched → the raw marker is printed. |

- **Merge with file 0 (2026-10-06):** file 0's `u0-hook-files-ownership` creates `CURSOR_HOOK_EVENTS` and `cursorHookDefects` and already fails `check` on a `.cursor/hooks.json` Cursor rejects (its S19); this unit reuses both and adds the per-package attribution. File 0 also owns `.codex/config.toml` per table (its S16), so an APM server table there survives `sync`: this row lists it instead of failing, and its `.codex/config.toml` fail arm is dropped.

## Execution order

*Rewritten 2026-10-06 for the updated brief:* this file gained five units (`u2-json-contract`, `u2-release-integrity`,
`u2-shrinkwrap`, `u2-copilot-touchpoints`, `u2-apm-deliveries`). It now runs as **two sessions, one pull request
each**, split where every unit of the second session depends only on units of the first or of earlier files.

1. **Intake, once per session** (the freshness guard): files 0 and 1 (`docs/plans/016-fork-distribution-00.md`, `-01.md`)
   and Packages 19, 18, 22 and Track B merged; every anchor this file cites re-read against the landed tree. Record in
   the run record the names files 0 and 1 landed that these units build on: the co-owned-document API,
   `LedgerEntry.coOwned`, `coOwnedDocumentLanes`, the owned-path bound and its export, the registry-bound call helper,
   `CURSOR_HOOK_EVENTS` and the `check --expect-*` flags (file 0); the `apm.lock.yaml` reader and its export name
   (`u1-foreign-paths`), `scripts/fork-identity.mjs --suffix` (`u1-renovate-suffix`), and every warning producer and
   `--json` field files 0 and 1 added (the census `u2-json-contract` needs).
2. **Measurements and maintainer steps first:** `u2-apm-sparse-measure` writes no repository file and decides the slim
   route (FOLDER or FALLBACK) and the root copy (KEEP or STOP) before `u2-apm-slim-package` starts; the payload probe of
   `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode` (session 2) runs as soon as that unit's harness exists; `u2-main-catalogs`' pre-merge
   VS Code catalog walk and `u2-shrinkwrap`'s lab measurement run early in session 1. The maintainer's steps (installing
   VS Code with GitHub Copilot Chat; the Codex trust entry) are scheduled at the start of the session that needs them.
3. **Session 1 — the CLI route, the release manifest and the APM package, one pull request**, in this serial order:
   `u2-apm-sparse-measure`, `u2-repo-file-waste`, `u2-copilot-touchpoints`, `u2-upgrade-verb`, `u2-json-contract`,
   `u2-main-catalogs`, `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`, `u2-apm-placeholders`, `u2-shrinkwrap`, `u2-release-integrity`,
   `u2-apm-slim-package`, `u2-apm-charter`. Lanes the edges allow: (A) `u2-repo-file-waste` → `u2-copilot-touchpoints`
   → `u2-upgrade-verb` → `u2-json-contract`; (B) `u2-main-catalogs` → `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`; (C) `u2-shrinkwrap`
   → `u2-release-integrity` → `u2-apm-slim-package` → `u2-apm-charter`, after `u2-apm-sparse-measure` and
   `u2-apm-placeholders`. **`release.json` moves to schema 2 once**, in `u2-release-integrity`, before the slim package
   and the charter (the merge's resolution of the two drafts' orders, 2026-10-06): schema 2's key set holds
   `packages[].archivePath` (optional; `u2-apm-slim-package` fills it) and keeps `apm.charterInstallSpec` optional
   (`u2-apm-charter` fills it).
4. **Session 2 — channels, admin templates, the APM-backed mode and the plugin route, one pull request**, in this
   serial order: `u2-channels-promote`, `u2-admin-templates`, `u2-apm-backed-mode`, `u2-apm-floor-current`,
   `u2-apm-deliveries`, `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode`, `u2-review-gate-all`, `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot`,
   `u2-plugin-overrides`, `u2-declare`, `u2-apm-renovate-preset`, `u2-apm-coexistence-fork`. Lanes the edges allow:
   `u2-channels-promote` → `u2-admin-templates` beside `u2-apm-backed-mode` → `u2-apm-floor-current` →
   `u2-apm-deliveries`; every other step waits for its predecessors.
5. **Shared files have one writer at a time**, through the session's integrator in integration order: `CHANGELOG.md`
   (each unit's bullet text is fixed in its `interfaces`; sections `### Added`, `### Changed`, `### Fixed`, `### Security`
   under `## [Unreleased]`), `docs/getting-started.md` (`u2-copilot-touchpoints`, `u2-upgrade-verb`, `u2-shrinkwrap`),
   `docs/enterprise-forks.md`, `docs/plugins.md`, `docs/troubleshooting.md`, `test/docsPages.test.ts`
   (`REATTESTATION_DATE` and the hand-page headers), `docs/cli-reference.md` (regenerated by `u2-upgrade-verb`,
   `u2-json-contract`, then `u2-declare`), `docs/capability-matrix.md` and `src/emit/capabilityMatrix.ts`
   (`u2-repo-file-waste`, `u2-copilot-touchpoints`, `u2-review-gate-all`, `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot`,
   `u2-plugin-overrides`), the golden snapshot `test/corpus/__snapshots__/emissionGoldens.test.ts.snap` (regenerated
   with the test file named first and `--update` last), `src/adapters/copilot.ts` (`u2-repo-file-waste`,
   `u2-copilot-touchpoints`, `u2-json-contract`, `u2-review-gate-all`, `u2-plugin-overrides`, `u2-declare`),
   `src/adapters/cursor.ts` (`u2-apm-deliveries` → `u2-hooks-self-filter-vscode` → `u2-review-gate-all` →
   `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot`), `src/cli/commands/sync/report.ts` (`u2-upgrade-verb`, then `u2-json-contract`),
   `src/cli/commands/check.ts` and its doctor list (final order `… plugin-runtime, plugin-duplicates, apm-client,
   apm-deliveries, review-gate, plugin-reach, pack-reach, invariants`; each unit re-pins the list it finds — fifteen rows
   at `d10db029`, more once files 0 and 1 add theirs), `scripts/plugins/releaseManifest.mjs` and
   `scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs` (`u2-release-integrity`, `u2-apm-slim-package`, `u2-apm-charter`,
   `u2-channels-promote`, `u2-admin-templates`, `u2-declare`), `.github/workflows/release.yml` and `fork-release.yml`
   (`u2-shrinkwrap`, `u2-release-integrity`, `u2-apm-slim-package`, `u2-admin-templates`, then `u2-apm-floor-current`
   and `u2-apm-coexistence-fork`, which touch other jobs), `renovate/companion.json` (file 1's `u1-renovate-suffix`
   first, then `u2-apm-renovate-preset`), `package.json` (`u2-shrinkwrap` only), and the four
   `test/ci/pluginPackages.*.test.ts`.
6. **One owner per shared contract** (the contract census): the owner rule at `src/emit/planner.ts` and
   `pluginOwnedClasses` change first in `u2-apm-backed-mode`, and `u2-cursor-rules-bugbot` and `u2-plugin-overrides`
   build on them; `scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs`'s `substitute` changes in `u2-skills-bundled-runtime`, and
   `u2-apm-placeholders` calls it without the plugin runtime; `PluginConfig` gains `mode: "apm-backed"` in
   `u2-apm-backed-mode` before `u2-declare` adds `declaration`; the declaration reader is `u2-apm-backed-mode`'s
   `pluginDeclarationsIn`, which `u2-declare` imports.
7. **The Prove phase** (`/st-work`) of each session merges the spec-delta entries of the units that session landed.
8. **The gates, per session:** `npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test`, then `npm test -- --coverage` (the
   `src/emit/` and `src/merge/` floors) and `npm run check`; every unit that changes a generated hook or a Claude
   configuration document re-syncs the dogfood (`npm run build && node dist/cli.js sync`); CI's Windows leg and coverage
   are required. Each pull request title is a conventional-commit subject, for example `feat(routes): a JSON contract,
   release integrity and a shrinkwrap, Copilot touchpoints as skills, and the slim APM package` (session 1) and
   `feat(routes): release channels, apm-backed mode, plugin-mode overrides and the review gate on every client`
   (session 2).

## Risks

| Risk | Severity | Guard |
|---|---|---|
| The distribution tree's root copy, kept so the 1.9–1.11 spec `<slug>#plugins/v<ver>` keeps working, deploys a primitive twice for a root install | Warning | `u2-apm-sparse-measure`'s arm D measures it before the slim package is built; `APM root copy: STOP` holds the unit for the maintainer's choice |
| An apm-backed repository also adds the charter package, so Claude loads two charters with contradicting facts | Warning | the duplicates row fails with the remedy; the README and docs say APM-backed repositories leave the charter package out |

## Open questions

None. The spec author's five markers are settled in the decisions above (S5, S6) and in file 1 (S8, S9); the drafters'
departures from the first spec draft are folded into the spec delta; and the maintainer answered the eight questions of
the walk.

**Amended 2026-10-06.** None open. The drafters' readings are settled as declared defaults the maintainer may reverse before a session starts: Copilot's touchpoints ship only as shared skills and Xcode, which reads no skills, loses them (S20, principle 2; a follow-up row keeps the Xcode case); a tag declaration's commit pin is operator input, so the CLI stays offline (S23); if the VS Code catalog walk ends in `STOP`, `u2-main-catalogs` stops for the maintainer (S25); a nested entry under a valid Cursor event key fails `apm-deliveries` until Cursor's reaction is measured (S30); the single `release.json` schema bump lands before the slim APM package and the charter (Execution order, step 3).

## Follow-ups (appended to `.stamity/inbox.md` at this plan's write)

- Minor · scripts/build-plugin-distribution.mjs:513 · the four plugin archives sit at the distribution root, so APM's sparse cone (which always materializes root-level files) fetches about 7.3 MB with any subfolder; moving them under `archives/` would cut the slim route's fetch to a few KB (probe 2026-10-01).
- Minor · scripts/generate-apm-package.mjs:669-689 · `.apm/skills/*/agents/openai.yaml` reaches every APM consumer whatever its targets (APM deploys skill directories whole, `.claude/skills/` included); apm-cli's per-dependency filters (`skill_subset`, `target_subset`, unchanged in 0.33.0) cannot drop one companion — revisit if APM gains per-target companion filtering.
- Minor · scripts/plugins/tokens.mjs:70-79 · the charter-reference phrases say "in AGENTS.md", while an APM-only Claude consumer receives the charter as `.claude/rules/stamity-charter.md` and may have no `AGENTS.md`; a route-neutral phrase ("listed under Verification gates in the charter") would read true on every route.
- Minor · content/charter/stamity-charter.md:68-70 · the Touchpoints paragraph promises the nine as skills to a client reading `.agents/skills/`, but APM-only Codex receives none (APM's codex profile carries no command class); reword per route in u3-route-guide.
